namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs
{
	public class CxsCase : INotifyPropertyChanged, IDisposable
	{
		private CxsSolver _Solver;

		private string _CaseWorkingDirectory;

		private string _CaseFilePath;

		private string _RunningCasePath;

		private CxsVersionRequirements _VersionRequirements = new CxsVersionRequirements();

		private CxsRemotingRequirements _RemotingRequirements = new CxsRemotingRequirements();

		private ICxsCaseAdapterTraits _AdapterTraits;

		private ICxsCaseAdapter _RunningCase;

		private Dictionary<string, object> _AdapterSettings = new Dictionary<string, object>();

		private List<CxsMessageFilterSpec> _MessageFilters;

		private EventHandler<CxsMessageEventArgs> _MessageHandlers;

		private CxsCaseMessageSink _MessageSink;

		private EventHandler<CxsNodesChangedEventArgs> _NodesChangedHandlers;

		private Dictionary<string, WeakReference> _CachedNodes = new Dictionary<string, WeakReference>();

		private CxsBatchRequestData _PendingNodeAssignments;

		private Dictionary<string, string> _SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs;

		private WeakReference _ChildNodesReference;

		public bool IsConnected => _RunningCase != null;

		[Category("Configuration")]
		public string CaseFilePath
		{
			get
			{
				return _CaseFilePath;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == _CaseFilePath) && string.Compare(_CaseFilePath, value, ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) != 0)
				{
					_CaseFilePath = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("CaseFilePath");
					RaisePropertyChanged("CaseFullFilePath");
					RaisePropertyChanged("CaseRelativeFilePath");
				}
			}
		}

		public string CaseFullFilePath
		{
			get
			{
				if (Path.IsPathRooted(_CaseFilePath))
				{
					return Path.GetFullPath(_CaseFilePath);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseWorkingDirectory))
				{
					return Path.Combine(Path.GetFullPath(_CaseWorkingDirectory), _CaseFilePath);
				}
				return Path.GetFullPath(_CaseFilePath);
			}
		}

		public string CaseRelativeFilePath
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Path.IsPathRooted(_CaseFilePath) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseWorkingDirectory))
				{
					return _CaseFilePath;
				}
				string text = Path.GetFullPath(_CaseWorkingDirectory) + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
				string fullPath = Path.GetFullPath(_CaseFilePath);
				if (string.Compare(fullPath, 0, text, 0, text.Length, ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture) == 0)
				{
					return fullPath.Substring(text.Length);
				}
				return _CaseFilePath;
			}
		}

		public bool CaseFileExists => File.Exists(CaseFullFilePath);

		public string RunningCasePath => _RunningCasePath;

		public string Id => CaseFileName;

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[Description("The working directory for resolve relative file paths")]
		public string CaseWorkingDirectory
		{
			get
			{
				return _CaseWorkingDirectory;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == _CaseWorkingDirectory) && string.Compare(_CaseWorkingDirectory, value, ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) != 0)
				{
					_CaseWorkingDirectory = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("CaseWorkingDirectory");
				}
			}
		}

		public string CaseFileName
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseFilePath))
				{
					return Path.GetFileName(_CaseFilePath);
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		[Category("Configuration")]
		public string ApplicationRequirement { get; set; }

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxsVersionRequirements VersionRequirements => _VersionRequirements;

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxsRemotingRequirements RemotingRequirements => _RemotingRequirements;

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Dictionary<string, object> AdapterSettings => _AdapterSettings;

		public CxsSolver Solver
		{
			get
			{
				if (_Solver == null)
				{
					_Solver = new CxsSolver(this);
				}
				return _Solver;
			}
		}

		public string ApplicationName => _AdapterTraits.ApplicationName;

		public string ApplicationVersion
		{
			get
			{
				if (_RunningCase == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return _RunningCase.ApplicationVersion;
			}
		}

		public string Status => CxsConverter.ValueToString(BatchGet("Top", "status"));

		public bool CanSaveCopyAs
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = false;
				if (_RunningCase != null)
				{
					result = _RunningCase.CanSaveCopyAs;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public int DefaultTimeoutToKillCase { get; set; }

		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				if (_RunningCase == null || _RunningCase.IsRemote)
				{
					return false;
				}
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(BatchGet("Top", "visible"));
				if (!flag.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return flag.Value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_RunningCase != null && !_RunningCase.IsRemote)
				{
					BatchSet("Top", "visible", value);
					RaisePropertyChanged("Visible");
				}
			}
		}

		public int SimulatorProcessId
		{
			get
			{
				int result = 0;
				if (_RunningCase != null && _RunningCase is CxsComCaseAdapterV22)
				{
					try
					{
						int? num = (_RunningCase as CxsComCaseAdapterV22).InternalGetProcessId();
						if (num.HasValue)
						{
							result = num.Value;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessageFilterSpec> MessageFilters
		{
			get
			{
				if (_MessageFilters == null)
				{
					return new ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessageFilterSpec>(new List<CxsMessageFilterSpec>());
				}
				return new ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessageFilterSpec>(_MessageFilters);
			}
		}

		internal ICxsCaseAdapterTraits AdapterTraits => _AdapterTraits;

		public ICxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits AdapterNodeTraits => _AdapterTraits as ICxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits;

		[Browsable(false)]
		public CxsNode TopNode
		{
			get
			{
				CxsNodeCollection childNodes = ChildNodes;
				if (childNodes == null || childNodes.Count <= 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return childNodes[0];
			}
		}

		public CxsNodeCollection ChildNodes
		{
			get
			{
				if (_ChildNodesReference == null || !_ChildNodesReference.IsAlive || (_ChildNodesReference.Target as CxsNodeCollection).Count == 0)
				{
					RefreshChildNodes();
				}
				if (_ChildNodesReference == null || !_ChildNodesReference.IsAlive)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _ChildNodesReference.Target as CxsNodeCollection;
			}
		}

		public bool AutoRetrieveMissingNodeData { get; set; }

		public bool QueueNodeAssignments { get; set; }

		public bool NodeAssignmentsArePending
		{
			get
			{
				if (_PendingNodeAssignments != null)
				{
					return _PendingNodeAssignments.RequestsCount > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public event EventHandler<CxsMessageEventArgs> Message
		{
			add
			{
				lock (this)
				{
					_MessageHandlers = (EventHandler<CxsMessageEventArgs>)Delegate.Combine(_MessageHandlers, value);
					ConnectMessageSink();
				}
			}
			remove
			{
				lock (this)
				{
					if (_MessageHandlers != null)
					{
						_MessageHandlers = (EventHandler<CxsMessageEventArgs>)Delegate.Remove(_MessageHandlers, value);
						if (_MessageHandlers == null)
						{
							DisconnectMessageSink();
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<CxsNodesChangedEventArgs> NodesChanged
		{
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			add
			{
				_NodesChangedHandlers = (EventHandler<CxsNodesChangedEventArgs>)Delegate.Combine(_NodesChangedHandlers, value);
			}
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			remove
			{
				if (_NodesChangedHandlers != null)
				{
					_NodesChangedHandlers = (EventHandler<CxsNodesChangedEventArgs>)Delegate.Remove(_NodesChangedHandlers, value);
				}
			}
		}

		public CxsCase()
		{
			InitializeFields();
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (_MessageSink != null)
				{
					_MessageSink.Case = null;
				}
				if (_RunningCase != null)
				{
					if (_RunningCase.IsRemote)
					{
						_RunningCase.CloseCase(DefaultTimeoutToKillCase);
					}
					else
					{
						_RunningCase.Dispose();
					}
				}
			}
			_MessageSink = null;
			_PendingNodeAssignments = null;
			_RunningCase = null;
		}

		public void OpenCase()
		{
			CloseCase();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseFilePath))
			{
				throw new CxsException("No case path specified");
			}
			SelectAdapterTraits();
			string caseFullFilePath = CaseFullFilePath;
			if (_RemotingRequirements.RemotingOption == CxsRemotingOption.InProcess)
			{
				_RunningCase = _AdapterTraits.CreateAdapterForCase(caseFullFilePath, _VersionRequirements, _AdapterSettings);
			}
			else if (_RemotingRequirements.UseWcfRemoting)
			{
				_RunningCase = CxsWcfCaseAdapter.OpenCase(this, caseFullFilePath);
			}
			else
			{
				_RunningCase = CxsRemotingCaseAdapter.OpenCase(this, caseFullFilePath);
			}
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				AfterOpenCase();
			}
		}

		public void OpenCaseFromByteArray(byte[] caseData)
		{
			CloseCase();
			if (caseData == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("caseData");
			}
			SelectAdapterTraits();
			if (_RemotingRequirements.RemotingOption == CxsRemotingOption.InProcess)
			{
				_RunningCase = _AdapterTraits.CreateAdapterFromByteArray(_CaseFilePath, caseData, _VersionRequirements, AdapterSettings);
			}
			else if (_RemotingRequirements.UseWcfRemoting)
			{
				_RunningCase = CxsWcfCaseAdapter.OpenCaseFromByteArray(this, caseData);
			}
			else
			{
				_RunningCase = CxsRemotingCaseAdapter.OpenCaseFromByteArray(this, caseData);
			}
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				AfterOpenCase();
			}
		}

		public bool CheckForRunningCase()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseFilePath))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ComHelpers.IsFileRunning(CaseFullFilePath);
		}

		public void AttachToRunningCase()
		{
			CloseCase();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseFilePath))
			{
				throw new CxsException("No case path specified");
			}
			SelectAdapterTraits();
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				string caseFullFilePath = CaseFullFilePath;
				obj = ComHelpers.ConnectToRunningFile(caseFullFilePath);
				if (obj == null)
				{
					throw new CxsCaseNotRunningException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Unable to connect to {0}, is the case running?", caseFullFilePath));
				}
				_RunningCase = _AdapterTraits.CreateAdapterForRunningCase(obj, caseFullFilePath, _VersionRequirements, AdapterSettings);
				if (_RunningCase != null)
				{
					_RunningCase.DetachOnClose = true;
					AfterOpenCase();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
					obj = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public void AttachToRunningCase(object runningCase)
		{
			CloseCase();
			SelectAdapterTraits();
			_RunningCase = _AdapterTraits.CreateAdapterForRunningCase(runningCase, _CaseFilePath, _VersionRequirements, _AdapterSettings);
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				_RunningCase.DetachOnClose = true;
				_RunningCasePath = _CaseFilePath;
				AfterOpenCase();
			}
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				throw new CxsCaseNotRunningException("Cannot save, case is not active");
			}
			_RunningCase.Save();
		}

		public void SaveAs(string filePath)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(filePath);
			}
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save, case is not open");
			}
			string text = (Path.IsPathRooted(filePath) ? Path.GetFullPath(filePath) : (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseWorkingDirectory) ? Path.GetFullPath(filePath) : Path.Combine(Path.GetFullPath(_CaseWorkingDirectory), filePath)));
			_RunningCase.SaveAs(text);
			_RunningCasePath = text;
			CaseFilePath = filePath;
		}

		public void SaveCopyAs(string filePath)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save, case is not open");
			}
			if (!CanSaveCopyAs)
			{
				throw new CxsException(ApplicationName + " does not support this action.");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(filePath);
			}
			string filePath2 = (Path.IsPathRooted(filePath) ? Path.GetFullPath(filePath) : (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseWorkingDirectory) ? Path.GetFullPath(filePath) : Path.Combine(Path.GetFullPath(_CaseWorkingDirectory), filePath)));
			_RunningCase.SaveCopyAs(filePath2);
		}

		public byte[] SaveAndCopyToByteArray()
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save, case is not open");
			}
			return _RunningCase.SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
		}

		public void CloseCase()
		{
			CloseCase(DefaultTimeoutToKillCase);
		}

		public bool CloseCase(int timeoutToKill)
		{
			ClearPendingNodeAssignments();
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			ICxsCaseAdapter runningCase = _RunningCase;
			_RunningCase = null;
			_RunningCasePath = string.Empty;
			if (_MessageSink != null)
			{
				_MessageSink.Case = null;
				_MessageSink = null;
			}
			if (_AdapterTraits.ApplicationName == "Aspen HTFS+")
			{
				((EdrAdapterTraits)_AdapterTraits).EDRFileClose();
			}
			bool result = true;
			try
			{
				result = runningCase.CloseCase(timeoutToKill);
				runningCase.Dispose();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void KillCase()
		{
			ClearPendingNodeAssignments();
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ICxsCaseAdapter runningCase = _RunningCase;
			_RunningCase = null;
			_RunningCasePath = string.Empty;
			if (_MessageSink != null)
			{
				_MessageSink.Case = null;
				_MessageSink = null;
			}
			try
			{
				runningCase.KillCase();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private void SelectAdapterTraits()
		{
			string text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseFilePath) ? string.Empty : Path.GetExtension(_CaseFilePath));
			if (_AdapterTraits != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ApplicationRequirement) && ApplicationRequirement != _AdapterTraits.ApplicationName)
				{
					_AdapterTraits = null;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !_AdapterTraits.IsValidExtension(text))
				{
					_AdapterTraits = null;
				}
			}
			if (_AdapterTraits == null)
			{
				_AdapterTraits = CxsAdapterServices.SelectAdapterTraits(ApplicationRequirement, text);
			}
			if (_AdapterTraits == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Could not identify a suitable adapter for the case");
			}
		}

		private void AfterOpenCase()
		{
			_RunningCasePath = _RunningCase.CasePath;
			ConnectMessageSink();
		}

		public CxsHistoryData GetHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double startTime, double endTime, double interval)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null || paths == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _RunningCase.GetHistory(paths, units, timeUnits, startTime, endTime, interval);
		}

		public CxsHistoryData GetMostRecentHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double interval, int numberOfTimePoints)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null || paths == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _RunningCase.GetMostRecentHistory(paths, units, timeUnits, interval, numberOfTimePoints);
		}

		public string[] GetClipboardPaths()
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsCaseInfo caseInfoFromClipboard = CxsServices.GetCaseInfoFromClipboard();
			if (caseInfoFromClipboard == null || caseInfoFromClipboard.ItemMonikers == null || caseInfoFromClipboard.ItemMonikers.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string[] array = ConvertMonikersToPaths(caseInfoFromClipboard.ItemMonikers);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return array;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[i]))
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[j]))
					{
						list.Add(array[j]);
					}
				}
				array = list.ToArray();
			}
			return array;
		}

		public string[] ConvertMonikersToPaths(string[] itemMonikers)
		{
			if (itemMonikers == null || itemMonikers.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string[] array = new string[itemMonikers.Length];
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < itemMonikers.Length; i++)
			{
				if (string.Compare(itemMonikers[i], 0, "cxs:", 0, 4, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
				{
					num++;
				}
				else
				{
					array[i] = itemMonikers[i].Substring(4);
				}
			}
			if (num > 0)
			{
				string[] array2 = new string[num];
				int[] array3 = new int[num];
				int num2 = 0;
				for (int j = 0; j < itemMonikers.Length; j++)
				{
					if (string.Compare(itemMonikers[j], 0, "cxs:", 0, 4, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
					{
						array2[num2] = itemMonikers[j];
						array3[num2] = j;
						num2++;
					}
				}
				string[] array4 = _RunningCase.ConvertMonikersToPaths(array2);
				if (array4 != null)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < array4.Length; k++)
					{
						array[array3[k]] = array4[k];
					}
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		public object GetAutomationProperty(string property, params object[] args)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _RunningCase.GetAutomationProperty(property, args);
		}

		public void SetAutomationProperty(string property, object value, params object[] args)
		{
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				_RunningCase.SetAutomationProperty(property, value, args);
			}
		}

		public object[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, object[] inputs)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _RunningCase.ExecuteVBScript(script, inputs);
		}

		protected void OnPropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, args);
			}
		}

		public void RaisePropertyChanged(string property)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged(new PropertyChangedEventArgs(property));
		}

		public static List<string> GetLocalInstalledSimulatorVersions(SimulatorType simType, SimulatorVersionType verType)
		{
			if (simType != SimulatorType.AspenPlus && simType != SimulatorType.Hysys)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<string> result = null;
			try
			{
				switch (simType)
				{
				case SimulatorType.Hysys:
					result = HysysAdapterTraits.GetInstalledHysysVersionStrings(verType);
					break;
				case SimulatorType.AspenPlus:
					result = AspenPlusAdapterTraits.GetInstalledAspenPlusVersionStrings(verType);
					break;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool IsAPEDInitialized(string version)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(version))
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = true;
			try
			{
				WindowsIdentity current = WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent();
				if (current != null)
				{
					string name = current.Name;
					string text = name;
					if (name.Contains('\\'))
					{
						string text2 = name;
						foreach (char num in text2)
						{
							if (text.Length > 0)
							{
								text = text.Remove(0, 1);
							}
							if (num == '\\')
							{
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					string folderPath = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.CommonApplicationData);
					if (!File.Exists(folderPath + "\\AspenTech\\APED " + version + "\\" + text + "\\config.aem"))
					{
						result = false;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static List<string> GetRemoteInstalledSimulatorVersions(SimulatorType simType, SimulatorVersionType verType, string remoteServer, int port)
		{
			if (simType != SimulatorType.AspenPlus && simType != SimulatorType.Hysys)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsRemotingRequirements cxsRemotingRequirements = new CxsRemotingRequirements();
			cxsRemotingRequirements.RemotePort = port;
			cxsRemotingRequirements.RemoteHostname = remoteServer;
			CxsRemotingService cxsRemotingService = null;
			try
			{
				cxsRemotingService = CxsRemotingService.ConnectToCxsRemotingService(cxsRemotingRequirements);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (cxsRemotingService == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<string> result = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				switch (simType)
				{
				case SimulatorType.Hysys:
					result = cxsRemotingService.GetInstalledVersionsOfHysys(verType);
					break;
				case SimulatorType.AspenPlus:
					result = cxsRemotingService.GetInstalledVersionsOfAspenPlus(verType);
					break;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitializeFields()
		{
			AutoRetrieveMissingNodeData = true;
			DefaultTimeoutToKillCase = 10000;
		}

		public void AddMessageFilter(CxsMessageFilterSpec specification)
		{
			if (_MessageFilters == null)
			{
				_MessageFilters = new List<CxsMessageFilterSpec>();
			}
			_MessageFilters.Add(specification);
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				_RunningCase.ResetMessageFilters((_MessageFilters != null) ? _MessageFilters.ToArray() : null);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveMessageFilter(CxsMessageFilterSpec specification)
		{
			if (_MessageFilters != null)
			{
				_MessageFilters.Remove(specification);
				if (_MessageFilters.Count == 0)
				{
					_MessageFilters = null;
				}
			}
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				_RunningCase.ResetMessageFilters((_MessageFilters != null) ? _MessageFilters.ToArray() : null);
			}
		}

		public void ClearMessageFilters()
		{
			_MessageFilters = null;
			if (_RunningCase != null)
			{
				_RunningCase.ResetMessageFilters((_MessageFilters != null) ? _MessageFilters.ToArray() : null);
			}
		}

		internal void ConnectMessageSink()
		{
			if (_MessageSink == null && _RunningCase != null && (_MessageHandlers != null || (_Solver != null && _Solver.TrackRunState)))
			{
				_MessageSink = new CxsCaseMessageSink(this);
				_RunningCase.ConnectMessageSink(_MessageSink);
				_RunningCase.ResetMessageFilters((_MessageFilters != null) ? _MessageFilters.ToArray() : null);
			}
		}

		internal void DisconnectMessageSink()
		{
			if (_MessageHandlers == null && _Solver != null && _Solver.TrackRunState)
			{
				if (_MessageSink != null)
				{
					_MessageSink.Case = null;
					_MessageSink = null;
				}
				if (_RunningCase != null)
				{
					_RunningCase.DisconnectMessageSink();
				}
			}
		}

		public void RaiseMessage(CxsMessageEventArgs args)
		{
			if (_Solver != null)
			{
				_Solver.OnCaseMessage(args);
			}
			if (_MessageHandlers != null)
			{
				_MessageHandlers(this, args);
			}
		}

		public bool IsValidAction(string nodePath, string action)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(BatchGet(nodePath, "action_is_valid", action));
			if (!flag.HasValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return flag.Value;
		}

		public CxsActionDescriptor GetActionDescriptor(string nodePath, string action)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object value = BatchGet(nodePath, "action_description", action);
			CxsActionDescriptor cxsActionDescriptor = CxsConverter.ValueToActionDescriptor(action, value);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxsActionDescriptor.Name))
			{
				throw new CxsException(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Action {0} is not valid", action));
			}
			return cxsActionDescriptor;
		}

		public CxsNodeTraits[] GetNodeTraits(string[] paths)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (paths == null || paths.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (string path in paths)
			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "valid_attributes");
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "value_type");
			}
			object[] array = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			CxsNodeTraits[] array2 = new CxsNodeTraits[paths.Length];
			for (int j = 0; j < paths.Length; j++)
			{
				string[] array3 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(array[2 * j]);
				if (array3 == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				array2[j] = new CxsNodeTraits(array3);
				CxsNodeValueType valueType = CxsNodeValueType.Unknown;
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[2 * j + 1]);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (text == "none")
					{
						valueType = CxsNodeValueType.None;
					}
					switch (text)
					{
					case "integer":
						valueType = CxsNodeValueType.Integer;
						break;
					case "real":
						valueType = CxsNodeValueType.Real;
						break;
					case "boolean":
						valueType = CxsNodeValueType.Boolean;
						break;
					case "string":
						valueType = CxsNodeValueType.String;
						break;
					case "enum":
						valueType = CxsNodeValueType.Enumeration;
						break;
					}
				}
				array2[j].ValueType = valueType;
			}
			return array2;
		}

		}

			cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, attribute, unit);
			object[] array = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);

				return null;

			return array[0];
		}

		public CxsBatchRequestData BatchGetAllAttributes(string[] paths, string[] units)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null || paths == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsNodeTraits[] nodeTraits = GetNodeTraits(paths);
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			CxsNodeTraits[] array = nodeTraits;
			foreach (CxsNodeTraits cxsNodeTraits in array)
			{
				if (cxsNodeTraits == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (string validAttribute in cxsNodeTraits.ValidAttributes)
				{
					int num = list.BinarySearch(validAttribute);
					if (num < 0)
					{
						list.Insert(~num, validAttribute);
					}
				}
			}
			return BatchGet(paths, units, list.ToArray());
		}

		public CxsVerificationResult[] VerifyPaths(string[] paths)
		{
			return VerifyPathsAndUnits(paths, null);
		}

		public CxsVerificationResult[] VerifyPathsAndUnits(string[] paths, string[] units)
		{
			if (paths == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = paths.Length;
			CxsVerificationResult[] array = new CxsVerificationResult[num];
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				array[i] = CxsVerificationResult.InvalidPath;
			}
			if (_RunningCase == null)
			{
				return array;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			string[] array2 = paths;
			foreach (string path in array2)
			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "id");
				if (units != null)
				{
					cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "uom_options");
				}
			}
			object[] array3 = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			if (array3 == null)
			{
				return array;
			}
			int num2 = ((units == null) ? 1 : 2);
			for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array3[k * num2])))
				{
					array[k] = CxsVerificationResult.InvalidPath;
					continue;
				}
				if (units == null)
				{
					array[k] = CxsVerificationResult.Valid;
					continue;
				}
				string text = ((k < units.Length) ? units[k] : string.Empty);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					array[k] = CxsVerificationResult.Valid;
					continue;
				}
				array[k] = CxsVerificationResult.InvalidUnits;
				string[] array4 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(array3[k * num2 + 1]);
				if (array4 == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				array2 = array4;
				foreach (string strB in array2)
				{
					if (string.Compare(text, strB, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
					{
						array[k] = CxsVerificationResult.Valid;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		public string[] GetChildPaths(string path)
		{
			return GetChildPaths(new string[1] { path });
		}

		public string[] GetChildPaths(string[] paths)
		{
			if (_RunningCase == null || paths == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (paths == null || paths.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			string[] array = paths;
			foreach (string path in array)
			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "child_ids");
			}
			object[] array2 = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			for (int j = 0; j < paths.Length; j++)
			{
				string[] array3 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(array2[j]);
				if (array3 != null)
				{
					array = array3;
					foreach (string id in array)
					{
						list.Add(CxsPath.CombinePathAndId(paths[j], id));
					}
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public CxsNode ResolveNode(string basePath, string path, CxsNodeRefreshOptions refreshOptions)
		{
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection = ResolveNodes(basePath, new string[1] { path }, refreshOptions);
			if (cxsNodeCollection == null || cxsNodeCollection.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return cxsNodeCollection[0];
		}

		public CxsNodeCollection ResolveNodes(string[] paths)
		{
			return ResolveNodes(paths, null);
		}

		public CxsNodeCollection ResolveNodes(string[] childPaths, CxsNodeRefreshOptions refreshOptions)
		{
			return ResolveNodes(string.Empty, childPaths, refreshOptions);
		}

		public CxsNodeCollection ResolveNodes(string basePath, string[] paths, CxsNodeRefreshOptions refreshOptions)
		{
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection = new CxsNodeCollection();
			if (paths == null)
			{
				return cxsNodeCollection;
			}
			CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction cxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction = CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction.RemoveUnboundNodes;
			if (refreshOptions != null && refreshOptions.UnboundNodeAction != CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction = refreshOptions.UnboundNodeAction;
			}
			List<CxsNode> list = new List<CxsNode>();
			for (int i = 0; i < paths.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = CxsPath.CombinePaths(basePath, paths[i]);
				CxsNode cxsNode = null;
				WeakReference value = null;
				if (_CachedNodes.TryGetValue(text, out value))
				{
					if (value.IsAlive)
					{
						cxsNode = value.Target as CxsNode;
					}
					else
					{
						_CachedNodes.Remove(text);
					}
				}
				if (cxsNode == null)
				{
					string nodeDefaultUnitsSpec = GetNodeDefaultUnitsSpec(text);
					cxsNode = new CxsNode(this, text, nodeDefaultUnitsSpec);
					_CachedNodes.Add(text, new WeakReference(cxsNode));
					if (cxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction != CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction.Leave)
					{
						list.Add(cxsNode);
					}
				}
				cxsNodeCollection.Add(cxsNode);
			}
			RefreshNodes(cxsNodeCollection, refreshOptions);
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction != CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction.Leave)
			{
				for (int num = cxsNodeCollection.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					if (!cxsNodeCollection[num].IsBound)
					{
						if (cxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction == CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction.ReplaceWithNull)
						{
							cxsNodeCollection[num] = null;
						}
						else
						{
							cxsNodeCollection.RemoveAt(num);
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (CxsNode item in list)
				{
					if (!item.IsBound)
					{
						_CachedNodes.Remove(item.Path);
					}
				}
			}
			return cxsNodeCollection;
		}

		public void RefreshChildNodes()
		{
			RefreshChildNodes(null);
		}

		public void RefreshChildNodes(CxsNodeRefreshOptions options)
		{
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection = null;
			if (_ChildNodesReference != null && _ChildNodesReference.IsAlive)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection = _ChildNodesReference.Target as CxsNodeCollection;
			}
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection2 = ResolveNodes(new string[1] { "Top" }, options);
			if (cxsNodeCollection == null)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection = ((cxsNodeCollection2 != null) ? cxsNodeCollection2 : new CxsNodeCollection());
				_ChildNodesReference = new WeakReference(cxsNodeCollection);
				return;
			}
			if (cxsNodeCollection2 == null || cxsNodeCollection2.Count == 0)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection.Clear();
				return;
			}
			if (cxsNodeCollection.Count == 0)
			{
				foreach (CxsNode item in cxsNodeCollection2)
				{
					cxsNodeCollection.Add(item);
				}
				return;
			}
			for (int num = cxsNodeCollection.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				int num2 = cxsNodeCollection2.IndexOf(cxsNodeCollection[num]);
				if (num2 >= 0)
				{
					cxsNodeCollection2.RemoveAt(num2);
				}
				else
				{
					cxsNodeCollection.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			foreach (CxsNode item2 in cxsNodeCollection2)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection.Add(item2);
			}
		}

		public void RefreshAllNodes()
		{
			RefreshAllNodes(null);
		}

		public void RefreshAllNodes(CxsNodeRefreshOptions refreshOptions)
		{
			CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default;
			if (refreshOptions != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = refreshOptions.ValidationSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting == CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateOnlyUnboundNodes;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting != CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateNone)
			{
				List<CxsNode> list = new List<CxsNode>();
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (WeakReference value in _CachedNodes.Values)
				{
					if (value.IsAlive)
					{
						CxsNode cxsNode = value.Target as CxsNode;
						if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting == CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateAllNodes || !cxsNode.IsBound)
						{
							list.Add(cxsNode);
							list2.Add(cxsNode.Path);
						}
					}
				}
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CxsNodeTraits[] nodeTraits = GetNodeTraits(list2.ToArray());
					if (nodeTraits != null)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
						{
							list[i].ResetNodeTraits(nodeTraits[i]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default;
			if (refreshOptions != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = refreshOptions.UpdateSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.ExcludeKnownConstants;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<CxsNode> list3 = new List<CxsNode>();
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (WeakReference value2 in _CachedNodes.Values)
			{
				if (value2.IsAlive)
				{
					CxsNode cxsNode2 = value2.Target as CxsNode;
					int requestsCount = cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount;
					cxsNode2.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData, refreshOptions);
					if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount > requestsCount)
					{
						list3.Add(cxsNode2);
					}
				}
			}
			if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			cxsBatchRequestData.UpdateRequestValues(array);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsNodesChangedEventArgs e = null;
			if (_NodesChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				e = new CxsNodesChangedEventArgs();
			}
			foreach (CxsNode item in list3)
			{
				item.StoreResultsFromDataRefresh(cxsBatchRequestData, e);
			}
			RaiseNodesChangedEvent(e);
		}

		public void RefreshNodes(CxsNode[] nodes)
		{
			RefreshNodes(nodes, null);
		}

		public void RefreshNodes(CxsNode[] nodes, CxsNodeRefreshOptions refreshOptions)
		{
			CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default;
			if (refreshOptions != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = refreshOptions.ValidationSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting == CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateOnlyUnboundNodes;
			}
			CxsNode[] array;
			if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting != CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateNone)
			{
				List<CxsNode> list = new List<CxsNode>();
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				array = nodes;
				foreach (CxsNode cxsNode in array)
				{
					if (cxsNode != null && cxsNode.Case == this && (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting == CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateAllNodes || !cxsNode.IsBound))
					{
						list.Add(cxsNode);
						list2.Add(cxsNode.Path);
					}
				}
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CxsNodeTraits[] nodeTraits = GetNodeTraits(list2.ToArray());
					if (nodeTraits != null)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < list.Count; j++)
						{
							list[j].ResetNodeTraits(nodeTraits[j]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default;
			if (refreshOptions != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = refreshOptions.UpdateSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.ExcludeKnownConstants;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			array = nodes;
			foreach (CxsNode cxsNode2 in array)
			{
				if (cxsNode2 != null && cxsNode2.Case == this)
				{
					cxsNode2.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData, refreshOptions);
				}
			}
			if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array2 = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			cxsBatchRequestData.UpdateRequestValues(array2);
			if (array2 != null)
			{
				CxsNodesChangedEventArgs e = null;
				if (_NodesChangedHandlers != null)
				{
					e = new CxsNodesChangedEventArgs();
				}
				array = nodes;
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					array[i].StoreResultsFromDataRefresh(cxsBatchRequestData, e);
				}
				RaiseNodesChangedEvent(e);
			}
		}

		public void RefreshNodes(IEnumerable<CxsNode> nodes)
		{
			RefreshNodes(nodes, null);
		}

		public void RefreshNodes(IEnumerable<CxsNode> nodes, CxsNodeRefreshOptions refreshOptions)
		{
			CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default;
			if (refreshOptions != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = refreshOptions.ValidationSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting == CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateOnlyUnboundNodes;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting != CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateNone)
			{
				List<CxsNode> list = new List<CxsNode>();
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (CxsNode node in nodes)
				{
					if (node != null && node.Case == this && (cxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting == CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateAllNodes || !node.IsBound))
					{
						list.Add(node);
						list2.Add(node.Path);
					}
				}
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CxsNodeTraits[] nodeTraits = GetNodeTraits(list2.ToArray());
					if (nodeTraits != null)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
						{
							list[i].ResetNodeTraits(nodeTraits[i]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default;
			if (refreshOptions != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = refreshOptions.UpdateSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.ExcludeKnownConstants;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (CxsNode node2 in nodes)
			{
				if (node2 != null && node2.Case == this)
				{
					node2.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData, refreshOptions);
				}
			}
			if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			cxsBatchRequestData.UpdateRequestValues(array);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsNodesChangedEventArgs e = null;
			if (_NodesChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				e = new CxsNodesChangedEventArgs();
			}
			foreach (CxsNode node3 in nodes)
			{
				node3.StoreResultsFromDataRefresh(cxsBatchRequestData, e);
			}
			RaiseNodesChangedEvent(e);
		}

		internal void QueueNodeAssignment(CxsNode node, string attribute, object value)
		{
			CxsNodesChangedEventArgs e = null;
			if (_NodesChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				e = new CxsNodesChangedEventArgs();
			}
			if (attribute == "uom")
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(value);
				bool flag = true;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					flag = false;
					ReadOnlyCollection<string> unitsList = node.UnitsList;
					if (unitsList != null)
					{
						string text2 = string.Empty;
						foreach (string item in unitsList)
						{
							if (string.Compare(text, item, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
							if (string.Compare(text, item, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
							{
								text2 = item;
							}
						}
						if (!flag && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text = text2;
							flag = true;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					if (_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs = new Dictionary<string, string>();
					}
					if (_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs != null)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs[node.Path] = text;
						}
						else
						{
							_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs.Remove(node.Path);
						}
					}
					node.InternalSetUnitsSpec(text, e);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(node.Path, attribute, node.UnitsSpec, value);
				node.StoreResultsFromDataRefresh(cxsBatchRequestData, e);
				if (!QueueNodeAssignments)
				{
					BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
				}
				else
				{
					if (_PendingNodeAssignments == null)
					{
						_PendingNodeAssignments = new CxsBatchRequestData();
					}
					_PendingNodeAssignments.AddRequest(node.Path, attribute, node.UnitsSpec, value);
				}
			}
			RaiseNodesChangedEvent(e);
			if (!QueueNodeAssignments)
			{
				node.RefreshData();
			}
		}

		private void RaiseNodesChangedEvent(CxsNodesChangedEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (_NodesChangedHandlers != null && eventArgs != null && eventArgs.RecordCount > 0)
			{
				_NodesChangedHandlers(this, eventArgs);
			}
		}

		public void SetNodeDefaultUnitsSpec(string path, string unitsSpec)
		{
			if (_CachedNodes.TryGetValue(path, out var value) && value.IsAlive)
			{
				(value.Target as CxsNode).UnitsSpec = unitsSpec;
				return;
			}
			if (_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs == null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitsSpec))
			{
				_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			}
			if (_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitsSpec))
				{
					_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs[path] = unitsSpec;
				}
				else
				{
					_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs.Remove(path);
				}
			}
		}

		public string GetNodeDefaultUnitsSpec(string path)
		{
			string value = string.Empty;
			if (_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs != null)
			{
				_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs.TryGetValue(path, out value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		public void ResetAllNodeUnitSpecs()
		{
			foreach (WeakReference value in _CachedNodes.Values)
			{
				if (value.IsAlive)
				{
					(value.Target as CxsNode).UnitsSpec = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			_SavedCxsNodeUnitsSpecs = null;
		}

		public void FlushPendingNodeAssignments(bool refreshAffectedNodes)
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData pendingNodeAssignments = _PendingNodeAssignments;
			_PendingNodeAssignments = null;
			if (pendingNodeAssignments == null || pendingNodeAssignments.RequestsCount == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			BatchSet(pendingNodeAssignments);
			if (!refreshAffectedNodes)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection = new CxsNodeCollection();
			foreach (string canonicalPath in pendingNodeAssignments.CanonicalPaths)
			{
				if (_CachedNodes.TryGetValue(canonicalPath, out var value) && value != null && value.IsAlive)
				{
					cxsNodeCollection.Add(value.Target as CxsNode);
				}
			}
			RefreshNodes(cxsNodeCollection, null);
		}

		public void ClearPendingNodeAssignments()
		{
			_PendingNodeAssignments = null;
		}
	}

	public enum CxsRemotingOption
	{
		InProcess,
		RemoteService
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsRemotingRequirements
	{
		public static int DefaultServicePortNumber => 9015;

		public CxsRemotingOption RemotingOption { get; set; }

		public string RemoteHostname { get; set; }

		public int RemotePort { get; set; }

		public bool UseWcfRemoting
		{
			get
			{
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
				UseWcfRemoting = value;
			}
		}

		public string RemoteChannel
		{
			get
			{
				int num = ((RemotePort != 0) ? RemotePort : DefaultServicePortNumber);
				string arg = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(RemoteHostname) ? "localhost" : RemoteHostname);
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "tcp://{0}:{1}", arg, num);
			}
		}

		public Uri WcfHttpBaseUri
		{
			get
			{
				int num = ((RemotePort != 0) ? RemotePort : DefaultServicePortNumber);
				string arg = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(RemoteHostname) ? "localhost" : RemoteHostname);
				return new Uri(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "http://{0}:{1}/AspenRemoteSimulationService/Services", arg, num));
			}
		}

		public Uri WcfHttpCaseServicesUri
		{
			get
			{
				int num = ((RemotePort != 0) ? RemotePort : DefaultServicePortNumber);
				string arg = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(RemoteHostname) ? "localhost" : RemoteHostname);
				return new Uri(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "http://{0}:{1}/AspenRemoteSimulationService/Services/CaseServices", arg, num));
			}
		}

		public CxsRemotingRequirements()
		{
			RemotingOption = CxsRemotingOption.InProcess;
		}
	}
	public static class CxsServices
	{
		public static bool VerifyRemotingRequirements(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			if (requirements.RemotingOption == CxsRemotingOption.InProcess)
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (requirements.UseWcfRemoting)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
			return CxsRemotingService.VerityRemotingRequirements(requirements);
		}

		public static CxsCaseInfo GetCaseInfoFromClipboard()
		{
			return CxsAsiClipboardFileInfo.ReadFromClipboard();
		}

		public static bool CxsClipboardDataExists()
		{
			return CxsAsiClipboardFileInfo.CxsClipboardDataExists();
		}

		public static CxsAppInfo[] GetAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options)
		{
			return GetAppInfo(options, null);
		}

		public static CxsAppInfo[] GetAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			CxsAppInfo[] array = null;
			if (adapterSettings == null)
			{
				adapterSettings = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			}
			if (options.Remoting.RemotingOption == CxsRemotingOption.InProcess)
			{
				return CxsAdapterServices.GetAppInfo(options, adapterSettings);
			}
			if (options.Remoting.UseWcfRemoting)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
			return CxsRemotingService.ConnectToCxsRemotingService(options.Remoting).GetAppInfo(options, adapterSettings);
		}

		public static string CreateFileFilterFromAppInfo(CxsAppInfo[] allAppInfo)
		{
			if (allAppInfo == null || allAppInfo.Length == 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("All supported files (");
			bool flag = false;
			CxsAppInfo[] array = allAppInfo;
			foreach (CxsAppInfo cxsAppInfo in array)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < cxsAppInfo.FileExtensionsCount; j++)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(',');
					}
					else
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					stringBuilder.Append('*');
					stringBuilder.Append(cxsAppInfo.FileExtensions(j));
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(")|");
			bool flag2 = false;
			array = allAppInfo;
			foreach (CxsAppInfo cxsAppInfo2 in array)
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < cxsAppInfo2.FileExtensionsCount; k++)
				{
					if (flag2)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(';');
					}
					else
					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
					stringBuilder.Append('*');
					stringBuilder.Append(cxsAppInfo2.FileExtensions(k));
				}
			}
			array = allAppInfo;
			foreach (CxsAppInfo cxsAppInfo3 in array)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append("|");
				stringBuilder.Append(cxsAppInfo3.AppName);
				stringBuilder.Append(" Files (");
				for (int l = 0; l < cxsAppInfo3.FileExtensionsCount; l++)
				{
					if (l > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(',');
					}
					stringBuilder.Append('*');
					stringBuilder.Append(cxsAppInfo3.FileExtensions(l));
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(")|");
				for (int m = 0; m < cxsAppInfo3.FileExtensionsCount; m++)
				{
					if (m > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(';');
					}
					stringBuilder.Append('*');
					stringBuilder.Append(cxsAppInfo3.FileExtensions(m));
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}

	[Serializable]
	public class CxsVersionRequirements
	{
		private string _origVerString = string.Empty;

		private string _fixedVerString = string.Empty;

		public CxsVersionOption VersionOption { get; set; }

		public string VersionString
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_origVerString) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_fixedVerString))
				{
					_fixedVerString = GetProperVersionString(_origVerString);
				}
				return _fixedVerString;
			}
			set
			{
				_origVerString = value;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_origVerString))
				{
					_fixedVerString = GetProperVersionString(_origVerString);
				}
			}
		}

		public CxsVersionRequirements()
		{
			VersionOption = CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion;
			VersionString = string.Empty;
		}

		private static string GetProperVersionString(string orig)
		{
			string text = orig;
			string versionDigits = GetVersionDigits(orig);
			if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "20065") == 0)
			{
				text = "2006.5";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "70") == 0)
			{
				text = "V7.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "7") == 0)
			{
				text = "V7.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "71") == 0)
			{
				text = "V7.1";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "72") == 0)
			{
				text = "V7.2";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "73") == 0)
			{
				text = "V7.3";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "732") == 0)
			{
				text = "V7.3.2";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "80") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "8") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "82") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.2";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "820") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.2";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "83") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.3";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "830") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.3";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "84") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.4";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "840") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.4";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "86") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.6";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "860") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.6";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "88") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.8";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "880") == 0)
			{
				text = "V8.8";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "9") == 0)
			{
				text = "V9.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "90") == 0)
			{
				text = "V9.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "900") == 0)
			{
				text = "V9.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "100") == 0)
			{
				text = "V10.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "1000") == 0)
			{
				text = "V10.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "110") == 0)
			{
				text = "V11.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "1100") == 0)
			{
				text = "V11.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "120") == 0)
			{
				text = "V12.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "1200") == 0)
			{
				text = "V12.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "121") == 0)
			{
				text = "V12.1";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "1210") == 0)
			{
				text = "V12.1";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "140") == 0)
			{
				text = "V14.0";
			}
			else if (string.CompareOrdinal(versionDigits, "1400") == 0)
			{
				text = "V14.0";
			}
			else
			{
				text = Regex.Replace(orig, "[^0-9.]", "");
				while (text.StartsWith("0") || text.StartsWith("."))
				{
					text = text.TrimStart('0');
					text = text.TrimStart('.');
				}
				while ((text.EndsWith("0") || text.EndsWith(".")) && text.Length >= 3)
				{
					text = text.TrimEnd('0');
					text = text.TrimEnd('.');
				}
				text = text.Insert(0, "V");
				if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "^V\\d{2}"))
				{
					if (text.Length <= 4)
					{
						text = text.Insert(3, ".0");
					}
				}
				else if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "^V\\d{1}") && text.Length <= 3)
				{
					text = text.Insert(2, ".0");
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private static string GetVersionDigits(string verString)
		{
			string text = verString;
			try
			{
				string text2 = text.ToLower();
				text2 = text2.Replace("version", "");
				text2 = text2.Replace("ver", "");
				text2 = text2.Replace("v", "");
				text2 = text2.Replace(".", "");
				text2 = text2.Replace(",", "");
				text2 = text2.Replace(" ", "");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text = text2;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return text;
		}
	}
	public enum CxsMessageType
	{
		GeneralInformation = 0,
		GeneralError = 1,
		GeneralWarning = 2,
		RunStarted = 3,
		RunFailed = 4,
		RunCompleted = 5,
		RunStepCompleted = 6,
		RunPaused = 7,
		SolverStateChanged = 8,
		DocumentClosed = 9,
		ActionStarted = 10,
		ActionFailed = 11,
		ActionCompleted = 12,
		Custom = 13,
		EconAnalysisCompleted = 31,
		RunPostProcessingCompleted = 32,
		OptimizerStarted = 33,
		OptimizerCompleted = 34
	}
	public enum CxsMessageSeverity
	{
		SystemError = -1,
		TerminalError,
		SevereError,
		Error,
		Warning,
		Information,
		Diagnostic
	}
	[Serializable]
	public struct CxsMessage
	{
		private CxsMessageType _MessageType;

		private CxsMessageSeverity _Severity;

		private int _Number;

		private int _System;

		private string _Text;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<object> _MessageData;

		public CxsMessageType MessageType => _MessageType;

		public CxsMessageSeverity Severity => _Severity;

		public string Text => _Text;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<object> MessageData => _MessageData;

		public int MessageDataCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (MessageData == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return MessageData.Count;
			}
		}

		public int Number => _Number;

		public int System => _System;

		public string HeaderText => "Severity: " + Severity.ToString() + ", System: " + System + ", Number" + Number;

		public bool IsErrorMessage
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = false;
				CxsMessageSeverity severity = Severity;
				if ((uint)(severity - -1) <= 3u)
				{
					result = true;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public bool IsWarningMessage => Severity == CxsMessageSeverity.Warning;

		public bool IsInformationMessage
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = false;
				CxsMessageSeverity severity = Severity;
				if ((uint)(severity - 4) <= 1u)
				{
					result = true;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public CxsMessage(CxsMessageType type, CxsMessageSeverity severity, int number, int system, string text, ReadOnlyCollection<object> messageData)
		{
			_MessageType = type;
			_Severity = severity;
			_Number = number;
			_System = system;
			_Text = text;
			_MessageData = messageData;
		}

		public object MessageDataItem(int index)
		{
			if (MessageData == null || index >= MessageData.Count)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return MessageData[index];
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null || obj.GetType() != typeof(CxsMessage))
			{
				return false;
			}
			CxsMessage cxsMessage = (CxsMessage)obj;
			if (_Severity == cxsMessage._Severity && _MessageType == cxsMessage._MessageType && _Number == cxsMessage._Number && _System == cxsMessage._System && _Text == cxsMessage._Text)
			{
				return _MessageData == cxsMessage._MessageData;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool operator ==(CxsMessage rhs, CxsMessage lhs)
		{
			return rhs.Equals(lhs);
		}

		public static bool operator !=(CxsMessage rhs, CxsMessage lhs)
		{
			return !rhs.Equals(lhs);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			int num = 0;
			num ^= _Severity.GetHashCode();
			num ^= _MessageType.GetHashCode();
			num ^= _Number.GetHashCode();
			num ^= _System.GetHashCode();
			num ^= _Text.GetHashCode();
			if (_MessageData != null)
			{
				num ^= _MessageData.GetHashCode();
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsMessageEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessage> Messages { get; private set; }

		public int MessageCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (Messages == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return Messages.Count;
			}
		}

		public int ErrorCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (CxsMessage message in Messages)
				{
					if (message.IsErrorMessage)
					{
						num++;
					}
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public int WarningCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (CxsMessage message in Messages)
				{
					if (message.IsWarningMessage)
					{
						num++;
					}
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public CxsMessageEventArgs(ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessage> messages)
		{
			Messages = messages;
		}

		public CxsMessage Message(int index)
		{
			return Messages[index];
		}
	}

	public class CxsActionParameter

		public string Summary { get; set; }

		public Type ParameterType { get; set; }

		public bool Optional { get; set; }
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsActionDescriptor
	{
		private string _Name;

		private string _Summary;

		private string _Returns;

		private Type _ReturnType;

		private CxsActionParameter[] _Parameters;

		public string Name
		{
			get
			{
				return _Name;
			}
			set
			{
				_Name = value;
			}
		}

		public string Summary
		{
			get
			{
				return _Summary;
			}
			set
			{
				_Summary = value;
			}
		}

		public int ParameterCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (_Parameters == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _Parameters.Length;
			}
		}

		public string Returns
		{
			get
			{
				return _Returns;
			}
			set
			{
				_Returns = value;
			}
		}

		public Type ReturnType
		{
			get
			{
				return _ReturnType;
			}
			set
			{
				_ReturnType = value;
			}
		}

		public bool RequiresParameters
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = false;
				if (_Parameters != null)
				{
					CxsActionParameter[] parameters = _Parameters;
					for (int i = 0; i < parameters.Length; i++)
					{
						if (!parameters[i].Optional)
						{
							result = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public CxsActionDescriptor()
		{
		}

		public CxsActionDescriptor(string name, string summary, string returns, Type returnType, CxsActionParameter[] parameters)
		{
			_Name = name;
			_Summary = summary;
			_Returns = returns;
			_ReturnType = returnType;
			_Parameters = parameters;
		}

		public CxsActionParameter Parameter(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= ParameterCount)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			return _Parameters[index];
		}
	}

	[Serializable]
	public class CxsAppInfo
	{
		private string _AppName;

		private string[] _FileExtensions;

		private string _FileFilter;

		private string[] _InstalledVersions;

		public string AppName
		{
			get
			{
				return _AppName;
			}
			set
			{
				_AppName = value;
			}
		}

		public int FileExtensionsCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (_FileExtensions == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _FileExtensions.Length;
			}
		}

		public string FileFilter
		{
			get
			{
				return _FileFilter;
			}
			set
			{
				_FileFilter = value;
			}
		}

		public int InstalledVersionsCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (_InstalledVersions == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _InstalledVersions.Length;
			}
		}

		public CxsAppInfo()
		{
		}

		public CxsAppInfo(string appName, string[] fileExtensions, string fileFilter, string[] installedVersions)
		{
			_AppName = appName;
			_FileFilter = fileFilter;
			if (fileExtensions != null)
			{
				_FileExtensions = new string[fileExtensions.Length];
				fileExtensions.CopyTo(_FileExtensions, 0);
			}
			if (installedVersions != null)
			{
				_InstalledVersions = new string[installedVersions.Length];
				installedVersions.CopyTo(_InstalledVersions, 0);
			}
		}

		public string FileExtensions(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= FileExtensionsCount)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			return _FileExtensions[index];
		}

		public string InstalledVersions(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > InstalledVersionsCount)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			return _InstalledVersions[index];
		}
	}
