	internal class CxsAsiClipboardFileInfo
	{
		public const string AsiClipFormat = "ASI Format";

		public const string AsiUnicodeClipFormat = "ASI UNICODE Format";

		public const string AsiFileClipFormat = "ASI File Format";

		public const string AsiUnicodeFileClipFormat = "ASI File UNICODE Format";

		private string _Application;

		private string _Version;

		private string _FileMoniker;

		private bool _IsGridFormat;

		private List<string> _ItemMonikers;

		private List<CxsClipboardGridCell> _GridData;

		public CxsAsiClipboardFileInfo()
		{
			_Application = string.Empty;
			_Version = string.Empty;
			_FileMoniker = string.Empty;
			_ItemMonikers = new List<string>();
			_GridData = new List<CxsClipboardGridCell>();
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			_Application = string.Empty;
			_FileMoniker = string.Empty;
			_Version = string.Empty;
			_ItemMonikers.Clear();
			_GridData.Clear();
		}

		public void LoadFromXml(string asiXml)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(asiXml))
			{
				return;
			}
			using XmlTextReader xmlTextReader = new XmlTextReader(new StringReader(asiXml));
			bool flag = true;
			while (xmlTextReader.Read() && flag)
			{
				if (xmlTextReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && (xmlTextReader.Name == "SelectionFile" || xmlTextReader.Name == "Selection"))
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Could not find outer SelectionFile element");
			}
			while (xmlTextReader.Read())
			{
				if (xmlTextReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					switch (xmlTextReader.Name)
					{
					case "Format":
					{
						string strA = ReadStringElement(xmlTextReader);
						_IsGridFormat = string.Compare(strA, "Grid", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
						break;
					}
					case "Product":
						_Application = ReadStringElement(xmlTextReader);
						break;
					case "Version":
						_Version = ReadStringElement(xmlTextReader);
						break;
					case "FileMoniker":
						_FileMoniker = ReadStringElement(xmlTextReader);
						break;
					case "Items":
						LoadItemsFromReader(xmlTextReader);
						break;
					default:
						throw new CxsException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Unrecognised element {0}", xmlTextReader.Name));
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadItemsFromReader(XmlReader reader)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read() && flag && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "Item")
				{
					LoadItemFromReader(reader);
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "Text")
				{
					LoadTextFromReader(reader);
					continue;
				}
				throw new CxsException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Unrecognised element {0}", reader.Name));
			}
		}

		private void LoadTextFromReader(XmlReader reader)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			string name = reader.Name;
			int result = 0;
			int result2 = 0;
			string text = string.Empty;
			while (reader.Read() && flag && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "Value")
				{
					text = ReadStringElement(reader);
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "Row")
				{
					string text2 = ReadStringElement(reader);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && !int.TryParse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result))
					{
						result = 0;
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "Col")
				{
					string text3 = ReadStringElement(reader);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && !int.TryParse(text3, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2))
					{
						result2 = 0;
					}
					continue;
				}
				throw new CxsException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Unrecognised element {0}", reader.Name));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && _IsGridFormat)
			{
				CxsClipboardGridCell cxsClipboardGridCell = new CxsClipboardGridCell();
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Row = result;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Column = result2;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Attribute = string.Empty;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Moniker = string.Empty;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.TextValue = text;
				_GridData.Add(cxsClipboardGridCell);
			}
		}

		private void LoadItemFromReader(XmlReader reader)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			string name = reader.Name;
			int result = 0;
			int result2 = 0;
			string attribute = string.Empty;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string textValue = string.Empty;
			_ = string.Empty;
			while (reader.Read() && flag && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "Path")
				{
					text = ReadStringElement(reader);
				}
				else if (reader.Name == "Row")
				{
					string text2 = ReadStringElement(reader);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && !int.TryParse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result))
					{
						result = 0;
					}
				}
				else if (reader.Name == "Col")
				{
					string text3 = ReadStringElement(reader);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && !int.TryParse(text3, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2))
					{
						result2 = 0;
					}
				}
				else if (reader.Name == "Attribute")
				{
					attribute = ReadStringElement(reader);
				}
				else if (reader.Name == "Value")
				{
					textValue = ReadStringElement(reader);
				}
				else
				{
					if (!(reader.Name == "Name"))
					{
						throw new CxsException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Unrecognised element {0}", reader.Name));
					}
					ReadStringElement(reader);
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag2 = true;
			foreach (string itemMoniker in _ItemMonikers)
			{
				if (string.Compare(itemMoniker, text, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					text = itemMoniker;
					flag2 = false;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				_ItemMonikers.Add(text);
			}
			if (_IsGridFormat)
			{
				CxsClipboardGridCell cxsClipboardGridCell = new CxsClipboardGridCell();
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Row = result;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Column = result2;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Attribute = attribute;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.Moniker = text;
				cxsClipboardGridCell.TextValue = textValue;
				_GridData.Add(cxsClipboardGridCell);
			}
		}

		private static string ReadStringElement(XmlReader reader)
		{
			if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				switch (reader.NodeType)
				{
				case XmlNodeType.Text:
				case XmlNodeType.CDATA:
					stringBuilder.Append(reader.Value);
					continue;
				default:
					continue;
				case XmlNodeType.EndElement:
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public CxsCaseInfo ConvertToCaseInfo()
		{
			CxsCaseInfo cxsCaseInfo = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_FileMoniker))
			{
				cxsCaseInfo = new CxsCaseInfo();
				cxsCaseInfo.SetData(_Application, _Version, _FileMoniker, _ItemMonikers.ToArray(), _GridData.ToArray());
			}
			return cxsCaseInfo;
		}

		private static string ReadUTF8StringFromDataObject(System.Windows.Forms.IDataObject dataObject, string clipFormatName)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				DataFormats.Format format = DataFormats.GetFormat(clipFormatName);
				if (!dataObject.GetDataPresent(format.Name))
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				object data = dataObject.GetData(format.Name);
				if (data == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (data.GetType() == typeof(MemoryStream))
				{
					MemoryStream obj = data as MemoryStream;
					int num = (int)obj.Length;
					byte[] array = new byte[num];
					obj.Read(array, 0, num);
					Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
					char[] array2 = new char[decoder.GetCharCount(array, 0, num)];
					decoder.GetChars(array, 0, num, array2, 0);
					text = new string(array2);
				}
				else
				{
					if (!(data.GetType() == typeof(string)))
					{
						return string.Empty;
					}
					text = data as string;
				}
				if (text[text.Length - 1] == '\0')
				{
					text = text.Remove(text.Length - 1, 1);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception arg)
			{
				Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception raised while parsing clipboard data: {0}", arg));
			}
			return text;
		}

		private static string ReadUTF16StringFromDataObject(System.Windows.Forms.IDataObject dataObject, string clipFormatName)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				DataFormats.Format format = DataFormats.GetFormat(clipFormatName);
				if (!dataObject.GetDataPresent(format.Name))
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				object data = dataObject.GetData(format.Name);
				if (data == null || data.GetType() != typeof(MemoryStream))
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				MemoryStream obj = data as MemoryStream;
				int num = (int)obj.Length;
				byte[] array = new byte[num];
				obj.Read(array, 0, num);
				Decoder decoder = Encoding.Unicode.GetDecoder();
				char[] array2 = new char[decoder.GetCharCount(array, 0, num)];
				decoder.GetChars(array, 0, num, array2, 0);
				text = new string(array2);
				if (text[text.Length - 1] == '\0')
				{
					text = text.Remove(text.Length - 1, 1);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception arg)
			{
				Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception raised while parsing clipboard data: {0}", arg));
			}
			return text;
		}

		public static CxsCaseInfo ReadFromClipboard()
		{
			CxsAsiClipboardFileInfo cxsAsiClipboardFileInfo = new CxsAsiClipboardFileInfo();
			try
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.IDataObject dataObject = Clipboard.GetDataObject();
				if (dataObject == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
				{
					cxsAsiClipboardFileInfo.Clear();
					try
					{
						string empty = string.Empty;
						empty = i switch
						{
							0 => ReadUTF8StringFromDataObject(dataObject, "ASI Format"), 
							1 => ReadUTF16StringFromDataObject(dataObject, "ASI UNICODE Format"), 
							2 => ReadUTF8StringFromDataObject(dataObject, "ASI File Format"), 
							3 => ReadUTF16StringFromDataObject(dataObject, "ASI File UNICODE Format"), 
							_ => throw new CxsException("Unexpected option to format switch"), 
						};
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
						{
							cxsAsiClipboardFileInfo.LoadFromXml(empty);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxsAsiClipboardFileInfo._FileMoniker))
							{
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					catch (Exception arg)
					{
						Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception raised while parsing clipboard data: {0}", arg));
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception arg2)
			{
				Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception raised while parsing clipboard data: {0}", arg2));
				cxsAsiClipboardFileInfo.Clear();
			}
			return cxsAsiClipboardFileInfo.ConvertToCaseInfo();
		}

		public static bool CxsClipboardDataExists()
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.IDataObject dataObject = Clipboard.GetDataObject();
				if (dataObject == null)
				{
					return result;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
				{
					try
					{
						switch (i)
						{
						case 0:
						{
							DataFormats.Format format = DataFormats.GetFormat("ASI Format");
							if (dataObject.GetDataPresent(format.Name))
							{
								return result = true;
							}
							break;
						}
						case 1:
						{
							DataFormats.Format format = DataFormats.GetFormat("ASI UNICODE Format");
							if (dataObject.GetDataPresent(format.Name))
							{
								return result = true;
							}
							break;
						}
						case 2:
						{
							DataFormats.Format format = DataFormats.GetFormat("ASI File Format");
							if (dataObject.GetDataPresent(format.Name))
							{
								return result = true;
							}
							break;
						}
						case 3:
						{
							DataFormats.Format format = DataFormats.GetFormat("ASI File UNICODE Format");
							if (dataObject.GetDataPresent(format.Name))
							{
								return result = true;
							}
							break;
						}
						default:
							throw new CxsException("Unexpected option to format switch");
						}
					}
					catch (Exception arg)
					{
						Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception raised while parsing clipboard data: {0}", arg));
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception arg2)
			{
				Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception raised while parsing clipboard data: {0}", arg2));
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	public struct CxsBatchRequest
	{
		private string _Path;

		private string _Units;

		private string _Attribute;

		private object _Value;

		[NonSerialized]
		private object _Tag;

		public string Path
		{
			get
			{
				return _Path;
			}
			set
			{
				_Path = value;
			}
		}

		public string Units
		{
			get
			{
				return _Units;
			}
			set
			{
				_Units = value;
			}
		}

		public string Attribute
		{
			get
			{
				return _Attribute;
			}
			set
			{
				_Attribute = value;
			}
		}

		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return _Tag;
			}
			set
			{
				_Tag = value;
			}
		}

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return _Value;
			}
			set
			{
				_Value = value;
			}
		}

		public string ValueAsString => CxsConverter.ValueToString(_Value);

		public bool? ValueAsBoolean => CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(_Value);

		public double? ValueAsDouble => CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(_Value);

		public int? ValueAsInteger => CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(_Value);

		public CxsBatchRequest(string path, string attribute)
		{
			_Path = path;
			_Units = null;
			_Attribute = attribute;
			_Tag = null;
			_Value = null;
		}

		public CxsBatchRequest(string path, string attribute, string units)
		{
			_Path = path;
			_Units = units;
			_Attribute = attribute;
			_Tag = null;
			_Value = null;
		}

		public CxsBatchRequest(string path, string attribute, string units, object value)
		{
			_Path = path;
			_Units = units;
			_Attribute = attribute;
			_Value = value;
			_Tag = null;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null || obj.GetType() != typeof(CxsBatchRequest))
			{
				return false;
			}
			CxsBatchRequest cxsBatchRequest = (CxsBatchRequest)obj;
			if (_Path == cxsBatchRequest._Path && _Units == cxsBatchRequest._Units && _Attribute == cxsBatchRequest._Attribute && _Value == cxsBatchRequest._Value)
			{
				return _Tag == cxsBatchRequest._Tag;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool operator ==(CxsBatchRequest rhs, CxsBatchRequest lhs)
		{
			return rhs.Equals(lhs);
		}

		public static bool operator !=(CxsBatchRequest rhs, CxsBatchRequest lhs)
		{
			return !rhs.Equals(lhs);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (_Path != null)
			{
				num ^= _Path.GetHashCode();
			}
			if (_Units != null)
			{
				num ^= _Units.GetHashCode();
			}
			if (_Attribute != null)
			{
				num ^= _Attribute.GetHashCode();
			}
			if (_Value != null)
			{
				num ^= _Value.GetHashCode();
			}
			if (_Tag != null)
			{
				num ^= _Tag.GetHashCode();
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsBatchRequestData
	{

		private List<CxsBatchRequest> _Requests;

		private List<string> _SortedPaths;

		private List<string> _SortedAttributes;

		[NonSerialized]
		private ReadOnlyCollection<CxsBatchRequest> _RequestsRO;

		[NonSerialized]
		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _SortedAttributesRO;

		[NonSerialized]
		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _SortedPathsRO;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> CanonicalPaths
		{
			get
			{
				if (_SortedPathsRO == null)
				{
					_SortedPathsRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_SortedPaths);
				}
				return _SortedPathsRO;
			}
		}

		internal List<string> CanonicalPathsList => _SortedPaths;

		public int CanonicalPathsCount => _SortedPaths.Count;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> CanonicalAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				if (_SortedAttributesRO == null)
				{
					_SortedAttributesRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_SortedAttributes);
				}
				return _SortedAttributesRO;
			}
		}

		internal List<string> CanonicalAttributesList => _SortedAttributes;

		public int CanonicalAttributesCount => _SortedAttributes.Count;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<CxsBatchRequest> Requests
		{
			get
			{
				if (_RequestsRO == null)
				{
					_RequestsRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<CxsBatchRequest>(_Requests);
				}
				return _RequestsRO;
			}
		}

		public int RequestsCount => _Requests.Count;

		public string CommonBasePath => _CommonBasePath;

		public CxsBatchRequestData()

			_CommonBasePath = string.Empty;
			_Requests = new List<CxsBatchRequest>();
			_SortedPaths = new List<string>();
			_SortedAttributes = new List<string>();

		public CxsBatchRequestData(CxsBatchRequest[] requests)

			_Requests = new List<CxsBatchRequest>();
			_SortedPaths = new List<string>();
			_SortedAttributes = new List<string>();
			AddRequests(requests);
		}

		{
			_CommonBasePath = string.Empty;
			_Requests = new List<CxsBatchRequest>();
			_SortedPaths = new List<string>();
			_SortedAttributes = new List<string>();
			AddRequests(requests);
		}

		public CxsBatchRequestData Clone()
		{
			return new CxsBatchRequestData
			{
				_CommonBasePath = _CommonBasePath,
				_Requests = new List<CxsBatchRequest>(_Requests),
				_SortedPaths = new List<string>(_SortedPaths),
				_SortedAttributes = new List<string>(_SortedAttributes)
			};
		}

		{
			_CommonBasePath = string.Empty;
			_Requests.Clear();
			_SortedPaths.Clear();
			_SortedAttributes.Clear();
		}

		public void AddRequest(CxsBatchRequest request)
		{
			InsertRequest(_Requests.Count, request);
		}

		public void AddRequest(string path, string attribute)
		{
			AddTaggedRequest(path, attribute, null, null, null);
		}

		public void AddRequest(string path, string attribute, string units)
		{
			AddTaggedRequest(path, attribute, units, null, null);

			AddTaggedRequest(path, attribute, units, value, null);

			AddTaggedRequest(path, attribute, null, tag);

		{
			AddTaggedRequest(path, attribute, units, null, tag);
		}

		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(_Requests.Count, path, attribute, units, value, tag);

			AddTaggedRequest(canonicalPathIndex, attribute, null, null, null);

			AddTaggedRequest(canonicalPathIndex, attribute, units, null, tag);

			AddTaggedRequest(canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, units, value, null);

			AddTaggedRequest(canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, null, null, tag);

			InsertTaggedRequest(_Requests.Count, canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, units, value, tag);
		}

		public void AddRequests(CxsBatchRequest[] requests)
		{
			if (requests != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < requests.Length; i++)
				{
					AddRequest(requests[i]);
				}
			}
		}

		public void AddRequests(IEnumerable<CxsBatchRequest> requests)
		{
			if (requests == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests)
			{
				AddRequest(request);
			}
		}

		public void AddRequests(string[] paths, string[] units, string[] attributes)
		{
			if (paths == null || attributes == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < paths.Length; i++)
			{
				string path = AddRequestPath(paths[i]);
				for (int j = 0; j < attributes.Length; j++)
				{
					string attribute = AddRequestAttribute(attributes[j]);
					_Requests.Add(new CxsBatchRequest(path, attribute, units[i]));
				}
			}
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, CxsBatchRequest request)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > _Requests.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(request.Path))
			{
				request.Path = "Top";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(request.Attribute))
			{
				request.Attribute = "value";
			}
			request.Path = AddRequestPath(request.Path);
			request.Attribute = AddRequestAttribute(request.Attribute);
			_Requests.Add(request);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, string path, string attribute)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, path, attribute, null, null, null);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, string path, string attribute, string units)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, path, attribute, units, null, null);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, string path, string attribute, string units, object value)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, path, attribute, units, value, null);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, string path, string attribute, object tag)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, path, attribute, null, null, tag);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, string path, string attribute, string units, object tag)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, path, attribute, units, null, tag);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, string path, string attribute, string units, object value, object tag)
		{
			CxsBatchRequest request = new CxsBatchRequest(path, attribute, units, value);
			request.Tag = tag;
			InsertRequest(index, request);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, string attribute)
		{
			InsertRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, attribute, null, null);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, string attribute, string units)
		{
			InsertRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, attribute, units, null);
		}

		private void InsertRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, string attribute, string units, object value)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, attribute, units, value, null);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, string attribute, object tag)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, attribute, null, null, tag);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, string attribute, string units, object tag)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, attribute, units, null, tag);
		}

		private void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, string attribute, string units, object value, object tag)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > _Requests.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			if (canonicalPathIndex < 0 || canonicalPathIndex >= _SortedPaths.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("canonicalPathIndex");
			}
			string path = _SortedPaths[canonicalPathIndex];
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(attribute))
			{
				attribute = "value";
			}
			attribute = AddRequestAttribute(attribute);
			CxsBatchRequest item = new CxsBatchRequest(path, attribute, units, value);
			item.Tag = tag;
			_Requests.Add(item);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, int canonicalAttributeIndex)
		{
			InsertRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, null, null);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, int canonicalAttributeIndex, string units)
		{
			InsertRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, units, null);
		}

		public void InsertRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, int canonicalAttributeIndex, string units, object value)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, units, value, null);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, int canonicalAttributeIndex, object tag)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, null, null, tag);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, int canonicalAttributeIndex, string units, object tag)
		{
			InsertTaggedRequest(index, canonicalPathIndex, canonicalAttributeIndex, units, null, tag);
		}

		public void InsertTaggedRequest(int index, int canonicalPathIndex, int canonicalAttributeIndex, string units, object value, object tag)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > _Requests.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			if (canonicalPathIndex < 0 || canonicalPathIndex >= _SortedPaths.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("canonicalPathIndex");
			}
			if (canonicalAttributeIndex < 0 || canonicalAttributeIndex >= _SortedAttributes.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("canonicalAttributeIndex");
			}
			string path = _SortedPaths[canonicalPathIndex];
			string attribute = _SortedAttributes[canonicalAttributeIndex];
			CxsBatchRequest item = new CxsBatchRequest(path, attribute, units, value);
			item.Tag = tag;
			_Requests.Add(item);
		}

		public void RemoveRequestAt(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= _Requests.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
			}
			string text = _Requests[index].Path;
			string text2 = _Requests[index].Attribute;
			_Requests.RemoveAt(index);
			for (int i = 0; i < _Requests.Count; i++)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && (object)Requests[i].Path == text)
				{
					text = string.Empty;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && (object)_Requests[i].Attribute == text2)
				{
					text2 = string.Empty;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				_SortedPaths.RemoveAt(GetCanonicalPathIndex(text));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				_SortedAttributes.RemoveAt(GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(text2));
			}
		}

		public int GetCanonicalPathIndex(string path)
		{
			return _SortedPaths.BinarySearch(path);
		}

		public string LookupCanonicalPath(string path)
		{
			int num = _SortedPaths.BinarySearch(path);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _SortedPaths[num];
		}

		public int GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(string attribute)
		{
			return _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute);
		}

		public string LookupCanonicalAttribute(string path)
		{
			int num = _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(path);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _SortedAttributes[num];
		}

		public object[] GetRequestValues()
		{
			object[] array = new object[_Requests.Count];
			for (int i = 0; i < _Requests.Count; i++)
			{
				array[i] = _Requests[i].Value;
			}
			return array;
		}

		public void UpdateRequestValues(object[] values)
		{
			if (values != null)
			{
				int num = values.Length;
				if (num > _Requests.Count)
				{
					num = _Requests.Count;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					CxsBatchRequest value = _Requests[i];
					value.Value = values[i];
					_Requests[i] = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public object GetValue(string path, string attribute)
		{
			int canonicalPathIndex = GetCanonicalPathIndex(path);
			if (canonicalPathIndex < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return GetValue(canonicalPathIndex, attribute);
		}

		public object GetValue(int pathIndex, string attribute)
		{
			if (pathIndex < 0 || pathIndex >= _SortedPaths.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("pathIndex");
			}
			int canonicalAttributeIndex = GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(attribute);
			if (canonicalAttributeIndex < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			attribute = _SortedAttributes[canonicalAttributeIndex];
			string text = _SortedPaths[pathIndex];
			object result = null;
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in _Requests)
			{
				if ((object)request.Path == text && (object)request.Attribute == attribute)
				{
					result = request.Value;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string AddRequestPath(string path)
		{
			int num = _SortedPaths.BinarySearch(path);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				return _SortedPaths[num];
			}
			_SortedPaths.Insert(~num, path);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CommonBasePath))
			{
				_CommonBasePath = path;
			}
			else
			{
				string text = _CommonBasePath;
				string childPath = path;
				if (path.Length > _CommonBasePath.Length)
				{
					text = path;
					childPath = _CommonBasePath;
				}
				string text2 = CxsPath.RelativePathFromParent(text, childPath);
				while (text2 == null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					try
					{
						text = CxsPath.ParentPath(text);
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
						text = "Top";
					}
					text2 = CxsPath.RelativePathFromParent(text, childPath);
				}
				_CommonBasePath = text;
			}
			return path;
		}

		private string AddRequestAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			int num = _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				return _SortedAttributes[num];
			}
			_SortedAttributes.Insert(~num, attribute);
			return attribute;
		}

		internal void LoadFromWcfBatchGet(CxsWcfBatchGetData wcfData)
		{
			_SortedPaths = wcfData.CanonicalPaths;
			_SortedAttributes = wcfData.CanonicalAttributes;
			foreach (CxsWcfBatchGetRecord request in wcfData.Requests)
			{
				CxsBatchRequest item = new CxsBatchRequest
				{
					Attribute = wcfData.CanonicalAttributes[request.AttributeIndex],
					Path = wcfData.CanonicalPaths[request.PathIndex],
					Units = request.Units
				};
				_Requests.Add(item);
			}
		}

		internal void LoadFromWcfBatchSet(CxsWcfBatchSetData wcfData)
		{
			_SortedPaths = wcfData.CanonicalPaths;
			_SortedAttributes = wcfData.CanonicalAttributes;
			foreach (CxsWcfBatchSetRecord request in wcfData.Requests)
			{
				CxsBatchRequest item = new CxsBatchRequest
				{
					Attribute = wcfData.CanonicalAttributes[request.AttributeIndex],
					Path = wcfData.CanonicalPaths[request.PathIndex],
					Units = request.Units,
					Value = CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValue(request.Value)
				};
				_Requests.Add(item);
			}
		}
	}

	public class CxsClipboardGridCell
	{
		public int Row { get; set; }

		public int Column { get; set; }

		public string Moniker { get; set; }

		public string Attribute { get; set; }

		public string TextValue { get; set; }
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsCaseInfo
	{
		private string _Application;

		private string _Version;

		private string _FileMoniker;

		private string[] _ItemMonikers;

		private CxsClipboardGridCell[] _GridData;

		public string Application => _Application;

		public string Version => _Version;

		public string FileMoniker => _FileMoniker;

		public string[] ItemMonikers => _ItemMonikers;

		public CxsClipboardGridCell[] GridData => _GridData;

		public void Clear()
		{
			_Application = string.Empty;
			_Version = string.Empty;
			_FileMoniker = string.Empty;
			_ItemMonikers = new string[0];
			_GridData = null;
		}

		public void SetData(string application, string version, string fileMoniker, string[] itemMonikers, CxsClipboardGridCell[] gridData)
		{
			_Application = application;
			_Version = version;
			_FileMoniker = fileMoniker;
			_ItemMonikers = itemMonikers;
			_GridData = gridData;
		}
	}
	public static class CxsConstants
	{
		public static string CxsExternalVersion => "V15";

		public static string CxsInternalVersion => "41.0";

		public static short BeginCopyright => 2003;

		public static short EndCopyright => 2025;

		public static int CxsInternalMajorVersion => 41;

		public static int CxsInternalMinorVersion => 0;

	public static class CxsConverter
	{
		public static string ValueToString(object value)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (value != null && value != DBNull.Value)
			{
				result = ((value.GetType() == typeof(string)) ? ((string)value) : ((value.GetType() == typeof(double)) ? ((double)value).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : ((value.GetType() == typeof(float)) ? ((float)value).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : ((value.GetType() == typeof(int)) ? ((int)value).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : ((!(value.GetType() == typeof(short))) ? value.ToString() : ((short)value).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))))));
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool? ValueToBoolean(object value)
		{
			bool? result = null;
			if (value != null && value != DBNull.Value)
			{
				Type type = value.GetType();
				if (type == typeof(bool))
				{
					result = (bool)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(string))
				{
					string text = (string)value;
					if (string.Compare(text, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						result = true;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(text, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						result = false;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(text, "1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						result = true;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(text, "0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						result = false;
					}
					else
					{
						bool result2 = false;
						int result3 = 0;
						if (bool.TryParse(text, out result2))
						{
							result = result2;
						}
						else if (int.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result3))
						{
							result = result3 != 0;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (type == typeof(double))
					{
						return (double)value != 0.0;
					}
					int? num = ValueToInt32(value);
					result = num.HasValue && num.Value != 0;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double? ValueToDouble(object value)
		{
			double? result = null;
			if (value != null && value != DBNull.Value)
			{
				Type type = value.GetType();
				if (type == typeof(double))
				{
					result = (double)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(float))
				{
					result = (float)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(int))
				{
					result = (int)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(short))
				{
					result = (short)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(string))
				{
					double result2 = 0.0;
					if (double.TryParse((string)value, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2))
					{
						result = result2;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static int? ValueToInt32(object value)
		{
			int? result = null;
			if (value != null && value != DBNull.Value)
			{
				Type type = value.GetType();
				if (type == typeof(int))
				{
					result = (int)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(short))
				{
					result = (int)value;
				}
				if (type == typeof(double))
				{
					result = Convert.ToInt32((double)value);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(float))
				{
					result = (int)value;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(string))
				{
					int result2 = 0;
					if (int.TryParse((string)value, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2))
					{
						result = result2;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string[] ValueToStringArray(object value)
		{
			if (value == null || value == DBNull.Value)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (value.GetType() != typeof(string))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = (string)value;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (text[text.Length - 1] == '\n')
			{
				text = text.Remove(text.Length - 1);
			}
			return text.Split('\n');
		}

		public static Collection<string> ValueToStringCollection(object value)
		{
			string[] array = ValueToStringArray(value);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new Collection<string>(array);
		}

		public static string[] ValueToOptionArray(object value)
		{
			return ValueToStringArray(value);
		}

		public static ReadOnlyCollection<string> ValueToOptionsCollection(object value)
		{
			string[] array = ValueToOptionArray(value);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(array);
		}

		public static string[] ValueToUnitsArray(object value)
		{
			string[] array = ValueToStringArray(value);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					int num = list.BinarySearch(text);
					if (num < 0)
					{
						list.Insert(~num, text);
					}
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public static ReadOnlyCollection<string> ValueToUnitsCollection(object value)
		{
			string[] array = ValueToUnitsArray(value);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(array);
		}

		public static CxsAutoSolveValue ValueToAutoSolveValue(object value)
		{
			string text = ValueToString(value);
			CxsAutoSolveValue result = CxsAutoSolveValue.NotSupported;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (string.Compare(text, "On", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					result = CxsAutoSolveValue.On;
				}
				else if (string.Compare(text, "Off", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					result = CxsAutoSolveValue.Off;
				}
				else if (string.Compare(text, "NotSupported", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					result = CxsAutoSolveValue.NotSupported;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static CxsAutoSolveStatus ValueToAutoSolveStatus(object value)
		{
			string text = ValueToString(value);
			CxsAutoSolveStatus result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.Unknown;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (string.Compare(text, "Idle", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.Idle;
				}
				else if (string.Compare(text, "RunPending", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.RunPending;
				}
				else if (string.Compare(text, "Running", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.Running;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static CxsActionStatusDetails ValueToActionStatusDetails(object value)
		{
			string text = ValueToString(value);
			CxsActionStatus cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvoke;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke;
				empty = "Action not supported";
			}
			else
			{
				switch (text[0])
				{
				case '0':
					cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvoke;
					empty = text.Substring(1);
					break;
				case '1':
					cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvokeWithWarnings;
					empty = text.Substring(1);
					break;
				case '2':
					cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke;
					empty = text.Substring(1);
					break;
				default:
					cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke;
					empty = text;
					break;
				}
			}
			return new CxsActionStatusDetails(cxsActionStatus, empty);
		}

		public static CxsActionDescriptor ValueToActionDescriptor(string action, object value)
		{
			string text = ValueToString(value);
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string summary = string.Empty;
			string returns = string.Empty;
			Type returnType = null;
			CxsActionParameter[] array = null;
			if (string.Compare(text, 0, "<ASIActionDescription>", 0, 22, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				empty = action;
				XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
				xmlDocument.LoadXml(text);
				XmlNode xmlNode = xmlDocument.SelectSingleNode("ASIActionDescription");
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("summary");
				if (xmlNode2 != null)
				{
					summary = xmlNode2.InnerText;
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode3 = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("returns");
				if (xmlNode3 != null)
				{
					returns = xmlNode3.InnerText;
					returnType = InferActionParameterType(xmlNode3);
				}
				XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlNode.SelectNodes("param");
				if (xmlNodeList != null)
				{
					array = new CxsActionParameter[xmlNodeList.Count];
					int num = 0;
					foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
					{
						CxsActionParameter cxsActionParameter = new CxsActionParameter();
						cxsActionParameter.Summary = item.InnerText;
						cxsActionParameter.ParameterType = InferActionParameterType(xmlNode3);
						cxsActionParameter.Optional = false;
						XmlNode namedItem = item.Attributes.GetNamedItem("name");
						if (namedItem != null)
						{
							cxsActionParameter.Name = namedItem.Value;
						}
						XmlNode namedItem2 = item.Attributes.GetNamedItem("optional");
						if (namedItem2 != null && string.Compare(namedItem2.Value, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							cxsActionParameter.Optional = true;
						}
						array[num] = cxsActionParameter;
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (string.Compare(text, "<ASIActionDescription/>", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				empty = action;
			}
			else
			{
				empty = action;
				summary = text;
			}
			return new CxsActionDescriptor(empty, summary, returns, returnType, array);
		}

		private static Type InferActionParameterType(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Type result = null;
			XmlNode namedItem = node.Attributes.GetNamedItem("type");
			if (namedItem != null)
			{
				string value = namedItem.Value;
				if (string.Compare(value, "double", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(double);
				}
				else if (string.Compare(value, "string", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(string);
				}
				else if (string.Compare(value, "int", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(int);
				}
				else if (string.Compare(value, "integer", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(int);
				}
				else if (string.Compare(value, "int32", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(int);
				}
				else if (string.Compare(value, "bool", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(bool);
				}
				else if (string.Compare(value, "Boolean", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = typeof(bool);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class CxsHistoryData
	{
		private Collection<Collection<double?>> _HistoryData;

		public int TimeCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (_HistoryData == null || _HistoryData.Count <= 0)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _HistoryData[0].Count;
			}
		}

		public int VariableCount
		{
			get
			{
				if (_HistoryData == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _HistoryData.Count - 1;
			}
		}

		public double StartTime
		{
			get
			{
				if (_HistoryData == null)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return _HistoryData[0][0].Value;
			}
		}

		public double EndTime
		{
			get
			{
				if (_HistoryData == null)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return _HistoryData[0][TimeCount - 1].Value;
			}
		}

		internal Collection<Collection<double?>> RawHistory => _HistoryData;

		public CxsHistoryData(Collection<Collection<double?>> historyData)
		{
			_HistoryData = historyData;
		}

		public double TimeAt(int timeIndex)
		{
			if (_HistoryData == null)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			return _HistoryData[0][timeIndex].Value;
		}

		public double? ValueAt(int timeIndex, int variable)
		{
			if (variable < 0 || variable >= VariableCount)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("variable");
			}
			if (timeIndex < 0 || timeIndex >= TimeCount)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("timeIndex");
			}
			if (_HistoryData == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _HistoryData[variable + 1][timeIndex];
		}
	}
	[TypeDescriptionProvider(typeof(CxsNodeTypeDescriptionProvider))]
	public class CxsNode : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _PropertyChangedHandlers;

		private CxsCase _Case;

		private bool _IsBound;

		private string _Path;

		private string _UnitsSpec;

		private List<string> _SortedAttributes;

		private Dictionary<string, object> _CachedAttributeData;

		private WeakReference _ChildNodesReference;

		private CxsNodeValueType _ValueType;

		[Browsable(false)]
		public CxsCase Case => _Case;

		public bool IsBound => _IsBound;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("valid_attributes")]
		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> ValidAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureNodeTraits();
				return new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_SortedAttributes);
			}
		}

		[Category("General Properties")]
		[Description("The full CXS path for this node")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("path")]
		public string Path => _Path;

		[Category("General Properties")]
		[Description("The path of this node relative to its parent container")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("container")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("path")]
		public string PathFromContainer
		{
			get
			{
				string stringAttribute = GetStringAttribute("container");
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringAttribute))
				{
					return _Path;
				}
				string[] array = CxsPath.Split(_Path);
				for (int num = array.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					if (string.Compare(array[num], stringAttribute, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						return CxsPath.CombineIds(array, num, array.Length - num);
					}
				}
				return _Path;
			}
		}

		[Category("System Properties")]
		[Description("The leaf identifier for this node.")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("id")]
		public string Id => CxsPath.LeafId(_Path);

		[Category("General Properties")]
		[Description("The leaf identifier for this node.")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("id")]
		public string Label => Id;

		[Category("General Properties")]
		[Description("The name of this node")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("display_name")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("browser_name")]
		public string Name
		{
			get
			{
				string stringAttribute = GetStringAttribute("display_name");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringAttribute))
				{
					return stringAttribute;
				}
				stringAttribute = GetStringAttribute("browser_name");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringAttribute))
				{
					return stringAttribute;
				}
				return Id;
			}
		}

		[Category("System Properties")]
		[Description("The path to the parent")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public string ParentPath => CxsPath.ParentPath(_Path);

		[Category("General Properties")]
		[Description("The units of measure")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("uom")]
		[Editor(typeof(CxsUnitsUIEditor), typeof(UITypeEditor))]
		public string Units
		{
			get
			{
				string text = GetStringAttribute("uom");
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					text = _UnitsSpec;
				}
				return text;
			}
		}

		[Category("System Properties")]
		[Description("The units of measure specification")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("uom")]
		[Editor(typeof(CxsUnitsUIEditor), typeof(UITypeEditor))]
		public string UnitsSpec
		{
			get
			{
				return _UnitsSpec;
			}
			set
			{
				if (string.Compare(value, _UnitsSpec, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
				{
					SetAttribute("uom", value);
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("System Properties")]
		[Description("The list of valid units of measure")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("uom_options")]
		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> UnitsList => CxsConverter.ValueToUnitsCollection(GetAttribute("uom_options"));

		[Category("System Properties")]
		[Description("The list of valid options values")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("option_list")]
		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> OptionList => CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(GetAttribute("option_list"));

		[Category("General Properties")]
		[Description("The value of this node")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("value")]
		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return GetAttribute("value");
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Case != null)
				{
					_Case.BatchSet(new CxsBatchRequest[1]
					{
						new CxsBatchRequest(_Path, "value", _UnitsSpec, value)
					});
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("The value of this node as a string")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("value")]
		public string StringValue
		{
			get
			{
				return CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetAttribute("value"));
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Case != null)
				{
					Value = value;
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("The value of this node as a double")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("value")]
		public double? RealValue
		{
			get
			{
				double? result = null;
				try
				{
					result = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(GetAttribute("value"));
					return result;
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
				catch (InvalidCastException)
				{
				}
				catch (FormatException)
				{
				}
				return result;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Case != null)
				{
					if (value.HasValue)
					{
						Value = value.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						Value = null;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("The value of this node as a bool")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("value")]
		public bool? BooleanValue
		{
			get
			{
				bool? result = null;
				try
				{
					result = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetAttribute("value"));
					return result;
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
				catch (InvalidCastException)
				{
				}
				catch (FormatException)
				{
				}
				return result;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Case != null)
				{
					if (value.HasValue)
					{
						Value = value.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						Value = null;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("The type of value held by this node")]
		public CxsNodeValueType ValueType
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureNodeTraits();
				if (_ValueType == CxsNodeValueType.Unknown)
				{
					if (!IsValidAttribute("value"))
					{
						_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.None;
					}
					else if (IsValidAttribute("dimension") || IsValidAttribute("uom"))
					{
						_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.Real;
					}
					else if (IsValidAttribute("option_list"))
					{
						if (string.Equals(NodeType, "EnumBoolean"))
						{
							_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.Boolean;
						}
						else
						{
							_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.Enumeration;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						string nodeType = NodeType;
						if (string.Equals(nodeType, "Real"))
						{
							_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.Real;
						}
						else if (string.Equals(nodeType, "Integer"))
						{
							_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.Integer;
						}
						else if (string.Equals(nodeType, "String"))
						{
							_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.String;
						}
					}
				}
				return _ValueType;
			}
		}

		[Description("The display value of the node as a string")]
		[CxsAttributeDependency("value")]
		[Editor(typeof(CxsValueUIEditor), typeof(UITypeEditor))]
		public string DisplayValue

					double result = 0.0;
					if (double.TryParse(value, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, out result))
					{
						Value = result;
					}
					else
					{
						Value = value;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		}

		public void ResetNodeTraits(CxsNodeTraits nodeTraits)
		{
			if (nodeTraits == null)
			{
				InternalSetIsBound(value: false);
				return;
			}
			if (nodeTraits.ValidAttributes == null || nodeTraits.ValidAttributes.Count == 0)
			{
				InternalSetIsBound(value: false);
				return;
			}
			_SortedAttributes = nodeTraits.SortedAttributes;
			if (_SortedAttributes != null && _CachedAttributeData != null)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string key in _CachedAttributeData.Keys)
				{
					if (!IsValidAttribute(key))
					{
						list.Add(key);
					}
				}
				foreach (string item in list)
				{
					_CachedAttributeData.Remove(item);
				}
			}
			if (nodeTraits.ValueType != CxsNodeValueType.Unknown)
			{
				_ValueType = nodeTraits.ValueType;
			}
			InternalSetIsBound(value: true);
		}

		public bool IsValidAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			EnsureNodeTraits();
			return _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
		}

		public bool AttributeIsCached(string attribute)
		{
			if (_CachedAttributeData != null)
			{
				return !_CachedAttributeData.ContainsKey(attribute);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object GetAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			object value = null;
			if ((_CachedAttributeData == null || !_CachedAttributeData.TryGetValue(attribute, out value)) && IsValidAttribute(attribute))
			{
				if (_Case.AdapterNodeTraits.ShouldExcludeAttributeFromCache(attribute))
				{
					CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
					cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(_Path, attribute, _UnitsSpec);
					object[] array = _Case.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
					if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
					{
						value = array[0];
					}
				}
				else if (_Case.AutoRetrieveMissingNodeData)
				{
					RefreshData();
					if (_CachedAttributeData != null)
					{
						_CachedAttributeData.TryGetValue(attribute, out value);
					}
					if (string.Compare(attribute, "path", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0 && value != null && value is string && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ToString()))
					{
						value = RemoveDoublePeriodFromPath(value.ToString());
					}
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		public void RefreshChildNodes(CxsNodeRefreshOptions options)
		{
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection = null;
			if (_ChildNodesReference != null && _ChildNodesReference.IsAlive)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection = _ChildNodesReference.Target as CxsNodeCollection;
			}
			string[] array = null;
			if (_Case != null)
			{
				array = _Case.GetChildPaths(_Path);
			}
			CxsNodeCollection cxsNodeCollection2 = null;
			if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection2 = _Case.ResolveNodes(array, options);
			}
			if (cxsNodeCollection == null)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection = ((cxsNodeCollection2 != null) ? cxsNodeCollection2 : new CxsNodeCollection());
				_ChildNodesReference = new WeakReference(cxsNodeCollection);
				return;
			}
			if (cxsNodeCollection2 == null || cxsNodeCollection2.Count == 0)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection.Clear();
				return;
			}
			if (cxsNodeCollection.Count == 0)
			{
				foreach (CxsNode item in cxsNodeCollection2)
				{
					cxsNodeCollection.Add(item);
				}
				return;
			}
			for (int num = cxsNodeCollection.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				int num2 = cxsNodeCollection2.IndexOf(cxsNodeCollection[num]);
				if (num2 >= 0)
				{
					cxsNodeCollection2.RemoveAt(num2);
				}
				else
				{
					cxsNodeCollection.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			foreach (CxsNode item2 in cxsNodeCollection2)
			{
				cxsNodeCollection.Add(item2);
			}
		}
