		private PropertyDescriptor[] GetDependentProperties(string[] attributes)
		{
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor property in TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(this))
			{
				foreach (Attribute attribute in property.Attributes)
				{
					if (!(attribute is CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute))
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < attributes.Length; i++)
					{
						if (attributes[i] == cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName)
						{
							list.Add(property);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		internal void StoreResultsFromDataRefresh(CxsBatchRequestData getRequests, CxsNodesChangedEventArgs nodesChangedArgs)
		{
			if (getRequests == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_CachedAttributeData == null)
			{
				_CachedAttributeData = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			}
			int count = _CachedAttributeData.Keys.Count;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string text = getRequests.LookupCanonicalPath(_Path);
			bool flag = _PropertyChangedHandlers != null || nodesChangedArgs != null;
			bool flag2 = false;
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in getRequests.Requests)
			{
				if ((object)request.Path != text)
				{
					if (flag2)
					{
						break;
					}
					continue;
				}
				flag2 = true;
				string attribute = request.Attribute;
				object value = request.Value;
				object value2 = null;
				if (count > 0)
				{
					_CachedAttributeData.TryGetValue(attribute, out value2);
				}
				if (string.Compare(attribute, "path", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0 && value2 != null && value2 is string && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2.ToString()))
				{
					value2 = RemoveDoublePeriodFromPath(value2.ToString());
				}
				_CachedAttributeData[attribute] = value;
				if (!flag)
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag3 = true;
				if (value2 == null && value == null)
				{
					flag3 = false;
				}
				else if (value2 == null || value == null)
				{
					flag3 = true;
				}
				else if (value.GetType() != value2.GetType())
				{
					flag3 = true;
				}
				else if (value.GetType() == typeof(double))
				{
					double num = (double)value;
					double num2 = (double)value2;
					double num3 = Math.Abs(num - num2);
					double num4 = (Math.Abs(num) + Math.Abs(num2)) / 2.0;
					flag3 = ((!(num4 < 9.99988867E-315)) ? (num3 / num4 >= 1E-06) : (num3 >= 9.9999E-319));
				}
				else
				{
					flag3 = !value.Equals(value2);
				}
				if (flag3)
				{
					int num5 = list.BinarySearch(attribute);
					if (num5 < 0)
					{
						list.Insert(~num5, attribute);
					}
				}
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_PropertyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor property in TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(this))
				{
					foreach (Attribute attribute2 in property.Attributes)
					{
						if (attribute2 is CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute && list.BinarySearch(cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName) >= 0)
						{
							list2.Add(property.Name);
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (string item in list2)
				{
					RaisePropertyChanged(item);
				}
			}
			if (nodesChangedArgs == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsNodesChangedRecord cxsNodesChangedRecord = new CxsNodesChangedRecord(this);
			foreach (string item2 in list)
			{
				cxsNodesChangedRecord.AddAttribute(item2);
			}
			nodesChangedArgs.AddChange(cxsNodesChangedRecord);
		}

		internal void AddToRefreshRequest(CxsBatchRequestData getRequests, CxsNodeRefreshOptions options)
		{
			ICxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits adapterNodeTraits = _Case.AdapterNodeTraits;
			CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default;
			if (options != null)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = options.UpdateSetting;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default)
			{
				cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.ExcludeKnownConstants;
			}
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = -1;
			if (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateInclusionsOnly && options != null)
			{
				if (options.IncludedProperties.Count == 0)
				{
					foreach (string includedAttribute in options.IncludedAttributes)
					{
						if ((_SortedAttributes == null || _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(includedAttribute) >= 0) && !adapterNodeTraits.ShouldExcludeAttributeFromCache(includedAttribute))
						{
							if (num >= 0)
							{
								getRequests.AddRequest(num, includedAttribute);
							}
							else
							{
								getRequests.AddRequest(_Path, includedAttribute);
								num = getRequests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(_Path);
							}
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string includedAttribute2 in options.IncludedAttributes)
				{
					if (_SortedAttributes == null || _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(includedAttribute2) >= 0)
					{
						int num2 = list.BinarySearch(includedAttribute2);
						if (num2 < 0)
						{
							list.Insert(~num2, includedAttribute2);
						}
					}
				}
				PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(this);
				foreach (string includedProperty in options.IncludedProperties)
				{
					PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = properties.Find(includedProperty, ignoreCase: false);
					if (propertyDescriptor == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (Attribute attribute in propertyDescriptor.Attributes)
					{
						if (attribute is CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute)
						{
							int num3 = list.BinarySearch(cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName);
							if (num3 < 0)
							{
								list.Insert(~num3, cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				{
					foreach (string item in list)
					{
						if (!adapterNodeTraits.ShouldExcludeAttributeFromCache(item))
						{
							if (num >= 0)
							{
								getRequests.AddRequest(num, item);
								continue;
							}
							getRequests.AddRequest(_Path, item);
							num = getRequests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(_Path);
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			if (options != null && options.HasPropertyInclusionsOrExclusions)
			{
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor property in TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(this))
				{
					bool? flag = options.WillPropertyUpdate(property.Name);
					if (!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (Attribute attribute2 in property.Attributes)
					{
						if (attribute2 is CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute2 && !adapterNodeTraits.ShouldExcludeAttributeFromCache(cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute2.AttributeName))
						{
							int num4 = list2.BinarySearch(cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute2.AttributeName);
							if (num4 < 0)
							{
								list2.Insert(~num4, cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute2.AttributeName);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (string sortedAttribute in _SortedAttributes)
			{
				if (adapterNodeTraits.ShouldExcludeAttributeFromCache(sortedAttribute))
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool? flag2 = null;
				if (list2.BinarySearch(sortedAttribute) >= 0)
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				else if (options != null)
				{
					flag2 = options.WillAttributeUpdate(sortedAttribute);
				}
				if (!flag2.HasValue)
				{
					switch (cxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting)
					{
					case CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default:
					case CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.ExcludeKnownConstants:
						flag2 = ((_CachedAttributeData != null && IsAttributeConstant(sortedAttribute)) ? new bool?(!_CachedAttributeData.ContainsKey(sortedAttribute)) : new bool?(true));
						break;
					case CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateAll:
						flag2 = true;
						break;
					case CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateInclusionsOnly:
						flag2 = false;
						break;
					case CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone:
						flag2 = false;
						break;
					default:
						flag2 = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag2.HasValue && flag2.Value)
				{
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						getRequests.AddRequest(num, sortedAttribute, _UnitsSpec);
						continue;
					}
					getRequests.AddRequest(_Path, sortedAttribute, _UnitsSpec);
					num = getRequests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(_Path);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InternalSetUnitsSpec(string value, CxsNodesChangedEventArgs nodesChangedArgs)
		{
			if (!(value == _UnitsSpec))
			{
				_UnitsSpec = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("UnitsSpec");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Units");
				if (nodesChangedArgs != null)
				{
					CxsNodesChangedRecord cxsNodesChangedRecord = new CxsNodesChangedRecord(this);
					cxsNodesChangedRecord.AddAttribute("uom");
					nodesChangedArgs.AddChange(cxsNodesChangedRecord);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedHandlers = _PropertyChangedHandlers;
			if (propertyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				try
				{
					propertyChangedHandlers(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		private void EnsureNodeTraits()
		{
			if (!IsBound && _Case != null && _Case.AutoRetrieveMissingNodeData)
			{
				CxsNodeRefreshOptions cxsNodeRefreshOptions = new CxsNodeRefreshOptions();
				cxsNodeRefreshOptions.UpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone;
				cxsNodeRefreshOptions.ValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateAllNodes;
				RefreshData(cxsNodeRefreshOptions);
			}
		}

		private void InternalSetIsBound(bool value)
		{
			if (value != _IsBound)
			{
				_IsBound = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("IsBound");
			}
		}

		private static string RemoveDoublePeriodFromPath(string origPath)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(origPath))
			{
				return origPath;
			}
			string result = origPath;
			if (origPath.Contains(".."))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				for (int i = 0; i < origPath.Length; i++)
				{
					if (i == 0 || origPath.Substring(i - 1, 2) != "..")
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(origPath.Substring(i, 1));
					}
				}
				result = stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class CxsNodesChangedRecord
	{
		private CxsNode _Node;

		private List<string> _ChangedAttributes;

		public CxsNode Node => _Node;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> ChangedAttributes => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_ChangedAttributes);

		public CxsNodesChangedRecord(CxsNode node)
		{
			_Node = node;
			_ChangedAttributes = new List<string>();
		}

		public void AddAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			int num = _ChangedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				_ChangedAttributes.Insert(~num, attribute);
			}
		}

		public bool IsAttributeChanged(string attribute)
		{
			return _ChangedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
		}
	}
	public class CxsNodesChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private List<CxsNodesChangedRecord> _ChangeRecords;

		public int RecordCount => _ChangeRecords.Count;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<CxsNodesChangedRecord> ChangeRecords => new ReadOnlyCollection<CxsNodesChangedRecord>(_ChangeRecords);

		public CxsNodesChangedEventArgs()
		{
			_ChangeRecords = new List<CxsNodesChangedRecord>();
		}

		public void AddChange(CxsNodesChangedRecord record)
		{
			_ChangeRecords.Add(record);
		}

		public bool ContainsNode(CxsNode node)
		{
			foreach (CxsNodesChangedRecord changeRecord in _ChangeRecords)
			{
				if (changeRecord.Node == node)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool ContainsPath(string path)
		{
			foreach (CxsNodesChangedRecord changeRecord in _ChangeRecords)
			{
				if (string.Compare(changeRecord.Node.Path, path, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class CxsNodeCollection : BindingList<CxsNode>, ITypedList
	{
		private PropertyDescriptorCollection _Properties;

		public CxsNodeCollection()
		{
			base.AllowNew = false;
		}

		public bool ContainsNode(string nodePath)
		{
			bool result = false;
			using (IEnumerator<CxsNode> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					if (enumerator.Current.Path == nodePath)
					{
						result = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void RefreshNodeData(CxsNodeRefreshOptions options)
		{
			if (base.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase cxsCase = null;
			List<CxsCase> list = new List<CxsCase>();
			using (IEnumerator<CxsNode> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					CxsNode current = enumerator.Current;
					if (current == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (cxsCase == null)
					{
						cxsCase = current.Case;
						cxsCase.RefreshNodes(this, options);
					}
					else
					{
						if (current.Case == cxsCase)
						{
							continue;
						}
						bool flag = false;
						foreach (CxsCase item in list)
						{
							if (item == current.Case)
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							list.Add(current.Case);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (CxsCase item2 in list)
			{
				item2.RefreshNodes(this, options);
			}
		}

		protected override void InsertItem(int index, CxsNode item)
		{
			if (item != null && _Properties != null)
			{
				PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(item);
				MergeProperties(properties, raiseChangeEvents: true);
			}
			base.InsertItem(index, item);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetItemProperties(PropertyDescriptor[] listAccessors)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection result = null;
			if (_Properties == null)
			{
				_Properties = new PropertyDescriptorCollection(null, readOnly: false);
				if (base.Count == 0)
				{
					MergeProperties(TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(typeof(CxsNode)), raiseChangeEvents: false);
				}
				else
				{
					using IEnumerator<CxsNode> enumerator = GetEnumerator();
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						CxsNode current = enumerator.Current;
						if (current != null)
						{
							PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(current);
							MergeProperties(properties, raiseChangeEvents: false);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (listAccessors == null || listAccessors.Length == 0)
			{
				result = _Properties;
			}
			else
			{
				Type propertyType = listAccessors[listAccessors.Length - 1].PropertyType;
				if (propertyType != null)
				{
					Type elementType = propertyType.GetElementType();
					if (elementType != null)
					{
						result = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(elementType);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string GetListName(PropertyDescriptor[] listAccessors)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (listAccessors != null)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor in listAccessors)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(propertyDescriptor.PropertyType.Name);
					stringBuilder.Append("_");
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString().TrimEnd('_');
			}
			return GetType().Name;
		}

		private void MergeProperties(PropertyDescriptorCollection properties, bool raiseChangeEvents)
		{
			if (properties == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_Properties.Count == 0)
			{
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor property in properties)
				{
					_Properties.Add(property);
				}
				if (!raiseChangeEvents)
				{
					return;
				}
				{
					foreach (PropertyDescriptor property2 in _Properties)
					{
						OnListChanged(new ListChangedEventArgs(ListChangedType.PropertyDescriptorAdded, property2));
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor property3 in properties)
			{
				if (_Properties.Find(property3.Name, ignoreCase: true) == null)
				{
					_Properties.Add(property3);
					if (raiseChangeEvents)
					{
						list.Add(property3);
					}
				}
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in list)
			{
				OnListChanged(new ListChangedEventArgs(ListChangedType.PropertyDescriptorAdded, item));
			}
		}
	}
	public enum CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting
	{
		Default,
		ValidateOnlyUnboundNodes,
		ValidateAllNodes,
		ValidateNone
	}
	public enum CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting
	{
		Default,
		ExcludeKnownConstants,
		UpdateAll,
		UpdateInclusionsOnly,
		UpdateNone
	}
	public enum CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction
	{
		Default,
		RemoveUnboundNodes,
		ReplaceWithNull,
		Leave
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsNodeRefreshOptions
	{
		private CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting _ValidationSetting;

		private CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting _UpdateSetting;

		private CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction _UnboundNodeAction;

		private List<string> _IncludedAttributes;

		private List<string> _ExcludedAttributes;

		private List<string> _IncludedProperties;

		private List<string> _ExcludedProperties;

		public CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting UpdateSetting
		{
			get
			{
				return _UpdateSetting;
			}
			set
			{
				_UpdateSetting = value;
			}
		}

		public CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction UnboundNodeAction
		{
			get
			{
				return _UnboundNodeAction;
			}
			set
			{
				_UnboundNodeAction = value;
			}
		}

		public CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting ValidationSetting
		{
			get
			{
				return _ValidationSetting;
			}
			set
			{
				_ValidationSetting = value;
			}
		}

		public bool HasPropertyInclusionsOrExclusions
		{
			get
			{
				if (_IncludedProperties == null || _IncludedProperties.Count <= 0)
				{
					if (_ExcludedProperties != null)
					{
						return _ExcludedProperties.Count > 0;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> IncludedAttributes => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>((_IncludedAttributes != null) ? _IncludedAttributes : new List<string>());

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> ExcludedAttributes => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>((_ExcludedAttributes != null) ? _ExcludedAttributes : new List<string>());

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> IncludedProperties => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>((_IncludedProperties != null) ? _IncludedProperties : new List<string>());

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> ExcludedProperties => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>((_ExcludedProperties != null) ? _ExcludedProperties : new List<string>());

		public CxsNodeRefreshOptions()
		{
			_UpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default;
			_ValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default;
			_UnboundNodeAction = CxsNodeRefreshUnboundNodeAction.Default;
		}

		public CxsNodeRefreshOptions(string[] attributesToInclude, string[] attributesToExclude)
		{
			_UpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.Default;
			_ValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.Default;
			if (attributesToInclude != null)
			{
				string[] array = attributesToInclude;
				foreach (string attribute in array)
				{
					IncludeAttribute(attribute);
				}
			}
			if (attributesToExclude != null)
			{
				string[] array = attributesToExclude;
				foreach (string attribute2 in array)
				{
					ExcludeAttribute(attribute2);
				}
			}
		}

		public void IncludeAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(attribute))
			{
				if (_IncludedAttributes == null)
				{
					_IncludedAttributes = new List<string>();
				}
				int num = _IncludedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_IncludedAttributes.Insert(~num, attribute);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ExcludeAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(attribute))
			{
				if (_ExcludedAttributes == null)
				{
					_ExcludedAttributes = new List<string>();
				}
				int num = _ExcludedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_ExcludedAttributes.Insert(~num, attribute);
				}
			}
		}

		public void IncludeProperty(string propName)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propName))
			{
				if (_IncludedProperties == null)
				{
					_IncludedProperties = new List<string>();
				}
				int num = _IncludedProperties.BinarySearch(propName);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_IncludedProperties.Insert(~num, propName);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ExcludeProperty(string propName)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propName))
			{
				if (_ExcludedProperties == null)
				{
					_ExcludedProperties = new List<string>();
				}
				int num = _ExcludedProperties.BinarySearch(propName);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_ExcludedProperties.Insert(~num, propName);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool? WillAttributeUpdate(string attribute)
		{
			bool? result = null;
			if (_UpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			if (_IncludedAttributes != null && _IncludedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			else if (_UpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateInclusionsOnly)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else if (_ExcludedAttributes != null && _ExcludedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) < 0)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else if (_UpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateAll)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool? WillPropertyUpdate(string propertyName)
		{
			bool? result = null;
			if (_UpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateNone)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			if (_IncludedProperties != null && _IncludedProperties.BinarySearch(propertyName) >= 0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			else if (_UpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateInclusionsOnly)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else if (_ExcludedProperties != null && _ExcludedProperties.BinarySearch(propertyName) < 0)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else if (_UpdateSetting == CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateAll)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public enum CxsNodeValueType
	{
		Unknown,
		Real,
		String,
		Integer,
		Enumeration,
		Boolean,
		None
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsNodeTraits
	{
		private List<string> _SortedAttributes;

		private CxsNodeValueType _ValueType;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> ValidAttributes => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_SortedAttributes);

		public CxsNodeValueType ValueType
		{
			get
			{
				return _ValueType;
			}
			set
			{
				_ValueType = value;
			}
		}

		internal List<string> SortedAttributes => _SortedAttributes;

		public CxsNodeTraits(string[] validAttributes)
		{
			_ValueType = CxsNodeValueType.Unknown;
			_SortedAttributes = new List<string>();
			foreach (string item in validAttributes)
			{
				int num = _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(item);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_SortedAttributes.Insert(~num, item);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsValidAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			return _SortedAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
		}
	}
	internal class CxsNodeTypeDescriptor : CustomTypeDescriptor
	{
		private static List<string> _AttributesPremappedToProperties;

		private CxsNode _Node;

		internal CxsNodeTypeDescriptor(ICustomTypeDescriptor baseTypeDescriptor, CxsNode node)
			: base(baseTypeDescriptor)
		{
			_Node = node;
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties()
		{
			if (_Node == null)
			{
				return base.GetProperties();
			}
			return GetProperties(null);
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = base.GetProperties(attributes);
			ICxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits cxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits = null;
			if (_Node != null && _Node.Case != null)
			{
				cxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits = _Node.Case.AdapterNodeTraits;
			}
			if (cxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits == null)
			{
				return properties;
			}
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list = null;
			foreach (string validAttribute in _Node.ValidAttributes)
			{
				if (IsAttributeMappedToProperty(properties, validAttribute))
				{
					continue;
				}
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = cxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits.CreateAttributePropertyDescriptor(_Node, validAttribute);
				if (propertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (list == null)
				{
					list = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();
					foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in properties)
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				if (properties.Find(propertyDescriptor.Name, ignoreCase: false) == null)
				{
					list.Add(propertyDescriptor);
				}
			}
			if (list == null)
			{
				return properties;
			}
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection(list.ToArray(), readOnly: true);
		}

		private static bool IsAttributeMappedToProperty(PropertyDescriptorCollection props, string attribute)
		{
			if (props == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_AttributesPremappedToProperties == null)
			{
				_AttributesPremappedToProperties = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor prop in props)
				{
					foreach (Attribute attribute2 in prop.Attributes)
					{
						if (attribute2 is CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName))
						{
							int num = _AttributesPremappedToProperties.BinarySearch(cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName);
							if (num < 0)
							{
								_AttributesPremappedToProperties.Insert(~num, cxsAttributeDependencyAttribute.AttributeName);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_AttributesPremappedToProperties != null)
			{
				return _AttributesPremappedToProperties.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class CxsNodeTypeDescriptionProvider : TypeDescriptionProvider
	{
		private TypeDescriptionProvider _BaseProvider;

		public CxsNodeTypeDescriptionProvider()
		{
			_BaseProvider = TypeDescriptor.GetProvider(typeof(CxsNode));
		}

		public override ICustomTypeDescriptor GetTypeDescriptor(Type objectType, object instance)
		{
			ICustomTypeDescriptor baseTypeDescriptor = null;
			if (_BaseProvider != null)
			{
				baseTypeDescriptor = _BaseProvider.GetTypeDescriptor(objectType, instance);
			}
			CxsNode node = instance as CxsNode;
			return new CxsNodeTypeDescriptor(baseTypeDescriptor, node);
		}
	}
	public static class CxsPath
	{
		public const string TopPath = "Top";

		public const char PathSeparator = '.';

		public const char PathQuote = '"';

		public const string ReservedCharacters = "%#'&!|()[].=\"";

		public static bool IdsAreEqual(string id1, string id2)
		{
			return string.Compare(id1, id2, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0;
		}

		public static bool IdContainsReservedCharacters(string id)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(id))
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			foreach (char value in id)
			{
				if ("%#'&!|()[].=\"".IndexOf(value) >= 0)
				{
					result = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string CombinePaths(string path1, string path2)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path1))
			{
				return path2;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path2))
			{
				return path1;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.EnsureCapacity(path1.Length + path2.Length + 1);
			stringBuilder.Append(path1);
			stringBuilder.Append('.');
			stringBuilder.Append(path2);
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static void AppendQuotedId(StringBuilder builder, string id)
		{
			builder.Append('"');
			foreach (char c in id)
			{
				if (c != '"')
				{
					builder.Append(c);
				}
				else
				{
					builder.Append(c, 2);
				}
			}
			builder.Append('"');
		}

		public static string CombinePathAndId(string path, string id)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(id))
			{
				return path;
			}
			if (!IdContainsReservedCharacters(id))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
				{
					return id;
				}
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.EnsureCapacity(path.Length + 1 + id.Length);
				stringBuilder.Append(path);
				stringBuilder.Append('.');
				stringBuilder.Append(id);
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				stringBuilder2.EnsureCapacity(id.Length + 4);
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder2.EnsureCapacity(path.Length + 1 + id.Length + 4);
				stringBuilder2.Append(path);
				stringBuilder2.Append('.');
			}
			AppendQuotedId(stringBuilder2, id);
			return stringBuilder2.ToString();
		}

		public static string CombineIds(string[] ids)
		{
			return CombineIds(ids, 0, ids.Length);
		}

		public static string CombineIds(string[] ids, int startPos, int count)
		{
			if (ids == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (startPos > ids.Length - 1)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < count && i + startPos < ids.Length; i++)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ids[i]))
				{
					num += ids[i].Length + 1;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.EnsureCapacity(num);
			for (int j = 0; j < count && j + startPos < ids.Length; j++)
			{
				string text = ids[startPos + j];
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append('.');
					}
					if (!IdContainsReservedCharacters(text))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(text);
					}
					else
					{
						AppendQuotedId(stringBuilder, text);
					}
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string[] Split(string path)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.EnsureCapacity(32);
			int length = path.Length;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				char c = path[i];
				if (c == '"')
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						if (i + 1 < length && path[i + 1] == '"')
						{
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
							i++;
						}
						else
						{
							flag = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (flag || c != '.')
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
				else if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
				{
					list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
					stringBuilder.Length = 0;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Malformed path specified");
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public static string ParentPath(string path)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			int length = path.Length;
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				char c = path[i];
				if (c == '"')
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						if (i + 1 < length && path[i + 1] == '"')
						{
							i++;
						}
						else
						{
							flag = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (!flag && c == '.')
				{
					num = i;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Malformed path specified");
			}
			if (num <= 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return path.Substring(0, num);
		}

		public static string RelativePathFromParent(string parentPath, string childPath)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(parentPath))
			{
				return childPath;
			}
			string result = null;
			int length = parentPath.Length;
			if (string.Compare(parentPath, 0, childPath, 0, length, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				if (length == childPath.Length)
				{
					result = string.Empty;
				}
				else if (childPath[length] == '.')
				{
					result = childPath.Substring(length + 1, childPath.Length - length - 1);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string LeafId(string path)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			int length = path.Length;
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				char c = path[i];
				if (c == '"')
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						if (i + 1 < length && path[i + 1] == '"')
						{
							i++;
						}
						else
						{
							flag = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (!flag && c == '.')
				{
					num = i + 1;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Malformed path specified");
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.EnsureCapacity(length - num);
			for (int j = num; j < length; j++)
			{
				char c2 = path[j];
				if (c2 == '"')
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						if (j + 1 < length && path[j + 1] == '"')
						{
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
							j++;
						}
						else
						{
							flag = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c2);
				}
				else if (c2 != '.')
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c2);
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string StripTopSegment(string path)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return path;
			}
			if (string.Compare("Top", 0, path, 0, 3, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
			{
				return path;
			}
			if (path.Length == 3)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (path[3] != '.')
			{
				return path;
			}
			return path.Substring(4, path.Length - 4);
		}
	}
	public class CxsSearchEngine
	{
		private string _StartPath;

		private bool _PausePending;

		private bool _isPaused;

		private bool _isIndexing;

		private int _indexingChildNum;

		private int _indexingLevelNum;

		private CxsCase _Case;

		private CxsNodeCollection _MatchedNodes;

		private CxsSearchExpression _SearchExpression;

		private int _MaxDepth;

		private EventHandler _ProgressReportHandlers;

		private EventHandler _IsPausedChangeHandlers;

		private List<string> _RequiredProperties;

		private CxsNodeRefreshOptions _NodeRefreshOptions;

		private List<string> _NextSearchPaths;

		private List<string> _NextSearchPathNextLevel = new List<string>();

		private CxsNodeCollection _CurrentSearchNodes;

		private int _NextSearchIndex;

		private int _CurrentSearchDepth;

		private int _SearchProgressCount;

		private int _SearchedNodesCount;

		private int _ProgressReportFrequency;

		private int _ProgressReportLastSearchCount;

		private int _ProgressReportLastMatchCount;

		private CultureInfo _CultureInfo;

		private int _maxNodeChunkSize = 5000;

		private int _nextSearchPathToRetrieve;

		private System.Windows.Forms.Timer _timerForPausePolling = new System.Windows.Forms.Timer();

		public CxsSearchExpression SearchExpression
		{
			get
			{
				return _SearchExpression;
			}
			set
			{
				_SearchExpression = value;
			}
		}

		public CxsNodeCollection MatchedNodes => _MatchedNodes;

		public CxsCase Case
		{
			get
			{
				return _Case;
			}
			set
			{
				_Case = value;
			}
		}

		public string StartPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _StartPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_StartPath = value;
			}
		}

		public int MaximumSearchDepth
		{
			get
			{
				return _MaxDepth;
			}
			set
			{
				_MaxDepth = value;
			}
		}

		public int SearchDepth => _CurrentSearchDepth;

		public int SearchedNodesCount => _SearchedNodesCount;

		public int ProgressReportFrequency
		{
			get
			{
				return _ProgressReportFrequency;
			}
			set
			{
				_ProgressReportFrequency = value;
			}
		}

		public bool PausePending => _PausePending;

		public bool SearchComplete
		{
			get
			{
				int num = ((_MaxDepth == 0) ? 10000000 : _MaxDepth);
				if (_CurrentSearchNodes != null && _CurrentSearchDepth <= num)
				{
					if (_CurrentSearchNodes.Count == 0 && _NextSearchIndex >= _CurrentSearchNodes.Count && _NextSearchPathNextLevel.Count == 0)
					{
						return _NextSearchPaths.Count == 0;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool SearchStarted => _RequiredProperties != null;

		public bool IsPaused
		{
			get
			{
				return _isPaused;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (_isPaused != value)
				{
					_timerForPausePolling.Enabled = value;
					_isPaused = value;
					if (_IsPausedChangeHandlers != null)
					{
						_IsPausedChangeHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsIndexing
		{
			get
			{
				return _isIndexing;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isIndexing != value)
				{
					_isIndexing = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public int IndexChildNum
		{
			get
			{
				return _indexingChildNum;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_indexingChildNum != value)
				{
					_indexingChildNum = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public int IndexLevelNum
		{
			get
			{
				return _indexingLevelNum;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_indexingLevelNum != value)
				{
					_indexingLevelNum = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public CultureInfo CultureInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return _CultureInfo;
			}
			set
			{
				_CultureInfo = value;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler ProgressReport
		{
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			add
			{
				_ProgressReportHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(_ProgressReportHandlers, value);
			}
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			remove
			{
				_ProgressReportHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(_ProgressReportHandlers, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler IsPausedChanged
		{
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			add
			{
				_IsPausedChangeHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(_IsPausedChangeHandlers, value);
			}
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			remove
			{
				_IsPausedChangeHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(_IsPausedChangeHandlers, value);
			}
		}

		public CxsSearchEngine()
		{
			_MatchedNodes = new CxsNodeCollection();
			_NextSearchPaths = new List<string>();
			_ProgressReportFrequency = 10;
			_CultureInfo = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
		}

		public void BeginSearch()
		{
			if (_SearchExpression == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("No search expression specified");
			}
			if (_Case == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("No case specified");
			}
			_MatchedNodes.Clear();
			_RequiredProperties = new List<string>();
			_RequiredProperties.Add("HasChildren");
			_RequiredProperties.Add("IsLeaf");
			_RequiredProperties.Add("IsLink");
			_SearchExpression.CollectRequiredProperties(_RequiredProperties);
			_NodeRefreshOptions = new CxsNodeRefreshOptions();
			foreach (string requiredProperty in _RequiredProperties)
			{
				_NodeRefreshOptions.IncludeProperty(requiredProperty);
			}
			_NodeRefreshOptions.ValidationSetting = CxsNodeRefreshValidationSetting.ValidateOnlyUnboundNodes;
			_NodeRefreshOptions.UpdateSetting = CxsNodeRefreshUpdateSetting.UpdateInclusionsOnly;
			string[] array = null;
			array = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_StartPath) ? new string[1] { "Top" } : _Case.GetChildPaths(_StartPath));
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (!text.EndsWith("SharedData.Parent"))
				{
					list.Add(text);
				}
			}
			array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				_SearchedNodesCount = 0;
				_MatchedNodes = new CxsNodeCollection();
				if (_ProgressReportHandlers != null)
				{
					_ProgressReportHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
				}
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				_CurrentSearchNodes = _Case.ResolveNodes(array, _NodeRefreshOptions);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			_NextSearchPaths.Clear();
			_NextSearchPathNextLevel.Clear();
			_NextSearchIndex = 0;
			_SearchProgressCount = 0;
			_CurrentSearchDepth = 0;
			_SearchedNodesCount = 0;
			_ProgressReportLastMatchCount = 0;
			_ProgressReportLastSearchCount = 0;
			_PausePending = false;
			_nextSearchPathToRetrieve = 0;
			IsIndexing = false;
			IndexChildNum = 0;
			IndexLevelNum = 0;
			ResumeSearch();
		}

		public void ResumeSearch()
		{
			if (!SearchStarted)
			{
				BeginSearch();
				return;
			}
			_PausePending = false;
			IsPaused = false;
			while (!SearchComplete && !_PausePending)
			{
				MatchNextNode();
			}
			if (_PausePending)
			{
				_PausePending = false;
				IsPaused = true;
			}
			if (_ProgressReportHandlers != null)
			{
				_ProgressReportHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public void PauseSearch()
		{
			_PausePending = true;
			_timerForPausePolling.Enabled = true;
		}

		private void MatchNextNode()
		{
			if (SearchComplete)
			{
				IsIndexing = false;
				return;
			}
			if (_NextSearchIndex >= _CurrentSearchNodes.Count)
			{
				_NextSearchIndex = 0;
				_CurrentSearchNodes.Clear();
				if (_MaxDepth != 0 && _CurrentSearchDepth >= _MaxDepth && (_CurrentSearchDepth != _MaxDepth || _nextSearchPathToRetrieve <= 0))
				{
					_NextSearchPaths.Clear();
					_NextSearchPathNextLevel.Clear();
					return;
				}
				if (_nextSearchPathToRetrieve == 0)
				{
					_CurrentSearchDepth++;
					_NextSearchPaths = _NextSearchPathNextLevel;
					_NextSearchPathNextLevel = new List<string>();
				}
				IsIndexing = true;
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = 5;
				for (int i = _nextSearchPathToRetrieve; i < _NextSearchPaths.Count; i++)
				{
					string[] array = null;
					try
					{
						array = _Case.GetChildPaths(_NextSearchPaths[i]);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (array == null)
					{
						array = new string[0];
					}
					num2++;
					for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
					{
						num2++;
						if (num2 >= num3)
						{
							num2 = 0;
							if (_ProgressReportHandlers != null)
							{
								_ProgressReportHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
							}
						}
						if (array[j].EndsWith("SharedData.Parent") || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(array[j]))
						{
							continue;
						}
						CxsNode cxsNode = null;
						try
						{
							if (string.CompareOrdinal(_Case.AdapterTraits.ApplicationName, "Aspen Plus") != 0 || !array[j].Contains("$OLVER"))
							{
								cxsNode = _Case.ResolveNode(array[j]);
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						if (cxsNode != null)
						{
							_CurrentSearchNodes.Add(cxsNode);
						}
					}
					num += array.Length;
					IndexLevelNum = _CurrentSearchDepth;
					IndexChildNum = i;
					if (_ProgressReportHandlers != null)
					{
						_ProgressReportHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
					}
					if (i == _NextSearchPaths.Count - 1)
					{
						_nextSearchPathToRetrieve = 0;
						IsIndexing = false;
						continue;
					}
					if (num > _maxNodeChunkSize)
					{
						_nextSearchPathToRetrieve = i + 1;
						break;
					}
					if (PausePending)
					{
						_nextSearchPathToRetrieve = i + 1;
						return;
					}
				}
				if (_CurrentSearchNodes == null || _CurrentSearchNodes.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			IsIndexing = false;
			CxsNode cxsNode2 = _CurrentSearchNodes[_NextSearchIndex];
			_NextSearchIndex++;
			if (_SearchExpression.MatchNode(cxsNode2, _CultureInfo))
			{
				_MatchedNodes.Add(cxsNode2);
			}
			if (!cxsNode2.IsLink && cxsNode2.HasChildren)
			{
				bool? flag = _SearchExpression.CouldMatchChildren(cxsNode2, _CultureInfo);
				if (!flag.HasValue || flag.Value)
				{
					_NextSearchPathNextLevel.Add(cxsNode2.Path);
				}
			}
			_SearchedNodesCount++;
			_SearchProgressCount++;
			IsIndexing = false;
			IndexChildNum = 0;
			IndexLevelNum = 0;
			if (_SearchProgressCount > _ProgressReportFrequency)
			{
				_ = _SearchedNodesCount;
				_ = _ProgressReportLastSearchCount;
				_ProgressReportLastSearchCount = _SearchedNodesCount;
				_ = _MatchedNodes.Count;
				_ = _ProgressReportLastMatchCount;
				_ProgressReportLastMatchCount = _MatchedNodes.Count;
				_SearchProgressCount = 0;
				if (_ProgressReportHandlers != null)
				{
					_ProgressReportHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public enum CxsSearchExpressionOperator
	{
		And,
		Or,
		Equal,
		NotEqual,
		LessThan,
		LessThanOrEqual,
		GreaterThan,
		GreaterThanOrEqual,
		Contains,
		StartsWith,
		EndsWith,
		Like,
		IsOneOf,
		Between,
		IsNull
	}
	public class CxsSearchExpression : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _PropertyChangedHandlers;

		private CxsSearchExpressionOperator _Operator;

		private bool _Not;

		private string _TestProperty;

		private object _TestValue;

		private bool _IgnoreCase;

		private object _CachedData;

		private BindingList<CxsSearchExpression> _Subexpressions;

		public CxsSearchExpressionOperator Operator
		{
			get
			{
				return _Operator;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _Operator)
				{
					_Operator = value;
					CxsSearchExpressionOperator cxsSearchExpressionOperator = _Operator;
					if ((uint)cxsSearchExpressionOperator > 1u)
					{
						_Subexpressions.Clear();
					}
					_CachedData = null;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("Operator");
				}
			}
		}

		public string TestProperty
		{
			get
			{
				return _TestProperty;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _TestProperty)
				{
					_TestProperty = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("TestProperty");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Not
		{
			get
			{
				return _Not;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _Not)
				{
					_Not = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("Not");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IgnoreCase
		{
			get
			{
				return _IgnoreCase;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _IgnoreCase)
				{
					_IgnoreCase = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("IgnoreCase");
				}
			}
		}

		public object TestValue
		{
			get
			{
				return _TestValue;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _TestValue)
				{
					_TestValue = value;
					_CachedData = null;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("TestValue");
				}
			}
		}

		public BindingList<CxsSearchExpression> Subexpressions => _Subexpressions;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged
		{
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			add
			{
				_PropertyChangedHandlers = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(_PropertyChangedHandlers, value);
			}
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			remove
			{
				_PropertyChangedHandlers = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(_PropertyChangedHandlers, value);
			}
		}

		public CxsSearchExpression()
		{
			_Subexpressions = new BindingList<CxsSearchExpression>();
		}

		public void CollectRequiredProperties(List<string> requiredProperties)
		{
			CxsSearchExpressionOperator cxsSearchExpressionOperator = _Operator;
			if ((uint)cxsSearchExpressionOperator <= 1u)
			{
				foreach (CxsSearchExpression subexpression in _Subexpressions)
				{
					subexpression.CollectRequiredProperties(requiredProperties);
				}
				return;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_TestProperty))
			{
				int num = requiredProperties.BinarySearch(_TestProperty);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					requiredProperties.Insert(~num, _TestProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool MatchNode(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool flag = !_Not;
			switch (_Operator)
			{
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.And:
				return MatchAnd(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.Or:
				return MatchOr(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.Equal:
				return MatchEqual(node, _Not, _TestValue, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.NotEqual:
				return MatchEqual(node, !_Not, _TestValue, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.StartsWith:
				return MatchStartsWith(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.EndsWith:
				return MatchEndsWith(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.Contains:
				return MatchContains(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.Like:
				return MatchLike(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.LessThan:
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.GreaterThan:
				return MatchLTOrGT(node, _Operator, _Not, _TestValue, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.LessThanOrEqual:
				return MatchLTOrGT(node, CxsSearchExpressionOperator.GreaterThan, !_Not, _TestValue, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.GreaterThanOrEqual:
				return MatchLTOrGT(node, CxsSearchExpressionOperator.LessThan, !_Not, _TestValue, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.IsNull:
				return MatchIsNull(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.IsOneOf:
				return MatchIsOneOf(node, cultureInfo);
			case CxsSearchExpressionOperator.Between:
				return MatchBetween(node, cultureInfo);
			default:
				Trace.WriteLine("Operator not implemented for CxsSearchExpression");
				return _Not;
			}
		}

		public bool? CouldMatchChildren(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool? flag = null;
			return _Operator switch
			{
				CxsSearchExpressionOperator.And => CouldMatchChildrenAnd(node, cultureInfo), 
				CxsSearchExpressionOperator.Or => CouldMatchChildrenOr(node, cultureInfo), 
				_ => flag, 
			};
		}

		private void NotifyPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (_PropertyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				_PropertyChangedHandlers(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private bool MatchOr(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool result = _Not;
			foreach (CxsSearchExpression subexpression in _Subexpressions)
			{
				if (subexpression.MatchNode(node, cultureInfo))
				{
					result = !_Not;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool MatchAnd(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool result = !_Not;
			foreach (CxsSearchExpression subexpression in _Subexpressions)
			{
				if (!subexpression.MatchNode(node, cultureInfo))
				{
					result = _Not;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool MatchEqual(CxsNode node, bool not, object testValue, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool flag = not;
			try
			{
				object property = node.GetProperty(_TestProperty);
				string strA = string.Empty;
				if (property != null)
				{
					strA = property.ToString();
				}
				string strB = string.Empty;
				if (testValue != null)
				{
					strB = testValue.ToString();
				}
				if (string.Compare(strA, strB, _IgnoreCase, cultureInfo) == 0)
				{
					return !not;
				}
				return not;
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return not;
			}
		}

		private bool MatchIsNull(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			if (cultureInfo == null)
			{
				cultureInfo = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
			}
			try
			{
				if (node.GetProperty(_TestProperty) == null)
				{
					return !_Not;
				}
				return _Not;
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return _Not;
			}
		}

		private bool MatchStartsWith(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			try
			{
				object property = node.GetProperty(_TestProperty);
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (property != null)
				{
					text = property.ToString();
				}
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (_TestValue != null)
				{
					text2 = _TestValue.ToString();
				}
				if (text.Length < text2.Length)
				{
					bool not = _Not;
				}
				if (string.Compare(text, 0, text2, 0, text2.Length, _IgnoreCase, cultureInfo) == 0)
				{
					return !_Not;
				}
				return _Not;
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return _Not;
			}
		}

		private bool MatchEndsWith(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			try
			{
				object property = node.GetProperty(_TestProperty);
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (property != null)
				{
					text = property.ToString();
				}
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (_TestValue != null)
				{
					text2 = _TestValue.ToString();
				}
				if (text.Length < text2.Length)
				{
					return _Not;
				}
				if (string.Compare(text, text.Length - text2.Length - 1, text2, 0, text2.Length, _IgnoreCase, cultureInfo) == 0)
				{
					return !_Not;
				}
				return _Not;
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return _Not;
			}
		}

		private bool MatchContains(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool not = _Not;
			try
			{
				object property = node.GetProperty(_TestProperty);
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (property != null)
				{
					text = property.ToString();
				}
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (_TestValue != null)
				{
					text2 = _TestValue.ToString();
				}
				if (text.Length < text2.Length)
				{
					not = _Not;
				}
				else
				{
					not = _Not;
					int num = text.Length - text2.Length - 1;
					for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
					{
						if (string.Compare(text, i, text2, 0, text2.Length, _IgnoreCase, cultureInfo) == 0)
						{
							not = !_Not;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				not = _Not;
			}
			return not;
		}

		private bool MatchLike(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool not = _Not;
			try
			{
				Regex regex = _CachedData as Regex;
				if (regex == null)
				{
					string text = ((_TestValue != null) ? _TestValue.ToString() : string.Empty);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
						stringBuilder.Append("");
						stringBuilder.Append(text.Replace("%", ".*"));
						stringBuilder.Append("$");
						RegexOptions regexOptions = RegexOptions.None;
						if (cultureInfo == CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
						{
							regexOptions |= RegexOptions.CultureInvariant;
						}
						if (_IgnoreCase)
						{
							regexOptions |= RegexOptions.IgnoreCase;
						}
						regex = (Regex)(_CachedData = new Regex(stringBuilder.ToString(), regexOptions));
					}
				}
				if (regex != null)
				{
					object property = node.GetProperty(_TestProperty);
					string input = string.Empty;
					if (property != null)
					{
						input = property.ToString();
					}
					if (regex.Match(input) != null)
					{
						return !_Not;
					}
					return _Not;
				}
				return _Not;
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return _Not;
			}
		}

		private bool MatchLTOrGT(CxsNode node, CxsSearchExpressionOperator opType, bool not, object testValue, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool flag = not;
			try
			{
				object property = node.GetProperty(_TestProperty);
				if (property == null)
				{
					return not;
				}
				if (property.GetType() == typeof(double) || property.GetType() == typeof(int))
				{
					double num = Convert.ToDouble(property, cultureInfo);
					double? num2 = null;
					if (testValue.GetType() == typeof(double) || testValue.GetType() == typeof(int))
					{
						num2 = Convert.ToDouble(testValue, cultureInfo);
					}
					else if (testValue.GetType() == typeof(string))
					{
						num2 = double.Parse(testValue as string, cultureInfo);
					}
					if (!num2.HasValue)
					{
						flag = not;
					}
					if (opType == CxsSearchExpressionOperator.LessThan && num < num2.Value)
					{
						return !not;
					}
					if (opType == CxsSearchExpressionOperator.GreaterThan && num > num2.Value)
					{
						return !not;
					}
					return not;
				}
				string strA = string.Empty;
				if (property != null)
				{
					strA = property.ToString();
				}
				string strB = string.Empty;
				if (testValue != null)
				{
					strB = testValue.ToString();
				}
				int num3 = string.Compare(strA, strB, _IgnoreCase, cultureInfo);
				if (opType == CxsSearchExpressionOperator.LessThan && num3 < 0)
				{
					return !not;
				}
				if (opType == CxsSearchExpressionOperator.GreaterThan && num3 > 0)
				{
					return !not;
				}
				return not;
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return not;
			}
		}

		private bool MatchIsOneOf(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool result = _Not;
			try
			{
				if (_TestValue is Array { Length: var length } array)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
					{
						if (MatchEqual(node, not: false, array.GetValue(i), cultureInfo))
						{
							result = !_Not;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				result = _Not;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool MatchBetween(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool result = _Not;
			try
			{
				if (_TestValue is Array array)
				{
					object testValue = null;
					object testValue2 = null;
					if (array.Length > 0)
					{
						testValue = array.GetValue(0);
					}
					if (array.Length > 1)
					{
						testValue2 = array.GetValue(1);
					}
					result = ((!MatchLTOrGT(node, CxsSearchExpressionOperator.LessThan, not: false, testValue, cultureInfo) || !MatchLTOrGT(node, CxsSearchExpressionOperator.GreaterThan, not: false, testValue2, cultureInfo)) ? _Not : (!_Not));
				}
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				result = _Not;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool? CouldMatchChildrenAnd(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool? result = null;
			foreach (CxsSearchExpression subexpression in _Subexpressions)
			{
				bool? flag = subexpression.CouldMatchChildren(node, cultureInfo);
				if (flag.HasValue)
				{
					if (!flag.Value)
					{
						result = _Not;
						break;
					}
					result = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool? CouldMatchChildrenOr(CxsNode node, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			bool? result = null;
			foreach (CxsSearchExpression subexpression in _Subexpressions)
			{
				result = _Not;
				bool? flag = subexpression.CouldMatchChildren(node, cultureInfo);
				if (!flag.HasValue)
				{
					result = null;
					break;
				}
				if (flag.Value)
				{
					result = !_Not;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class CxsSnapshotData
	{
		private List<string> _Attributes;

		private List<string> _SnapshotIds;

		private Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, object>> _SnapshotData;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> Attributes => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_Attributes);

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> SnapshotIds => new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_SnapshotIds);

		public CxsSnapshotData()
		{
			_Attributes = new List<string>();
			_SnapshotIds = new List<string>();
			_SnapshotData = new Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, object>>();
		}

		public object GetSnapshotAttribute(string snapshotId, string attribute)
		{
			object value = null;
			if (_SnapshotData.TryGetValue(snapshotId, out var value2))
			{
				value2.TryGetValue(attribute, out value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		internal void StoreSnapshotAttribute(string snapshotId, string attribute, object value)
		{
			if (!_SnapshotData.TryGetValue(snapshotId, out var value2))
			{
				value2 = new Dictionary<string, object>();
				_SnapshotData[snapshotId] = value2;
				int num = _SnapshotIds.BinarySearch(snapshotId);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_SnapshotIds.Insert(~num, snapshotId);
				}
			}
			int num2 = _Attributes.BinarySearch(attribute);
			if (num2 < 0)
			{
				_Attributes.Insert(~num2, attribute);
			}
			value2[attribute] = value;
		}
	}
	public enum CxsAutoSolveValue
	{
		NotSupported,
		Off,
		On
	}
	public enum CxsAutoSolveStatus
	{
		Unknown,
		Idle,
		RunPending,
		Running
	}
	public enum CxsSolverRunState
	{
		Unknown,
		Idle,
		Running,
		Failed,
		Completed,
		Paused
	}
	public class CxsSolver
	{
		private CxsCase _Case;

		private bool _TrackRunState;

		private CxsSolverRunState _RunState;

		public CxsCase Case => _Case;

		public bool TrackRunState
		{
			get
			{
				return _TrackRunState;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _TrackRunState)
				{
					_TrackRunState = value;
					if (_TrackRunState)
					{
						_Case.ConnectMessageSink();
					}
					else
					{
						_Case.DisconnectMessageSink();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public CxsSolverRunState RunState
		{
			get
			{
				return _RunState;
			}
			set
			{
				_RunState = value;
			}
		}

		public string RunMode
		{
			get
			{
				return CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetSolverProperty("RunMode"));
			}
			set
			{
				SetSolverProperty("RunMode", value);
			}
		}

		public string ActiveEnvironment
		{
			get
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetSolverProperty("ActiveEnvironment"));
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return "Simulate";
				}
				return text;
			}
			set
			{
				SetSolverProperty("ActiveEnvironment", value);
			}
		}

		public double? SimulationTime => CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(GetSolverProperty("SimulationTime"));

		public string SimulationTimeUnits => CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetSolverProperty("SimulationTime", "uom"));

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> SimulationTimeUnitsList => CxsConverter.ValueToUnitsCollection(GetSolverProperty("SimulationTime", "uom_options"));

		public CxsAutoSolveValue AutoSolveValue
		{
			get
			{
				return CxsConverter.ValueToAutoSolveValue(GetSolverProperty("Autosolve"));
			}
			set
			{
				string value2 = ((value == CxsAutoSolveValue.On) ? "On" : "Off");
				SetSolverProperty("Autosolve", value2);
			}
		}

		public CxsAutoSolveStatus AutoSolveStatus
		{
			get
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetSolverProperty("Autosolve", "status"));
				CxsAutoSolveStatus result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.Unknown;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (string.Compare(text, "Idle", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.Idle;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(text, "RunPending", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.RunPending;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(text, "Running", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						result = CxsAutoSolveStatus.Running;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public bool RunIsSynchronous
		{
			get
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetSolverProperty("RunSynchronous"));
				if (!flag.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return flag.Value;
			}
		}

		public CxsActionStatusDetails RunActionStatus => GetActionStatus("Run");

		public bool CanRun => RunActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public CxsActionStatusDetails RunForActionStatus => GetActionStatus("RunFor");

		public bool CanRunFor => RunForActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public CxsActionStatusDetails StepActionStatus => GetActionStatus("Step");

		public bool CanStep => StepActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public CxsActionStatusDetails PauseActionStatus => GetActionStatus("Pause");

		public bool CanPause => PauseActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public CxsActionStatusDetails StopActionStatus => GetActionStatus("Stop");

		public bool CanStop => StopActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public CxsActionStatusDetails RestartActionStatus => GetActionStatus("Restart");

		public bool CanRestart => RestartActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public CxsActionStatusDetails InitializeActionStatus => GetActionStatus("Initialize");

		public bool CanInitialize => InitializeActionStatus.CanInvoke;

		public bool SnapshotsSupported
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsValidAction("ListSnapshots"))
				{
					return true;
				}
				string text = CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", "Snapshots");
				CxsVerificationResult[] array = _Case.VerifyPathsAndUnits(new string[1] { text }, null);
				if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
				{
					return array[0] == CxsVerificationResult.Valid;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public CxsSnapshotData SnapshotData
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_Case.IsConnected)
				{
					return null;
				}
				CxsSnapshotData cxsSnapshotData = null;
				if (IsValidAction("ListSnapshots"))
				{
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(InvokeAction("ListSnapshots"));
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						return null;
					}
					cxsSnapshotData = new CxsSnapshotData();
					XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
					xmlDocument.LoadXml(text);
					foreach (XmlNode childNode in xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
					{
						if (childNode.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
						{
							continue;
						}
						XmlElement obj = childNode as XmlElement;
						string attribute = obj.GetAttribute("id");
						foreach (XmlAttribute attribute2 in obj.Attributes)
						{
							cxsSnapshotData.StoreSnapshotAttribute(attribute, attribute2.Name, attribute2.Value);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					string text2 = CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", "Snapshots");
					string[] childPaths = _Case.GetChildPaths(new string[1] { text2 });
					if (childPaths == null || childPaths.Length == 0)
					{
						return null;
					}
					CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = _Case.BatchGetAllAttributes(childPaths, null);
					cxsSnapshotData = new CxsSnapshotData();
					foreach (string canonicalPath in cxsBatchRequestData.CanonicalPaths)
					{
						string snapshotId = CxsConverter.ValueToString(cxsBatchRequestData.GetValue(canonicalPath, "id"));
						foreach (string canonicalAttribute in cxsBatchRequestData.CanonicalAttributes)
						{
							if (canonicalAttribute != "path" && canonicalAttribute != "can_enter" && canonicalAttribute != "browser_name" && canonicalAttribute != "display_name" && canonicalAttribute != "isleaf" && canonicalAttribute != "has_children")
							{
								object value = cxsBatchRequestData.GetValue(canonicalPath, canonicalAttribute);
								cxsSnapshotData.StoreSnapshotAttribute(snapshotId, canonicalAttribute, value);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return cxsSnapshotData;
			}
		}

		internal CxsSolver(CxsCase cxsCase)
		{
			_Case = cxsCase;
		}

		public void LoadSnapshot(string snapshotId)
		{
			if (!_Case.IsConnected)
			{
				throw new CxsCaseNotRunningException("Cannot load snapshots if the case is not running");
			}
			if (IsValidAction("LoadSnapshot"))
			{
				InvokeAction("LoadSnapshot", snapshotId);
			}
			else
			{
				string path = CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", "Snapshots");
				path = CxsPath.CombinePathAndId(path, snapshotId);
				_Case.InvokeAction(path, "LoadSnapshot", null);
			}
		}

		public void CreateSnapshot(string description)
		{
			if (!_Case.IsConnected)
			{
				throw new CxsCaseNotRunningException("Cannot create a snapshot if the case is not running");
			}
			if (IsValidAction("CreateSnapshot"))
			{
				InvokeAction("CreateSnapshot", description);
			}
			else
			{
				string nodePath = CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", "Snapshots");
				_Case.InvokeAction(nodePath, "CreateSnapshot", description);
			}
		}

		public object GetSolverProperty(string propertyPath)
		{
			return GetSolverProperty(propertyPath, "value", string.Empty);
		}

		{
			return GetSolverProperty(propertyPath, attribute, string.Empty);
		}

		{
			return _Case.BatchGet(CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", propertyPath), attribute, units);
		}

		{
			SetSolverProperty(propertyPath, "value", string.Empty, value);
		}

		{
			SetSolverProperty(propertyPath, attribute, string.Empty, value);
		}

		public void SetSolverProperty(string propertyPath, string attribute, string units, object value)
		{
			_Case.BatchSet(CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", propertyPath), attribute, units, value);
		}

		internal void OnCaseMessage(CxsMessageEventArgs args)
		{
			if (!TrackRunState)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<CxsMessage> enumerator = args.Messages.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				switch (enumerator.Current.MessageType)
				{
				case CxsMessageType.RunStarted:
					RunState = CxsSolverRunState.Running;
					break;
				case CxsMessageType.RunPaused:
					RunState = CxsSolverRunState.Paused;
					break;
				case CxsMessageType.RunCompleted:
					RunState = CxsSolverRunState.Completed;
					break;
				case CxsMessageType.RunFailed:
					RunState = CxsSolverRunState.Failed;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public static class CxsAttributeUtil
	{
		public static List<string> GetAllStdAttributes()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			FieldInfo[] fields = typeof(CxsStandardAttributes).GetFields();
			for (int i = 0; i < fields.Length; i++)
			{
				object value = fields[i].GetValue(null);
				if (value is string)
				{
					string item = value.ToString();
					if (!list.Contains(item))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (CxsComCaseAdapterTraits item3 in new List<CxsComCaseAdapterTraits>
			{
				new AcmAdapterTraits(),
				new AdsimAdapterTraits(),
				new AmrAdapterTraits(),
				new AspenDynamicsAdapterTraits(),
				new AspenEnergyAnalyzerTraits(),
				new AspenPlusAdapterTraits(),
				new BatchSepAdapterTraits(),
				new ChromAdapterTraits(),
				new EdrAdapterTraits(),
				new HysysAdapterTraits(),
				new IcarusAdapterTraits(),
				new OomfAdapterTraits()
			})
			{
				string[] standardAttributes = item3.GetStandardAttributes();
				foreach (string item2 in standardAttributes)
				{
					if (!list.Contains(item2))
					{
						list.Add(item2);
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}
	}

			{
				return 0.0;
