	internal class CxsWcfCaseAdapter : ICxsCaseAdapter, IDisposable
	{
		private CxsWcfCaseServiceClient _serviceClient;

		private string _localCasePath;

		public bool IsRemote => true;

		public string ApplicationVersion => _serviceClient.GetApplicationVersion();

		public string WorkingDirectory
		{
			get
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public string CasePath => _localCasePath;

		public bool IsClosed
		{
			get
			{
				if (_serviceClient != null)
				{
					return _serviceClient.GetIsClosed();
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool DetachOnClose
		{
			get
			{
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public bool CanSaveCopyAs => true;

		public static ICxsCaseAdapter OpenCase(CxsCase cxsCase, string localCasePath)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				fileStream = new FileStream(localCasePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
				int num = (int)fileStream.Length;
				array = new byte[num];
				fileStream.Read(array, 0, num);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
			return OpenCaseFromByteArray(cxsCase, array, localCasePath);
		}

		public static ICxsCaseAdapter OpenCaseFromByteArray(CxsCase cxsCase, byte[] caseData)
		{
			return OpenCaseFromByteArray(cxsCase, caseData, string.Empty);
		}

		public static ICxsCaseAdapter OpenCaseFromByteArray(CxsCase cxsCase, byte[] caseData, string localCasePath)
		{
			string caseFilePath = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(localCasePath)) ? localCasePath : cxsCase.CaseFilePath);
			CxsWcfCaseInformation cxsWcfCaseInformation = new CxsWcfCaseInformation();
			cxsWcfCaseInformation.ApplicationName = cxsCase.AdapterTraits.ApplicationName;
			cxsWcfCaseInformation.VersionOption = cxsCase.VersionRequirements.VersionOption;
			cxsWcfCaseInformation.VersionString = cxsCase.VersionRequirements.VersionString;
			cxsWcfCaseInformation.CaseFilePath = caseFilePath;
			cxsWcfCaseInformation.CaseDataSize = caseData.Length;
			cxsWcfCaseInformation.AdapterSettings = cxsCase.AdapterSettings;
			EndpointAddress remoteAddress = new EndpointAddress(CxsWcfHelpers.WcfCaseServiceHttpUri(cxsCase.RemotingRequirements));
			CxsWcfCaseServiceClient cxsWcfCaseServiceClient = new CxsWcfCaseServiceClient(new WSHttpBinding
			{
				MaxReceivedMessageSize = 2147483646L,
				ReaderQuotas = new XmlDictionaryReaderQuotas
				{
					MaxArrayLength = int.MaxValue,
					MaxBytesPerRead = int.MaxValue,
					MaxDepth = int.MaxValue,
					MaxNameTableCharCount = int.MaxValue,
					MaxStringContentLength = int.MaxValue
				}
			}, remoteAddress);
			foreach (OperationDescription operation in cxsWcfCaseServiceClient.ChannelFactory.Endpoint.Contract.Operations)
			{
				DataContractSerializerOperationBehavior dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior = operation.Behaviors.Find<DataContractSerializerOperationBehavior>();
				if (dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior != null)
				{
					dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior.MaxItemsInObjectGraph = int.MaxValue;
				}
			}
			cxsWcfCaseServiceClient.OpenCase(cxsWcfCaseInformation, caseData, isPackage: false);
			return new CxsWcfCaseAdapter(cxsWcfCaseServiceClient, localCasePath);
		}

		private CxsWcfCaseAdapter(CxsWcfCaseServiceClient serviceClient, string localCasePath)
		{
			_localCasePath = localCasePath;
			_serviceClient = serviceClient;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				CloseCase(0);
			}
			_serviceClient = null;
		}

		public bool CloseCase(int timeoutBeforeKill)
		{
			if (_serviceClient == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			CxsWcfCaseServiceClient serviceClient = _serviceClient;
			_serviceClient = null;
			return serviceClient.CloseCase(timeoutBeforeKill);
		}

		public object InvokeAction(string nodePath, string action, params object[] args)
		{
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValue(_serviceClient.InvokeAction(nodePath, action, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValues(args)));
		}

		public object[] BatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			CxsWcfBatchGetData cxsWcfBatchGetData = new CxsWcfBatchGetData();
			cxsWcfBatchGetData.CanonicalAttributes = requests.CanonicalAttributesList;
			cxsWcfBatchGetData.CanonicalPaths = requests.CanonicalPathsList;
			List<CxsWcfBatchGetRecord> list = new List<CxsWcfBatchGetRecord>();
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
			{
				CxsWcfBatchGetRecord item = new CxsWcfBatchGetRecord
				{
					PathIndex = requests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(request.Path),
					AttributeIndex = requests.GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(request.Attribute),
					Units = request.Units
				};
				list.Add(item);
			}
			cxsWcfBatchGetData.Requests = list;
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValues(_serviceClient.BatchGet(cxsWcfBatchGetData));
		}

		public void BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			CxsWcfBatchSetData cxsWcfBatchSetData = new CxsWcfBatchSetData();
			cxsWcfBatchSetData.CanonicalAttributes = requests.CanonicalAttributesList;
			cxsWcfBatchSetData.CanonicalPaths = requests.CanonicalPathsList;
			List<CxsWcfBatchSetRecord> list = new List<CxsWcfBatchSetRecord>();
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
			{
				CxsWcfBatchSetRecord cxsWcfBatchSetRecord = new CxsWcfBatchSetRecord();
				cxsWcfBatchSetRecord.PathIndex = requests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(request.Path);
				cxsWcfBatchSetRecord.AttributeIndex = requests.GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(request.Attribute);
				cxsWcfBatchSetRecord.Units = request.Units;
				cxsWcfBatchSetRecord.Value = CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValue(request.Value);
				list.Add(cxsWcfBatchSetRecord);
			}
			cxsWcfBatchSetData.Requests = list;
			_serviceClient.BatchSet(cxsWcfBatchSetData);
		}

		public void KillCase()
		{
			_serviceClient.KillCase();
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_localCasePath))
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save case, no file path specified");
			}
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
				fileStream = new FileStream(_localCasePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
				fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
		}

		public void SaveCopyAs(string filePath)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
				fileStream = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
				fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
		}

		public void SaveAs(string filePath)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
				fileStream = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
				fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
				_localCasePath = filePath;
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
		}

		public byte[] SaveAndCopyToByteArray()
		{
			if (_serviceClient.SaveCaseToMemoryAndReturnSize() < 0)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Error save case data");
			}
			return _serviceClient.SaveCaseToMemory();
		}

		public string[] ConvertMonikersToPaths(string[] monikers)
		{
			return _serviceClient.ConvertMonikersToPaths(monikers);
		}

		public CxsHistoryData GetHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double startTime, double endTime, double interval)
		{
			return new CxsHistoryData(_serviceClient.GetHistory(paths, units, timeUnits, startTime, endTime, interval));
		}

		public CxsHistoryData GetMostRecentHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double interval, int numberOfTimePoints)
		{
			return new CxsHistoryData(_serviceClient.GetMostRecentHistory(paths, units, timeUnits, interval, numberOfTimePoints));
		}

		public object GetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, params object[] args)
		{
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValue(_serviceClient.GetAutomationProperty(propertyName, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValues(args)));
		}

		public void SetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, object value, params object[] args)
		{
			_serviceClient.SetAutomationProperty(propertyName, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValue(value), CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValues(args));
		}

		public object[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, object[] inputs)
		{
			return _serviceClient.ExecuteVBScript(script, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValues(inputs));
		}

		public void ResetMessageFilters(CxsMessageFilterSpec[] filters)
		{
			CxsWcfMessageFilterSpec[] array = null;
			if (filters != null)
			{
				array = new CxsWcfMessageFilterSpec[filters.Length];
				for (int i = 0; i < filters.Length; i++)
				{
					array[i] = new CxsWcfMessageFilterSpec
					{
						FilterBy = filters[i].FilterBy,
						FilterOperand = filters[i].FilterOperand,
						TestValue = filters[i].TestValue
					};
				}
			}
			_serviceClient.ResetMessageFilters(array);
		}

		public void ConnectMessageSink(CxsCaseMessageSink messageSink)
		{
		}

		public void DisconnectMessageSink()
		{
		}
	}
	[DataContract(Name = "CxsWcfCaseInformation", Namespace = "http://schemas.datacontract.org/2004/07/AspenTech.AspenCxs.WcfServices")]

	public static class CxsWcfHelpers
	{
		public static string WcfCaseServiceEndpoint => "CxsWcfService";

		public static string WcfAdminServiceEndpoint => "CxsWcfAdminService";

		public static Uri WcfCaseServiceBaseHttpUri(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			int num = ((requirements != null && requirements.RemotePort != 0) ? requirements.RemotePort : CxsRemotingRequirements.DefaultServicePortNumber);
			string arg = ((requirements != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(requirements.RemoteHostname)) ? requirements.RemoteHostname : "localhost");
			return new Uri(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "http://{0}:{1}/AspenRemoteSimulationService/Services/", arg, num), UriKind.Absolute);
		}

		public static Uri WcfAdminServiceBaseHttpUri(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			int num = ((requirements != null && requirements.RemotePort != 0) ? requirements.RemotePort : CxsRemotingRequirements.DefaultServicePortNumber);
			string arg = ((requirements != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(requirements.RemoteHostname)) ? requirements.RemoteHostname : "localhost");
			return new Uri(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "http://{0}:{1}/AspenRemoteSimulationService/Services/", arg, num), UriKind.Absolute);
		}

		public static Uri WcfCaseServiceHttpUri(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			return new Uri(WcfCaseServiceBaseHttpUri(requirements), new Uri(WcfCaseServiceEndpoint, UriKind.Relative));
		}

		public static Uri WcfAdminServiceHttpUri(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			Uri baseUri = WcfAdminServiceBaseHttpUri(requirements);
			Uri relativeUri = new Uri(WcfAdminServiceEndpoint, UriKind.Relative);
			return new Uri(baseUri, relativeUri);
		}

		public static CxsWcfAdminServiceClient GetCxsWcfAdminServiceClient(CxsRemotingRequirements remotingRequirements)
		{
			EndpointAddress remoteAddress = new EndpointAddress(WcfAdminServiceHttpUri(remotingRequirements));
			return new CxsWcfAdminServiceClient(new WSHttpBinding(), remoteAddress);
		}

		public static int GetWcfServiceVersion(CxsRemotingRequirements remotingRequirements)
		{
			return GetCxsWcfAdminServiceClient(remotingRequirements).GetVersionInformation();
		}

		internal static CxsWcfTypeCode ConvertToWcfTypeCode(Type t)
		{
			if (t == null)
			{
				return CxsWcfTypeCode.Null;
			}
			CxsWcfTypeCode result = CxsWcfTypeCode.Object;
			switch (Type.GetTypeCode(t))
			{
			case TypeCode.Boolean:
				result = CxsWcfTypeCode.Boolean;
				break;
			case TypeCode.String:
				result = CxsWcfTypeCode.String;
				break;
			case TypeCode.Int16:
			case TypeCode.Int32:
			case TypeCode.Int64:
				result = CxsWcfTypeCode.Integer;
				break;
			case TypeCode.Single:
			case TypeCode.Double:
				result = CxsWcfTypeCode.Double;
				break;
			case TypeCode.DBNull:
				result = CxsWcfTypeCode.Null;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static Type ConvertFromWcfTypeCode(CxsWcfTypeCode typecode)
		{
			Type result = null;
			switch (typecode)
			{
			case CxsWcfTypeCode.Boolean:
				result = typeof(bool);
				break;
			case CxsWcfTypeCode.Double:
				result = typeof(double);
				break;
			case CxsWcfTypeCode.Integer:
				result = typeof(int);
				break;
			case CxsWcfTypeCode.String:
				result = typeof(string);
				break;
			case CxsWcfTypeCode.Object:
				result = typeof(object);
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static CxsWcfValue ConvertToWcfValue(object value)
		{
			CxsWcfValue cxsWcfValue = new CxsWcfValue();
			if (value == null || value == DBNull.Value)
			{
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.Null;
				return cxsWcfValue;
			}
			cxsWcfValue.StringValue = value.ToString();
			switch (Type.GetTypeCode(value.GetType()))
			{
			case TypeCode.Boolean:
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.Boolean;
				cxsWcfValue.DoubleValue = (((bool)value) ? 1 : (-1));
				break;
			case TypeCode.String:
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.String;
				cxsWcfValue.StringValue = (string)value;
				break;
			case TypeCode.Int16:
			case TypeCode.Int32:
			case TypeCode.Int64:
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.Integer;
				cxsWcfValue.DoubleValue = (int)value;
				break;
			case TypeCode.Single:
			case TypeCode.Double:
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.Double;
				cxsWcfValue.DoubleValue = (double)value;
				cxsWcfValue.StringValue = value.ToString();
				break;
			case TypeCode.DBNull:
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.Null;
				break;
			default:
				cxsWcfValue.TypeCode = CxsWcfTypeCode.Object;
				break;
			}
			return cxsWcfValue;
		}

		internal static object ConvertFromWcfValue(CxsWcfValue wcfValue)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (wcfValue != null)
			{
				switch (wcfValue.TypeCode)
				{
				case CxsWcfTypeCode.Boolean:
					result = wcfValue.DoubleValue > 0.0;
					break;
				case CxsWcfTypeCode.Double:
					result = wcfValue.DoubleValue;
					break;
				case CxsWcfTypeCode.Integer:
					result = (int)wcfValue.DoubleValue;
					break;
				case CxsWcfTypeCode.String:
					result = wcfValue.StringValue;
					break;
				case CxsWcfTypeCode.Object:
					result = null;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static CxsWcfValue[] ConvertToWcfValues(object[] values)
		{
			if (values == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsWcfValue[] array = new CxsWcfValue[values.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				array[i] = ConvertToWcfValue(values[i]);
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal static object[] ConvertFromWcfValues(CxsWcfValue[] wcfValues)
		{
			if (wcfValues == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object[] array = new object[wcfValues.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < wcfValues.Length; i++)
			{
				array[i] = ConvertFromWcfValue(wcfValues[i]);
			}
			return array;
		}
	}
	[DataContract]

		public CxsMessageFilterBy FilterBy { get; set; }

		public int TestValue { get; set; }
	}

	public class CxsWcfServicesHost : IDisposable
	{
		private ServiceHost _CaseServiceHost;

		private ServiceHost _AdminServiceHost;

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				StopServices();
			}
			_CaseServiceHost = null;
			_AdminServiceHost = null;
		}

		public int EstimateStartTime()
		{
			return 10000;
		}

		public int EstimateStopTime()
		{
			return 5000;
		}

		public void StartServices()
		{
			StartCaseService();
			StartAdminService();
		}

		public void StopServices()
		{
			StopCaseService();
			StopAdminService();
		}

		private void StartCaseService()
		{
			if (_CaseServiceHost != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Uri uri = CxsWcfHelpers.WcfCaseServiceBaseHttpUri(null);
			_CaseServiceHost = new ServiceHost(typeof(CxsWcfCaseServiceServer), uri);
			try
			{
				WSHttpBinding wSHttpBinding = new WSHttpBinding();
				wSHttpBinding.MaxReceivedMessageSize = 2147483646L;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxArrayLength = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxBytesPerRead = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxDepth = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxNameTableCharCount = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxStringContentLength = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.SendTimeout = new TimeSpan(0, 30, 0);
				_CaseServiceHost.AddServiceEndpoint(typeof(ICxsWcfCaseService), wSHttpBinding, CxsWcfHelpers.WcfCaseServiceEndpoint);
				foreach (ServiceEndpoint endpoint in _CaseServiceHost.Description.Endpoints)
				{
					foreach (OperationDescription operation in endpoint.Contract.Operations)
					{
						DataContractSerializerOperationBehavior dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior = operation.Behaviors.Find<DataContractSerializerOperationBehavior>();
						if (dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior != null)
						{
							dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior.MaxItemsInObjectGraph = int.MaxValue;
						}
					}
				}
				ServiceMetadataBehavior serviceMetadataBehavior = new ServiceMetadataBehavior();
				serviceMetadataBehavior.HttpGetEnabled = true;
				_CaseServiceHost.Description.Behaviors.Add(serviceMetadataBehavior);
				_CaseServiceHost.Open();
			}
			catch (CommunicationException inner)
			{
				if (_CaseServiceHost != null)
				{
					_CaseServiceHost.Close();
					_CaseServiceHost = null;
				}
				throw new CxsException("Exception starting service host", inner);
			}
		}

		private void StopCaseService()
		{
			if (_CaseServiceHost != null)
			{
				_CaseServiceHost.Close();
				_CaseServiceHost = null;
			}
		}

		private void StartAdminService()
		{
			if (_AdminServiceHost != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Uri uri = CxsWcfHelpers.WcfAdminServiceBaseHttpUri(null);
			_AdminServiceHost = new ServiceHost(typeof(CxsWcfAdminServiceServer), uri);
			try
			{
				WSHttpBinding wSHttpBinding = new WSHttpBinding();
				wSHttpBinding.MaxReceivedMessageSize = 2147483646L;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxArrayLength = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxBytesPerRead = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxDepth = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxNameTableCharCount = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.ReaderQuotas.MaxStringContentLength = int.MaxValue;
				wSHttpBinding.SendTimeout = new TimeSpan(0, 30, 0);
				_AdminServiceHost.AddServiceEndpoint(typeof(ICxsWcfAdminService), wSHttpBinding, CxsWcfHelpers.WcfAdminServiceEndpoint);
				foreach (ServiceEndpoint endpoint in _AdminServiceHost.Description.Endpoints)
				{
					foreach (OperationDescription operation in endpoint.Contract.Operations)
					{
						DataContractSerializerOperationBehavior dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior = operation.Behaviors.Find<DataContractSerializerOperationBehavior>();
						if (dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior != null)
						{
							dataContractSerializerOperationBehavior.MaxItemsInObjectGraph = int.MaxValue;
						}
					}
				}
				ServiceMetadataBehavior serviceMetadataBehavior = new ServiceMetadataBehavior();
				serviceMetadataBehavior.HttpGetEnabled = true;
				_AdminServiceHost.Description.Behaviors.Add(serviceMetadataBehavior);
				_AdminServiceHost.Open();
			}
			catch (CommunicationException inner)
			{
				if (_AdminServiceHost != null)
				{
					_AdminServiceHost.Close();
					_AdminServiceHost = null;
				}
				throw new CxsException("Exception starting service host", inner);
			}
		}

		private void StopAdminService()
		{
			if (_AdminServiceHost != null)
			{
				_AdminServiceHost.Close();
				_AdminServiceHost = null;
			}
		}
	}
	[ServiceBehavior(InstanceContextMode = InstanceContextMode.PerSession)]
	public class CxsWcfCaseServiceServer : ICxsWcfCaseService, IDisposable
	{
		private CxsWcfCaseInformation _CaseInformation;

		private byte[] _CaseData;

		private ICxsCaseAdapter _CaseAdapter;

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
		}

		public int SaveCaseToMemoryAndReturnSize()
		{
			_CaseData = null;
			if (_CaseAdapter != null)
			{
				_CaseData = _CaseAdapter.SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
			}
			if (_CaseData == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return _CaseData.Length;
		}

		public byte[] SaveCaseToMemory()
		{
			if (_CaseAdapter == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save, case is not open");
			}
			return _CaseAdapter.SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
		}

		public byte[] SaveCaseToMemoryWithPackage()
		{
			if (_CaseAdapter == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save, case is not open");
			}
			return _CaseAdapter.SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
		}

		public void OpenCase(CxsWcfCaseInformation caseInformation, byte[] caseData, bool isPackage)
		{
			_CaseInformation = caseInformation;
			if (_CaseInformation.CaseDataSize != caseData.Length)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Case data does not match case size specified in caseInformation");
			}
			_CaseData = caseData;
			string fileExtension = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CaseInformation.CaseFilePath) ? string.Empty : Path.GetExtension(_CaseInformation.CaseFilePath));
			ICxsCaseAdapterTraits cxsCaseAdapterTraits = CxsAdapterServices.SelectAdapterTraits(_CaseInformation.ApplicationName, fileExtension);
			if (cxsCaseAdapterTraits == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Could not identify a suitable adapter for the case");
			}
			CxsVersionRequirements cxsVersionRequirements = new CxsVersionRequirements();
			cxsVersionRequirements.VersionOption = _CaseInformation.VersionOption;
			cxsVersionRequirements.VersionString = _CaseInformation.VersionString;
			_CaseAdapter = cxsCaseAdapterTraits.CreateAdapterFromByteArray(_CaseInformation.CaseFilePath, _CaseData, cxsVersionRequirements, _CaseInformation.AdapterSettings);
		}

		public bool CloseCase(int timeoutBeforeKill)
		{
			if (_CaseAdapter == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			ICxsCaseAdapter caseAdapter = _CaseAdapter;
			_CaseAdapter = null;
			bool result = caseAdapter.CloseCase(timeoutBeforeKill);
			caseAdapter.Dispose();
			return result;
		}

		public CxsWcfValue[] BatchGet(CxsWcfBatchGetData requestData)
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.LoadFromWcfBatchGet(requestData);
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValues(_CaseAdapter.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData));
		}

		public void BatchSet(CxsWcfBatchSetData requestData)
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.LoadFromWcfBatchSet(requestData);
			_CaseAdapter.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
		}

		public bool GetIsClosed()
		{
			if (_CaseAdapter != null)
			{
				return _CaseAdapter.IsClosed;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public string GetApplicationVersion()
		{
			if (_CaseAdapter == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return _CaseAdapter.ApplicationVersion;
		}

		public CxsWcfValue InvokeAction(string nodePath, string action, params CxsWcfValue[] args)
		{
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValue(_CaseAdapter.InvokeAction(nodePath, action, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValues(args)));
		}

		public void KillCase()
		{
			_CaseAdapter.KillCase();
		}

		public string[] ConvertMonikersToPaths(string[] monikers)
		{
			return _CaseAdapter.ConvertMonikersToPaths(monikers);
		}

		public CxsWcfValue GetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, CxsWcfValue[] args)
		{
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValue(_CaseAdapter.GetAutomationProperty(propertyName, args));
		}

		public void SetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, CxsWcfValue value, CxsWcfValue[] args)
		{
			_CaseAdapter.SetAutomationProperty(propertyName, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValue(value), CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValues(args));
		}

		public CxsWcfValue[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, CxsWcfValue[] inputs)
		{
			return CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertToWcfValues(_CaseAdapter.ExecuteVBScript(script, CxsWcfHelpers.ConvertFromWcfValues(inputs)));
		}

		public void ResetMessageFilters(CxsWcfMessageFilterSpec[] filters)
		{
			CxsMessageFilterSpec[] array = null;
			if (filters != null)
			{
				array = new CxsMessageFilterSpec[filters.Length];
				for (int i = 0; i < filters.Length; i++)
				{
					array[i] = new CxsMessageFilterSpec
					{
						FilterBy = array[i].FilterBy,
						FilterOperand = array[i].FilterOperand,
						TestValue = array[i].TestValue
					};
				}
			}
			_CaseAdapter.ResetMessageFilters(array);
		}

		public Collection<Collection<double?>> GetHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double startTime, double endTime, double interval)
		{
			return _CaseAdapter.GetHistory(paths, units, timeUnits, startTime, endTime, interval)?.RawHistory;
		}

		public Collection<Collection<double?>> GetMostRecentHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double interval, int numberOfTimePoints)
		{
			return _CaseAdapter.GetMostRecentHistory(paths, units, timeUnits, interval, numberOfTimePoints)?.RawHistory;
		}
	}

		Null,
		[EnumMember]
		String,
		[EnumMember]
		Integer,
		[EnumMember]
		Double,
		[EnumMember]
		Boolean,
		[EnumMember]
		Object
	}

		public CxsWcfTypeCode TypeCode { get; set; }

		[DataMember]
		public string StringValue { get; set; }

		[DataMember]
		public double DoubleValue { get; set; }
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs.RemotingServices
{
	internal class CxsRemotingCaseAdapter : ICxsCaseAdapter, IDisposable
	{
		private ICxsCaseAdapter _remoteAdapter;

		private ClientSponsor _clientSponsor = new ClientSponsor();

		private string _localCasePath;

		public bool IsRemote => true;

		public string ApplicationVersion => _remoteAdapter.ApplicationVersion;

		public string WorkingDirectory
		{
			get
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public string CasePath => _localCasePath;

		public bool IsClosed
		{
			get
			{
				if (_remoteAdapter != null)
				{
					return _remoteAdapter.IsClosed;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool DetachOnClose
		{
			get
			{
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public bool CanSaveCopyAs => true;

		public static ICxsCaseAdapter OpenCase(CxsCase cxsCase, string localCasePath)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				fileStream = new FileStream(localCasePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
				int num = (int)fileStream.Length;
				array = new byte[num];
				fileStream.Read(array, 0, num);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
			return OpenCaseFromByteArray(cxsCase, array, localCasePath);
		}

		public static ICxsCaseAdapter OpenCaseFromByteArray(CxsCase cxsCase, byte[] caseData)
		{
			return OpenCaseFromByteArray(cxsCase, caseData, string.Empty);
		}

		public static ICxsCaseAdapter OpenCaseFromByteArray(CxsCase cxsCase, byte[] caseData, string localCasePath)
		{
			return new CxsRemotingCaseAdapter((CxsRemotingService.ConnectToCxsRemotingService(cxsCase.RemotingRequirements.RemoteChannel) ?? throw new CxsException("Could not connect to remote simulation services")).CreateCaseAdapterFromByteArray(caseFilePath: (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(localCasePath)) ? localCasePath : cxsCase.CaseFilePath, applicationName: cxsCase.AdapterTraits.ApplicationName, caseData: caseData, versionRequirements: cxsCase.VersionRequirements, adapterSettings: cxsCase.AdapterSettings) ?? throw new CxsException("Failed to activate remote case"), localCasePath);
		}

		private CxsRemotingCaseAdapter(ICxsCaseAdapter remoteAdapter, string localCasePath)
		{
			_remoteAdapter = remoteAdapter;
			_localCasePath = localCasePath;
			if (remoteAdapter is MarshalByRefObject obj)
			{
				_clientSponsor.Register(obj);
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				CloseCase(0);
			}
			_remoteAdapter = null;
			_clientSponsor = null;
		}

		public bool CloseCase(int timeoutBeforeKill)
		{
			if (_remoteAdapter == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (_clientSponsor != null && _remoteAdapter is MarshalByRefObject obj)
			{
				_clientSponsor.Unregister(obj);
			}
			ICxsCaseAdapter remoteAdapter = _remoteAdapter;
			_remoteAdapter = null;
			bool result = remoteAdapter.CloseCase(timeoutBeforeKill);
			remoteAdapter.Dispose();
			return result;
		}

		public object InvokeAction(string nodePath, string action, params object[] args)
		{
			return _remoteAdapter.InvokeAction(nodePath, action, args);
		}

		}

			_remoteAdapter.BatchSet(requests);
		}

		public void KillCase()
		{
			_remoteAdapter.KillCase();
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_localCasePath))
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot save case, no file path specified");
			}
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
				fileStream = new FileStream(_localCasePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
				fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
		}

		public void SaveCopyAs(string filePath)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
				fileStream = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
				fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
		}

		public void SaveAs(string filePath)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = SaveAndCopyToByteArray();
				fileStream = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
				fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
				_localCasePath = filePath;
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream?.Close();
			}
		}

	public class CxsRemotingService : MarshalByRefObject, ITrackingHandler, IDisposable
	{
		public const string RemoteCxsServicesUri = "RemoteCxsServices210";

		private bool _ServiceStarted;

		private TcpChannel _TcpChannel;

		private double _AvgMemoryFree;

		private double _AvgProcessorUsage;

		private double[] _MemoryFreeHistory = new double[100];

		private double[] _ProcessorUsageHistory = new double[100];

		private PerformanceCounter _PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage;

		private PerformanceCounter _PerformanceCounterMemoryFree;

		private System.Threading.Timer _WatchTimer;

		private List<WeakReference> _RunningCases = new List<WeakReference>();

		private List<string> _Messages = new List<string>();

		private int _MaxMessageCount = 50;

		public double AverageMemoryFree => _AvgMemoryFree;

		public double CurrentMemoryFree => _MemoryFreeHistory[0];

		public double AverageProcessorLoad => _AvgProcessorUsage;

		public double CurrentProcessorLoad => _ProcessorUsageHistory[0];

		public IList<string> LogMessages
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_Messages)
				{
					return new List<string>(_Messages);
				}
			}
		}

		public IList<string> ActiveDocNames
		{
			get
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				lock (_RunningCases)
				{
					if (_RunningCases != null)
					{
						foreach (WeakReference runningCase in _RunningCases)
						{
							if (runningCase.IsAlive)
							{
								list.Add((runningCase.Target as ICxsCaseAdapter).CasePath);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return list;
			}
		}

		public static CxsRemotingService ConnectToCxsRemotingService(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			return ConnectToCxsRemotingService(requirements.RemoteChannel);
		}

		public static CxsRemotingService ConnectToCxsRemotingService(string channel)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(channel))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = "CxsTcpCallback";
			bool flag = false;
			System.Runtime.Remoting.Channels.IChannel[] registeredChannels = ChannelServices.RegisteredChannels;
			for (int i = 0; i < registeredChannels.Length; i++)
			{
				if (string.Compare(text, registeredChannels[i].ChannelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				BinaryServerFormatterSinkProvider binaryServerFormatterSinkProvider = new BinaryServerFormatterSinkProvider();
				binaryServerFormatterSinkProvider.TypeFilterLevel = TypeFilterLevel.Full;
				BinaryClientFormatterSinkProvider clientSinkProvider = new BinaryClientFormatterSinkProvider();
				int num = 0;
				ChannelServices.RegisterChannel(new TcpChannel(new Hashtable
				{
					[(object)"port"] = num,
					[(object)"name"] = text
				}, clientSinkProvider, binaryServerFormatterSinkProvider), ensureSecurity: false);
			}
			string url = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/{1}", channel, "RemoteCxsServices210");
			return Activator.GetObject(typeof(CxsRemotingService), url) as CxsRemotingService;
		}

		public static bool VerityRemotingRequirements(CxsRemotingRequirements requirements)
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				CxsRemotingService cxsRemotingService = ConnectToCxsRemotingService(requirements);
				if (cxsRemotingService != null && cxsRemotingService.CurrentProcessorLoad >= 0.0)
				{
					result = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public List<string> GetInstalledVersionsOfHysys(SimulatorVersionType verType)
		{
			List<string> result = null;
			try
			{
				result = HysysAdapterTraits.GetInstalledHysysVersionStrings(verType);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public List<string> GetInstalledVersionsOfAspenPlus(SimulatorVersionType verType)
		{
			List<string> result = null;
			try
			{
				result = AspenPlusAdapterTraits.GetInstalledAspenPlusVersionStrings(verType);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public int EstimateStartTime()
		{
			return 10000;
		}

		public int EstimateStopTime()
		{
			int num = 5000;
			foreach (WeakReference runningCase in _RunningCases)
			{
				if (runningCase.IsAlive)
				{
					num += 10000;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.Infrastructure)]
		public override object InitializeLifetimeService()
		{
			return null;
		}

		public void ServiceStart(string configFile)
		{
			InitializeService();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(configFile))
			{
				RemotingConfiguration.Configure(configFile, ensureSecurity: false);
			}
			System.Runtime.Remoting.RemotingServices.Marshal(this, "RemoteCxsServices210");
			_ServiceStarted = true;
		}

		public void ServiceStop()
		{
			if (!_ServiceStarted)
			{
				return;
			}
			TrackingServices.UnregisterTrackingHandler(this);
			KillAllRunningCases();
			try
			{
				System.Runtime.Remoting.RemotingServices.Disconnect(this);
				if (_TcpChannel != null)
				{
					ChannelServices.UnregisterChannel(_TcpChannel);
					_TcpChannel = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			_ServiceStarted = false;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				ServiceStop();
				if (_WatchTimer != null)
				{
					_WatchTimer.Dispose();
				}
				if (_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage != null)
				{
					_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage.Dispose();
				}
				if (_PerformanceCounterMemoryFree != null)
				{
					_PerformanceCounterMemoryFree.Dispose();
				}
			}
			_WatchTimer = null;
			_PerformanceCounterMemoryFree = null;
			_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage = null;
		}

		public void KillDocument(string fileName)
		{
			FindRunningCaseByFilename(fileName)?.KillCase();
		}

		public ICxsCaseAdapter CreateCaseAdapterFromByteArray(string applicationName, string caseFilePath, byte[] caseData, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string fileExtension = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(caseFilePath) ? string.Empty : Path.GetExtension(caseFilePath));
			return CxsAdapterServices.SelectAdapterTraits(applicationName, fileExtension).CreateAdapterFromByteArray(caseFilePath, caseData, versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
		}

		public CxsAppInfo[] GetAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			return CxsAdapterServices.GetAppInfo(options, adapterSettings);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.Infrastructure)]
		public void DisconnectedObject(object obj)
		{
			if (obj != null)
			{
				AddLog("Disconnected Object: " + obj.ToString());
				RemoveCaseAdapter(obj as ICxsCaseAdapter);
			}
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.Infrastructure)]
		public void MarshaledObject(object obj, ObjRef or)
		{
			if (obj != null)
			{
				AddLog("Marshaled Object: " + obj.ToString());
				AddCaseAdapter(obj as ICxsCaseAdapter);
			}
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.Infrastructure)]
		public void UnmarshaledObject(object obj, ObjRef or)
		{
			if (obj != null)
			{
				AddLog("Unmarshaled Object: " + obj.ToString());
				RemoveCaseAdapter(obj as ICxsCaseAdapter);
			}
		}

		internal void AddLog(string message)
		{
			lock (_Messages)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(DateTime.Now.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
				stringBuilder.Append(":");
				stringBuilder.Append(message);
				_Messages.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
				if (_Messages.Count > _MaxMessageCount)
				{
					_Messages.RemoveAt(0);
				}
			}
		}

		internal ICxsCaseAdapter FindRunningCaseByFilename(string casePath)
		{
			ICxsCaseAdapter result = null;
			lock (_RunningCases)
			{
				if (_RunningCases != null)
				{
					foreach (WeakReference runningCase in _RunningCases)
					{
						if (runningCase.IsAlive && runningCase.Target is ICxsCaseAdapter cxsCaseAdapter && string.Compare(cxsCaseAdapter.CasePath, casePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							result = cxsCaseAdapter;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void AddCaseAdapter(ICxsCaseAdapter adapter)
		{
			if (adapter == null || _RunningCases == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			lock (_RunningCases)
			{
				foreach (WeakReference runningCase in _RunningCases)
				{
					if (runningCase.IsAlive && runningCase.Target == adapter)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				_RunningCases.Add(new WeakReference(adapter));
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveCaseAdapter(ICxsCaseAdapter adapter)
		{
			if (adapter == null || _RunningCases == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			lock (_RunningCases)
			{
				foreach (WeakReference runningCase in _RunningCases)
				{
					if (runningCase.IsAlive && runningCase.Target == adapter)
					{
						_RunningCases.Remove(runningCase);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal void KillAllRunningCases()
		{
			List<WeakReference> list = null;
			lock (_RunningCases)
			{
				list = _RunningCases;
				_RunningCases = new List<WeakReference>();
			}
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (WeakReference item in list)
			{
				if (item.IsAlive && item.Target is ICxsCaseAdapter { IsClosed: false } cxsCaseAdapter)
				{
					cxsCaseAdapter.CloseCase(20000);
				}
			}
			list.Clear();
		}

		private void InitializeService()
		{
			TrackingServices.RegisterTrackingHandler(this);
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			TimerCallback callback = WatchTimer_Tick;
			_WatchTimer = new System.Threading.Timer(callback, null, 5000, 5000);
			_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage = new PerformanceCounter();
			_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage.CategoryName = "Processor";
			_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage.CounterName = "% Processor Time";
			_PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage.InstanceName = "_Total";
			_PerformanceCounterMemoryFree = new PerformanceCounter();
			_PerformanceCounterMemoryFree.CategoryName = "Memory";
			_PerformanceCounterMemoryFree.CounterName = "Available MBytes";
			num = _PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage.NextValue();
			num2 = _PerformanceCounterMemoryFree.NextValue();
			for (int i = 0; i < _ProcessorUsageHistory.Length; i++)
			{
				_ProcessorUsageHistory[i] = num;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < _MemoryFreeHistory.Length; j++)
			{
				_MemoryFreeHistory[j] = num2;
			}
			_AvgMemoryFree = num2;
			_AvgProcessorUsage = num;
		}

		private void WatchTimer_Tick(object obj)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			for (int num3 = _ProcessorUsageHistory.Length - 1; num3 > 0; num3--)
			{
				_ProcessorUsageHistory[num3] = _ProcessorUsageHistory[num3 - 1];
				num2 += _ProcessorUsageHistory[num3];
			}
			for (int num4 = _MemoryFreeHistory.Length - 1; num4 > 0; num4--)
			{
				_MemoryFreeHistory[num4] = _MemoryFreeHistory[num4 - 1];
				num += _MemoryFreeHistory[num4];
			}
			_MemoryFreeHistory[0] = _PerformanceCounterMemoryFree.NextValue();
			_ProcessorUsageHistory[0] = _PerformanceCounterProcessorUsage.NextValue();
			num += _MemoryFreeHistory[0];
			num2 += _ProcessorUsageHistory[0];
			_AvgMemoryFree = num / (double)_ProcessorUsageHistory.Length;
			_AvgProcessorUsage = num2 / (double)_ProcessorUsageHistory.Length;
		}
	}
}

		public static TService QueryServiceMakeUnique<TService>(object serviceProvider, Guid serviceId) where TService : class
		{
			TService result = null;
			if (serviceId == Guid.Empty)
			{
				return result;
			}
			Guid riid = IUnknownGuid;
			if (serviceProvider is UCOMIServiceProvider uCOMIServiceProvider)
			{
				object service = null;
				try
				{
					uCOMIServiceProvider.QueryService(ref serviceId, ref riid, out service);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				if (service != null)
				{
					IntPtr iUnknownForObject = Marshal.GetIUnknownForObject(service);
					result = Marshal.GetUniqueObjectForIUnknown(iUnknownForObject) as TService;
					Marshal.Release(iUnknownForObject);
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(service);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static Guid ClsidFromProgId(string progid)
		{
			Guid pclsid = Guid.Empty;
			if (NativeMethods.CLSIDFromProgID(progid, out pclsid) != 0)
			{
				return Guid.Empty;
			}
			return pclsid;
		}

		public static object CreateObjectFromProgid(string progId, bool TryAgainOnFailure = false)
		{
			object result = null;
			int num = 0;
			Guid iUnknownGuid = IUnknownGuid;
			Guid rclsid = ClsidFromProgId(progId);
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			do
			{
				try
				{
					num++;
					object obj = NativeMethods.CoCreateInstance(rclsid, null, CLSCTX.CLSCTX_SERVER, iUnknownGuid);
					if (obj != null)
					{
						IntPtr iUnknownForObject = Marshal.GetIUnknownForObject(obj);
						result = Marshal.GetUniqueObjectForIUnknown(iUnknownForObject);
						Marshal.Release(iUnknownForObject);
						Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
						break;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					if (!TryAgainOnFailure || num >= 3)
					{
						throw new CxsException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Failed to create COM object {0}", progId), inner);
					}
					if ((DateTime.Now - now).Seconds >= 120)
					{
						Thread.Sleep(60000);
					}
				}
			}
			while (TryAgainOnFailure && num < 3);
			return result;
		}

		public static bool IsProgIdInstalled(string progId)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(progId))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ClsidFromProgId(progId) != Guid.Empty;
		}

		public static bool IPersistMatchesProgId(object testValue, string progId)
		{
			Guid guid = ClsidFromProgId(progId);
			if (guid == Guid.Empty)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(testValue is UCOMIPersist uCOMIPersist))
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				Guid clsid = Guid.Empty;
				uCOMIPersist.GetClassID(out clsid);
				if (clsid == guid)
				{
					result = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool IsFileRunning(string filePath)
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				IMoniker ppmk = null;
				if (NativeMethods.CreateFileMoniker(filePath, out ppmk) != 0 || ppmk == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				IBindCtx bindCtx = null;
				if (NativeMethods.CreateBindCtx(0, out bindCtx) != 0 || bindCtx == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				result = ppmk.IsRunning(bindCtx, null, null) == 0;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static object ConnectToRunningFile(string filePath)
		{
			IMoniker ppmk = null;
			if (NativeMethods.CreateFileMoniker(filePath, out ppmk) != 0 || ppmk == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			IBindCtx bindCtx = null;
			if (NativeMethods.CreateBindCtx(0, out bindCtx) != 0 || bindCtx == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (ppmk.IsRunning(bindCtx, null, null) != 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object ppvResult = null;
			Guid riidResult = IUnknownGuid;
			ppmk.BindToObject(bindCtx, null, ref riidResult, out ppvResult);
			return ppvResult;
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxsComCaseAdapter : CxsCaseAdapter
	{
		private CxsComMessageAsyncQueue _MessageQueue;

		private CxsProcessMonitor _ProcessMonitor;

		protected override bool CanKillCase => _ProcessMonitor != null;

		protected CxsComCaseAdapter(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
			: base(adapterTraits)
		{
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				CloseCase(0);
				if (_ProcessMonitor != null)
				{
					if (!base.DetachOnClose)
					{
						_ProcessMonitor.WaitForExitOrQuit(10000, 10000);
					}
					_ProcessMonitor.Dispose();
				}
			}
			_MessageQueue = null;
			_ProcessMonitor = null;
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		protected override void Shutdown()
		{
			if (_MessageQueue != null)
			{
				_MessageQueue.Shutdown();
				_MessageQueue = null;
			}
			base.Shutdown();
		}

		public void MonitorProcess()
		{
			if (_ProcessMonitor != null)
			{
				_ProcessMonitor.Dispose();
				_ProcessMonitor = null;
			}
			int? num = InternalGetProcessId();
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				_ProcessMonitor = new CxsProcessMonitor();
				_ProcessMonitor.StartMonitoring(num.Value);
			}
		}

		public override void KillCase()
		{
			if (_ProcessMonitor != null)
			{
				_ProcessMonitor.KillProcess();
			}
		}

		protected override bool WaitForExitOrKillCase(int maxWaitTime)
		{
			if (_ProcessMonitor == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return _ProcessMonitor.WaitForExitOrQuit(maxWaitTime, 5000);
		}

		public virtual int? InternalGetProcessId()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected void SetMessageSink(CxsCaseMessageSink messageSink)
		{
			if (messageSink != null && _MessageQueue == null)
			{
				_MessageQueue = new CxsComMessageAsyncQueue(this);
			}
			if (_MessageQueue != null)
			{
				_MessageQueue.SetMessageSink(messageSink);
			}
		}

		protected void EnqueueMessage(CxsMessageEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (_MessageQueue != null)
			{
				_MessageQueue.EnqueueMessage(eventArgs);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxsComCaseAdapterV22 : CxsComCaseAdapter
	{
		[ComVisible(true)]
		[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
		private sealed class CXSDocumentEventsV22_EventHandler : _ICXSDocumentEventsV22
		{
			private CxsComCaseAdapterV22 _Adapter;

			private IConnectionPoint _ConnectPoint;

			private ICxsEventProxyV22 _EventProxy;

			private int _AdviseCookie;

			public void Notify(Array messageMatrix)
			{
				if (_Adapter == null || messageMatrix == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					if (messageMatrix == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					int length = messageMatrix.GetLength(0);
					int length2 = messageMatrix.GetLength(1);
					if (length2 == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					List<CxsMessage> list = new List<CxsMessage>(length2);
					for (int i = 0; i < length2; i++)
					{
						int j = 0;
						int eventType = (int)messageMatrix.GetValue(j++, i);
						int comSeverity = (int)messageMatrix.GetValue(j++, i);
						int system = (int)messageMatrix.GetValue(j++, i);
						int number = (int)messageMatrix.GetValue(j++, i);
						string text = (string)messageMatrix.GetValue(j++, i);
						CxsMessageType type = ComEventTypeToCxsMessageType((CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22)eventType);
						CxsMessageSeverity severity = ComSeverityToCxsSeverity((CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22)comSeverity);
						List<object> list2 = null;
						int num = 0;
						for (; j < length; j++)
						{
							object value = messageMatrix.GetValue(j, i);
							if (value != null && value.GetType() != typeof(DBNull))
							{
								if (list2 == null)
								{
									list2 = new List<object>();
								}
								while (num > 0)
								{
									list2.Add(null);
									num--;
								}
								list2.Add(value);
							}
							else
							{
								num++;
							}
						}
						ReadOnlyCollection<object> messageData = null;
						if (list2 != null)
						{
							messageData = new ReadOnlyCollection<object>(list2);
						}
						CxsMessage item = new CxsMessage(type, severity, number, system, text, messageData);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					_Adapter.EnqueueMessage(new CxsMessageEventArgs(new ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessage>(list)));
				}
				catch (Exception arg)
				{
					Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception while notifying SolverSuccess message: {0}", arg));
				}
			}

			internal CXSDocumentEventsV22_EventHandler()
			{
			}

			public void Connect(CxsComCaseAdapterV22 adapter, ICXSDocumentV22 doc, int majorVersion, int minorVersion)
			{
				_Adapter = adapter;
				object pUnkSink = this;
				if (majorVersion == 220 && minorVersion == 0)
				{
					_EventProxy = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid("CxsInteropComV22.CxsEventProxyV22") as ICxsEventProxyV22;
					if (_EventProxy == null)
					{
						throw new CxsException("Failed to create event proxy required to receive simulation messages");
					}
					_EventProxy.AttachEventDelegate(this);
					pUnkSink = _EventProxy;
				}
				Guid riid = typeof(_ICXSDocumentEventsV22).GUID;
				try
				{
					if (doc is IConnectionPointContainer connectionPointContainer)
					{
						connectionPointContainer.FindConnectionPoint(ref riid, out _ConnectPoint);
						if (_ConnectPoint != null)
						{
							_ConnectPoint.Advise(pUnkSink, out _AdviseCookie);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}

			public void Disconnect()
			{
				_Adapter = null;
				if (_EventProxy != null)
				{
					_EventProxy.DetachEventDelegate();
					_EventProxy = null;
				}
				if (_ConnectPoint != null)
				{
					try
					{
						_ConnectPoint.Unadvise(_AdviseCookie);
						Marshal.ReleaseComObject(_ConnectPoint);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					_ConnectPoint = null;
				}
			}

			private static CxsMessageType ComEventTypeToCxsMessageType(CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22 eventType)
			{
				return eventType switch
				{
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_INFORMATION => CxsMessageType.GeneralInformation, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_WARNING => CxsMessageType.GeneralWarning, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_ERROR => CxsMessageType.GeneralError, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_STARTED => CxsMessageType.RunStarted, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_FAILED => CxsMessageType.RunFailed, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.RunCompleted, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_STEP_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.RunStepCompleted, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_PAUSED => CxsMessageType.RunPaused, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_DOCUMENT_CLOSED => CxsMessageType.DocumentClosed, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_ECON_ANALYSIS_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.EconAnalysisCompleted, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_POSTPROCESSING_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.RunPostProcessingCompleted, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_HYSYS_OPTIMIZER_STARTED => CxsMessageType.OptimizerStarted, 
					CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_HYSYS_OPTIMIZER_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.OptimizerCompleted, 
					_ => CxsMessageType.Custom, 
				};
			}

			private static CxsMessageSeverity ComSeverityToCxsSeverity(CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22 comSeverity)
			{
				CxsMessageSeverity result = CxsMessageSeverity.Information;
				switch (comSeverity)
				{
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_SYSTEM:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.SystemError;
					break;
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_TERMINAL:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.TerminalError;
					break;
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERE:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.SevereError;
					break;
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_ERROR:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.Error;
					break;
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_WARNING:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.Warning;
					break;
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_INFORMATION:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.Information;
					break;
				case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V22.CXS_MESSAGE_DIAGNOSTIC:
					result = CxsMessageSeverity.Diagnostic;
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public override object[] BatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			if (requests == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			ReadOnlyCollection<CxsBatchRequest> requests2 = requests.Requests;
			int count = requests2.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return new object[0];
			}
			object[,] array = new object[3, count];
			string text = string.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest item in requests2)
			{
				if ((object)text != item.Path)
				{
					text = item.Path;
					num = requests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(item.Path);
				}
				if ((object)text2 != item.Attribute)
				{
					text2 = item.Attribute;
					num2 = requests.GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(item.Attribute);
				}
				array[0, num3] = num;
				array[1, num3] = num2;
				array[2, num3] = item.Units;
				num3++;
			}
			string commonBasePath = requests.CommonBasePath;
			ReadOnlyCollection<string> canonicalPaths = requests.CanonicalPaths;
			object[] array2 = new object[canonicalPaths.Count];
			int num4 = 0;
			bool flag = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(commonBasePath);
			foreach (string item2 in canonicalPaths)
			{
				array2[num4] = (flag ? CxsPath.RelativePathFromParent(commonBasePath, item2) : item2);
				num4++;
			}
			ReadOnlyCollection<string> canonicalAttributes = requests.CanonicalAttributes;
			object[] array3 = new object[canonicalAttributes.Count];
			int num5 = 0;
			foreach (string item3 in canonicalAttributes)

				array3[num5] = item3;
				num5++;

		public override void BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)

				return;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData requests3 = requests;
			if (BatchSetPreprocessRequired(requests))
			{
				requests = requests.Clone();
				BatchSetPreprocess(requests);
			}
			object[,] array = new object[4, count];
			string text = string.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			string text2 = string.Empty;

					num = requests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(item.Path);
				}
				if ((object)text2 != item.Attribute)
				{
					text2 = item.Attribute;
					num2 = requests.GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(item.Attribute);
				}
				array[0, num3] = num;
				array[1, num3] = num2;
				array[2, num3] = item.Units;
				array[3, num3] = item.Value;
				num3++;
			}
			string commonBasePath = requests.CommonBasePath;

		public override string[] ConvertMonikersToPaths(string[] monikers)

		public override CxsHistoryData GetHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double startTime, double endTime, double interval)
		{
			if (paths == null || paths.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Collection<Collection<double?>> collection = null;
			object[,] array = new object[2, paths.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < paths.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = ((units != null && i < units.Length) ? units[i] : string.Empty);
				array[0, i] = paths[i];
				array[1, i] = text;
			}
			Array history = _CxsDocument.GetHistory(array, timeUnits, startTime, endTime, interval);
			if (history != null)
			{
				long num = history.GetLength(0);
				long num2 = history.GetLength(1);
				collection = new Collection<Collection<double?>>();
				for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
				{
					collection.Add(new Collection<double?>());
				}
				for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
				{
					for (int l = 0; l < num2; l++)
					{
						collection[k].Add(CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(history.GetValue(k, l)));
					}
				}
			}
			return new CxsHistoryData(collection);
		}

		public override CxsHistoryData GetMostRecentHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double interval, int numberOfTimePoints)
		{
			if (paths == null || paths.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Collection<Collection<double?>> collection = null;
			object[,] array = new object[2, paths.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < paths.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = ((units != null && i < units.Length) ? units[i] : string.Empty);
				array[0, i] = paths[i];
				array[1, i] = text;
			}
			Array mostRecentHistory = _CxsDocument.GetMostRecentHistory(array, timeUnits, interval, numberOfTimePoints);
			if (mostRecentHistory != null)
			{
				long num = mostRecentHistory.GetLength(0);
				long num2 = mostRecentHistory.GetLength(1);
				collection = new Collection<Collection<double?>>();
				for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
				{
					collection.Add(new Collection<double?>());
				}
				bool[] array2 = new bool[num2];
				for (int k = 0; k < num2; k++)
				{
					array2[k] = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(mostRecentHistory.GetValue(0, k)).HasValue;
				}
				for (int l = 0; l < num; l++)
				{
					for (int m = 0; m < num2; m++)
					{
						if (array2[m])
						{
							collection[l].Add(CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(mostRecentHistory.GetValue(l, m)));
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return new CxsHistoryData(collection);
		}

		public override void ResetMessageFilters(CxsMessageFilterSpec[] filters)
		{
			if (_CxsDocument == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (filters == null || filters.Length == 0)
			{
				_CxsDocument.ResetMessageFilters(null);
				return;
			}
			object[,] array = new object[3, filters.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < filters.Length; i++)
			{
				CxsMessageFilterSpec cxsMessageFilterSpec = filters[i];
				CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22 cXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V = cxsMessageFilterSpec.FilterBy switch
				{
					CxsMessageFilterBy.Severity => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_SEVERITY, 
					CxsMessageFilterBy.System => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_SYSTEM, 
					CxsMessageFilterBy.Number => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_NUMBER, 
					CxsMessageFilterBy.Type => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_TYPE, 
					CxsMessageFilterBy.SuppressAll => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_SUPPRESS_ALL, 
					_ => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_SEVERITY, 
				};
				CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V22 cXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V = cxsMessageFilterSpec.FilterOperand switch
				{
					CxsMessageFilterOperand.Except => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_EXCEPT, 
					CxsMessageFilterOperand.Only => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_ONLY, 
					CxsMessageFilterOperand.Lower => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_LOWER, 
					_ => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V22.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_LOWER, 
				};
				array[0, i] = cXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V;
				array[1, i] = cXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V;
				array[2, i] = cxsMessageFilterSpec.TestValue;
			}
			_CxsDocument.ResetMessageFilters(array);
		}

		public override void ConnectMessageSink(CxsCaseMessageSink messageSink)
		{
			if (_CxsDocument != null)
			{
				if (_EventHandler == null)
				{
					_EventHandler = new CXSDocumentEventsV22_EventHandler();
					_EventHandler.Connect(this, _CxsDocument, _CxsComMajorVersion, _CxsComMinorVersion);
				}
				SetMessageSink(messageSink);
			}
		}

		public override void DisconnectMessageSink()
		{
			if (_EventHandler != null)
			{
				_EventHandler.Disconnect();
				_EventHandler = null;
			}
			SetMessageSink(null);
		}

		public override object GetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, params object[] args)
		{
			return _DocUnknown.GetType().InvokeMember(propertyName, BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, _DocUnknown, args, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		public override void SetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, object value, params object[] args)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			list.Add(value);
			if (args != null)
			{
				foreach (object item in args)
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			_DocUnknown.GetType().InvokeMember(propertyName, BindingFlags.SetProperty, null, _DocUnknown, list.ToArray(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		public override object[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, object[] inputs)
		{
			ICxsDispatchHelperV22 cxsDispatchHelperV = null;
			try
			{
				cxsDispatchHelperV = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid("CxsInteropComV22.CxsDispatchHelperV22") as ICxsDispatchHelperV22;
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Could not create dispatch helper", inner);
			}
			if (cxsDispatchHelperV == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("No access to dispatch helper required to run scripts");
			}
			Array array = null;
			try
			{
				array = cxsDispatchHelperV.ExecuteScript(_DocUnknown, script, inputs);
			}
			catch (Exception inner2)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Error processing script", inner2);
			}
			object[] array2 = null;
			if (array != null)
			{
				array2 = new object[array.Length];
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					array2[i] = array.GetValue(i);
				}
			}
			return array2;
		}
	}
	internal class CxsComMessageAsyncQueue
	{
		private CxsCaseMessageSink _MessageSink;

		private WeakReference _ParentRef;

		private Queue<CxsMessageEventArgs> _QueuedMessages;

		private bool IsAlive
		{
			get
			{
				if (_ParentRef != null)
				{
					return _ParentRef.IsAlive;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal CxsComMessageAsyncQueue(CxsComCaseAdapter parent)
		{
			_ParentRef = new WeakReference(parent);
			_QueuedMessages = new Queue<CxsMessageEventArgs>();
		}

		internal void Shutdown()
		{
			_ParentRef = null;
			_QueuedMessages = null;
		}

		internal void SetMessageSink(CxsCaseMessageSink sink)
		{
			_MessageSink = sink;
		}

		internal void EnqueueMessage(CxsMessageEventArgs msg)
		{
			if (_MessageSink == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			lock (_QueuedMessages)
			{
				_QueuedMessages.Enqueue(msg);
				Task.Factory.StartNew(delegate
				{
					OnFlushQueue();
				});
			}
		}

		private void OnFlushQueue()
		{
			if (!IsAlive)
			{
				return;
			}
			lock (this)
			{
				int num = 1;
				while (num > 0)
				{
					CxsMessageEventArgs e = null;
					if (!IsAlive)
					{
						break;
					}
					lock (_QueuedMessages)
					{
						num = _QueuedMessages.Count;
						if (num > 0)
						{
							e = _QueuedMessages.Dequeue();
						}
					}
					if (e != null)
					{
						try
						{
							_MessageSink.OnMessage(e);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxsComCaseAdapterTraits : CxsCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		protected string VersionSpecificProgId(CxsVersionRequirements version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			CxsVersionOption cxsVersionOption = CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion;
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (version != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(version.VersionString))
			{
				cxsVersionOption = version.VersionOption;
				text = version.VersionString;
			}
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			switch (cxsVersionOption)
			{
			case CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion:
				text2 = LatestInstalledProgId(string.Empty, adapterSettings);
				break;
			case CxsVersionOption.ExactVersion:
				text2 = ExactVersionProgId(text, adapterSettings);
				if (!ComHelpers.IsProgIdInstalled(text2))
				{
					text2 = string.Empty;
				}
				break;
			case CxsVersionOption.MinimumVersion:
				text2 = LatestInstalledProgId(text, adapterSettings);
				break;
			}
			return text2;
		}

		protected string LatestInstalledProgId(string minimumVersion, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string text = LatestInstalledProgId(adapterSettings);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(minimumVersion))
			{
				int num = ProgIdOrdinal(text);
				string progId = ExactVersionProgId(minimumVersion, adapterSettings);
				int num2 = ProgIdOrdinal(progId);
				if (num != 0 && num2 != 0)
				{
					if (num2 > num)
					{
						text = string.Empty;
					}
				}
				else if (!IsMinReqSatisfied(minimumVersion, text))
				{
					text = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected abstract bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID);

		protected abstract string GetLatestInstalledProgID(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings);

		protected virtual string LatestInstalledProgId(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string text = GetLatestInstalledProgID(adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				foreach (string item in SupportedVersions())
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						text = ExactVersionProgId(item, adapterSettings);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected abstract string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings);

		protected virtual bool IsVersionInstalled(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			return ComHelpers.IsProgIdInstalled(ExactVersionProgId(version, adapterSettings));
		}

		protected abstract int ProgIdOrdinal(string progId);

		protected virtual IEnumerable<string> SupportedVersions()
		{
			return CxsStandardVersions.CommonSupportedVersions;
		}
	}
	public class CxsComCaseAdapterV20 : CxsComCaseAdapter
	{
		[ComVisible(true)]
		[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
		private sealed class CXSDocumentEventsV20_EventHandler : _ICXSMessageServiceEventsV20
		{
			private CxsComCaseAdapterV20 _Adapter;

			private IConnectionPoint _ConnectPoint;

			private ICxsEventProxyV20 _EventProxy;

			private int _AdviseCookie;

			public bool IgnoreEvents { get; set; }

			public void Notify2(object[,] messageMatrix)
			{
				if (_Adapter == null || messageMatrix == null || IgnoreEvents)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					int length = messageMatrix.GetLength(0);
					int length2 = messageMatrix.GetLength(1);
					if (length2 == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					List<CxsMessage> list = new List<CxsMessage>(length2);
					for (int i = 0; i < length2; i++)
					{
						int j = 0;
						int eventType = (int)messageMatrix[j++, i];
						int comSeverity = (int)messageMatrix[j++, i];
						int system = (int)messageMatrix[j++, i];
						int number = (int)messageMatrix[j++, i];
						string text = (string)messageMatrix[j++, i];
						CxsMessageType type = ComEventTypeToCxsMessageType((CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20)eventType);
						CxsMessageSeverity severity = ComSeverityToCxsSeverity((CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20)comSeverity);
						List<object> list2 = null;
						int num = 0;
						for (; j < length; j++)
						{
							object obj = messageMatrix[j, i];
							if (obj != null && obj.GetType() != typeof(DBNull))
							{
								if (list2 == null)
								{
									list2 = new List<object>();
								}
								while (num > 0)
								{
									list2.Add(null);
									num--;
								}
								list2.Add(obj);
							}
							else
							{
								num++;
							}
						}
						ReadOnlyCollection<object> messageData = null;
						if (list2 != null)
						{
							messageData = new ReadOnlyCollection<object>(list2);
						}
						CxsMessage item = new CxsMessage(type, severity, number, system, text, messageData);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					_Adapter.EnqueueMessage(new CxsMessageEventArgs(new ReadOnlyCollection<CxsMessage>(list)));
				}
				catch (Exception arg)
				{
					Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Exception while notifying SolverSuccess message: {0}", arg));
				}
			}

			internal CXSDocumentEventsV20_EventHandler()
			{
				IgnoreEvents = false;
			}

			public void Connect(CxsComCaseAdapterV20 adapter, ICXSMessageServiceV20 messageService)
			{
				_Adapter = adapter;
				_EventProxy = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid("CxsInteropComV20.CxsEventProxyV20") as ICxsEventProxyV20;
				if (_EventProxy == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Failed to create event proxy required to receive simulation messages");
				}
				_EventProxy.AttachEventDelegate(this);
				Guid riid = typeof(_ICXSMessageServiceEventsV20).GUID;
				try
				{
					if (messageService is IConnectionPointContainer connectionPointContainer)
					{
						connectionPointContainer.FindConnectionPoint(ref riid, out _ConnectPoint);
						if (_ConnectPoint != null)
						{
							_ConnectPoint.Advise(_EventProxy, out _AdviseCookie);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}

			public void Disconnect()
			{
				_Adapter = null;
				if (_EventProxy != null)
				{
					_EventProxy.DetachEventDelegate();
					_EventProxy = null;
				}
				if (_ConnectPoint != null)
				{
					try
					{
						_ConnectPoint.Unadvise(_AdviseCookie);
						Marshal.ReleaseComObject(_ConnectPoint);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					_ConnectPoint = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool IsRealAttribute(string attribute)

		protected virtual object GetValidActions(string path)
		{
			if (FilterSolverNodePath(path))
			{
				return LegacySolverGetValidActions(path);
			}
			object[] array = null;
			ICXSNodeCursorV20 iCXSNodeCursorV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSNodeCursorV = ResolveCursor(path);
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					array = iCXSNodeCursorV.ValidActions;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				}
			}
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (array != null)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				object[] array2 = array;
				for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
				{
					string value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array2[i]);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
					{
						if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)

							stringBuilder.Append('\n');

			}

		}

		protected virtual object GetActionDescription(string path, string action)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(action))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (FilterSolverNodePath(path))
			{
				if (string.Compare(path, "Top.Solver", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					switch (action)
					{
					case "Run":
						return "Start the solver";
					case "Initialize":
						return "Initialize the solver";
					case "Stop":
						return "Stop the solver";
					}

			}
			string result = string.Empty;
			ICXSNodeCursorV20 iCXSNodeCursorV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSNodeCursorV = ResolveCursor(path);
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)

				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual object GetActionStatus(string path, string action)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(action))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (FilterSolverNodePath(path))
			{
				return LegacySolverGetActionStatus(path, action);
			}
			object[,] array = null;
			ICXSNodeCursorV20 iCXSNodeCursorV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSNodeCursorV = ResolveCursor(path);
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					array = iCXSNodeCursorV.CanInvokeAction(action);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				}
			}
			object result = null;
			if (array != null && array.GetLength(0) >= 4)
			{
				CxsActionStatus cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvoke;
				string value = string.Empty;
				int length = array.GetLength(1);
				for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
				{
					CxsMessageSeverity cxsMessageSeverity = CxsMessageSeverity.Error;
					string empty = string.Empty;
					int num = 0;
					if (array.GetLength(0) > 4)
					{
						num++;
					}
					cxsMessageSeverity = ComSeverityToCxsSeverity((CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20)array[num, i]);
					num++;
					num++;
					num++;
					empty = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[num, i]);
					num++;
					if (cxsMessageSeverity != CxsMessageSeverity.Warning)
					{
						if ((uint)(cxsMessageSeverity - 4) <= 1u)
						{
							continue;
						}
						cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke;
						value = empty;
					}
					else
					{
						if (cxsActionStatus == CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke)
						{
							continue;
						}
						cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvokeWithWarnings;
						value = empty;
					}
					if (cxsActionStatus == CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				switch (cxsActionStatus)
				{
				case CxsActionStatus.CanInvoke:
					stringBuilder.Append('0');
					break;
				case CxsActionStatus.CanInvokeWithWarnings:
					stringBuilder.Append('1');
					break;
				case CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke:
					stringBuilder.Append('2');
					break;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
				result = stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual object LegacySolverGetActionStatus(string path, string action)
		{
			if (string.Compare(path, "Top.Solver", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string result = "2Action is not supported";
			ICXSSolverV20 iCXSSolverV = null;
			try
			{
				switch (action)
				{
				case "Run":
				{
					iCXSSolverV = ComHelpers.QueryServiceMakeUnique<ICXSSolverV20>(_CxsDocument, typeof(ICXSSolverV20).GUID);
					if (iCXSSolverV == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					object[,] array = iCXSSolverV.CanSolve();
					if (array == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					CxsActionStatus cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvoke;
					string value = string.Empty;
					bool flag = array.GetLength(0) > 4;
					int length = array.GetLength(1);
					for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
					{
						CxsMessageSeverity cxsMessageSeverity = CxsMessageSeverity.Information;
						string empty = string.Empty;
						int num = 0;
						if (flag)
						{
							num++;
						}
						cxsMessageSeverity = ComSeverityToCxsSeverity((CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20)array[num, i]);
						num++;
						num++;
						num++;
						empty = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[num, i]);
						num++;
						if (cxsMessageSeverity != CxsMessageSeverity.Warning)
						{
							if ((uint)(cxsMessageSeverity - 4) <= 1u)
							{
								continue;
							}
							cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke;
							value = empty;
						}
						else
						{
							if (cxsActionStatus == CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke)
							{
								continue;
							}
							cxsActionStatus = CxsActionStatus.CanInvokeWithWarnings;
							value = empty;
						}
						if (cxsActionStatus == CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
					switch (cxsActionStatus)
					{
					case CxsActionStatus.CanInvoke:
						stringBuilder.Append('0');
						break;
					case CxsActionStatus.CanInvokeWithWarnings:
						stringBuilder.Append('1');
						break;
					case CxsActionStatus.CannotInvoke:
						stringBuilder.Append('2');
						break;
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
					result = stringBuilder.ToString();
					break;
				}
				case "Initialize":
					result = "0";
					break;
				case "Stop":
					result = "0";
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSSolverV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSSolverV);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual object GetActionIsValid(string path, string action)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(action))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (FilterSolverNodePath(path))
			{
				if (string.Compare(path, "Top.Solver", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					switch (action)
					{
					case "Run":
						return true;
					case "Initialize":
						return true;
					case "Stop":
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			ICXSNodeCursorV20 iCXSNodeCursorV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSNodeCursorV = ResolveCursor(path);
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					flag = iCXSNodeCursorV.IsValidAction(action);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				}
			}
			return flag ? 1 : 0;
		}

					if (!flag.HasValue)
					{
						bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(iCXSNodeCursorV["isleaf"]);
						flag = ((!flag2.HasValue) ? new bool?(true) : new bool?(!flag2.Value));
					}
					if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
					{
						ICXSNodeCollectionV20 iCXSNodeCollectionV = null;

					}
