			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				}
			}
			bool flag3 = !_solverNodeExists.HasValue && path == "Top";
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (array != null)
			{
				object[] array2 = array;
				for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
				{
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array2[i]);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append('\n');
						}
						stringBuilder.Append(text);
						if (flag3 && text == "Solver")
						{
							_solverNodeExists = true;
							flag3 = false;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag3 && !_solverNodeExists.HasValue)
			{
				_solverNodeExists = false;
			}
			if (path == "Top" && !_solverNodeExists.Value)
			{
				if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append('\n');
				}
				stringBuilder.Append("Solver");
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length <= 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		protected virtual bool BatchGetPostProcessRequired(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			foreach (string canonicalAttribute in requests.CanonicalAttributes)
			{
				if (string.Compare(canonicalAttribute, "action_description", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Compare(canonicalAttribute, "action_status", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Compare(canonicalAttribute, "action_is_valid", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Compare(canonicalAttribute, "valid_actions", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Compare(canonicalAttribute, "valid_attributes", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Compare(canonicalAttribute, "child_ids", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void BatchGetPostProcess(CxsBatchRequestData requests, object[] values)
		{
			int num = -1;
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
			{
				num++;
				if (values[num] == null)
				{
					if (string.Compare(request.Attribute, "valid_actions", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						values[num] = GetValidActions(request.Path);
					}
					else if (string.Compare(request.Attribute, "action_description", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						string units = request.Units;
						values[num] = GetActionDescription(request.Path, units);
					}
					else if (string.Compare(request.Attribute, "action_status", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						string units2 = request.Units;
						values[num] = GetActionStatus(request.Path, units2);
					}
					else if (string.Compare(request.Attribute, "action_is_valid", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						string units3 = request.Units;
						values[num] = GetActionIsValid(request.Path, units3);
					}
					else if (string.Compare(request.Attribute, "valid_attributes", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						values[num] = GetValidAttributes(request.Path);
					}
					else if (string.Compare(request.Attribute, "child_ids", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						values[num] = GetChildIds(request.Path);
					}
					else if (FilterSolverNodePath(request.Path))
					{
						string units4 = request.Units;
						values[num] = LegacySolverGetAttribute(request.Path, request.Attribute, units4);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public override object[] BatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			if (requests == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object[] array = null;
			ReadOnlyCollection<CxsBatchRequest> requests2 = requests.Requests;
			int count = requests2.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return new object[0];
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int[] array2 = new int[requests.CanonicalAttributes.Count];
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < requests.CanonicalAttributes.Count; i++)
			{
				string text = requests.CanonicalAttributes[i];
				if (IsRealAttribute(text))
				{
					list.Add(text);
					array2[i] = list.Count - 1;
				}
				else
				{
					array2[i] = -1;
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			int[] array3 = new int[requests.CanonicalPaths.Count];
			bool flag2 = false;
			for (int j = 0; j < requests.CanonicalPaths.Count; j++)
			{
				string text2 = requests.CanonicalPaths[j];
				if (IsRealPath(text2))
				{
					string item = string.Empty;
					foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
					{
						if ((object)request.Path == text2 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(request.Units))
						{
							string text3 = request.Attribute;
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
							{
								text3 = "value";
							}
							if (!flag || list.IndexOf(text3) >= 0)
							{
								item = request.Units;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					list2.Add(text2);
					list3.Add(item);
					array3[j] = list2.Count - 1;
				}
				else
				{
					array3[j] = -1;
					flag2 = true;
				}
			}
			object[,] array4 = null;
			if (list2.Count > 0 && list.Count > 0)
			{
				ICXSNodeCollectionV20 iCXSNodeCollectionV = null;
				try
				{
					iCXSNodeCollectionV = CreateCollection(list2.ToArray(), list3.ToArray());
					object[] array5 = new object[list.Count];
					for (int k = 0; k < list.Count; k++)
					{
						array5[k] = list[k];
					}
					array4 = iCXSNodeCollectionV.GetAttributes(array5);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					array4 = null;
				}
				finally
				{
					if (iCXSNodeCollectionV != null)
					{
						Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCollectionV);
						iCXSNodeCollectionV = null;
					}
				}
			}
			array = new object[count];
			if (array4 != null)
			{
				string text4 = string.Empty;
				int num = 0;
				string text5 = string.Empty;
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = 0;
				foreach (CxsBatchRequest item2 in requests2)
				{
					if ((object)text4 != item2.Path)
					{
						text4 = item2.Path;
						num = requests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(item2.Path);
					}
					if ((object)text5 != item2.Attribute)
					{
						text5 = item2.Attribute;
						num2 = requests.GetCanonicalAttributeIndex(item2.Attribute);
					}
					object obj = null;
					int num4 = array3[num];
					int num5 = array2[num2];
					if (num4 >= 0 && num5 >= 0)
					{
						obj = array4[num5, num4];
						if (obj != null && obj.GetType() == typeof(DBNull))
						{
							obj = null;
						}
					}
					array[num3] = obj;
					num3++;
				}
			}
			if (flag2 || BatchGetPostProcessRequired(requests))
			{
				BatchGetPostProcess(requests, array);
			}
			return array;
		}

		public override void BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			if (requests == null || requests.Requests.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			List<CxsBatchRequest> list = new List<CxsBatchRequest>();
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
			{
				if (request.Attribute == "uom")
				{
					continue;
				}
				string units = request.Units;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(units))
				{
					string value = null;
					dictionary.TryGetValue(request.Path, out value);
					if (value != units)
					{
						list.Add(new CxsBatchRequest(request.Path, "uom", null, units));
						dictionary[request.Path] = units;
					}
				}
				list.Add(request);
			}
			BatchSetPreprocess(requests, list);
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				object[,] array = new object[3, list.Count];
				string text = string.Empty;
				int num = -1;
				int num2 = 0;
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (CxsBatchRequest item in list)
				{
					if ((object)item.Path != text)
					{
						text = item.Path;
						if (IsRealPath(item.Path))
						{
							num = list2.IndexOf(item.Path);
							if (num < 0)
							{
								list2.Add(item.Path);
								num = list2.Count - 1;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num = -1;
						}
					}
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						array[0, num2] = num;
						array[1, num2] = item.Attribute;
						array[2, num2] = item.Value;
						num2++;
					}
				}
				if (list2.Count > 0)
				{
					ICXSNodeCollectionV20 iCXSNodeCollectionV = null;
					try
					{
						iCXSNodeCollectionV = CreateCollection(list2.ToArray(), null);
						iCXSNodeCollectionV.SetSpecificAttributes(array);
					}
					finally
					{
						if (iCXSNodeCollectionV != null)
						{
							Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCollectionV);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			BatchSetPostprocess(requests, list);
		}

		public override int? InternalGetProcessId()
		{

		public override object InvokeAction(string nodePath, string action, params object[] args)
		{
			if (_CxsDocument == null)
			{
				throw new CxsCaseNotRunningException("Cannot invoke action case is not active");
			}
			if (FilterSolverNodePath(nodePath))
			{
				return LegacySolverInvokeAction(nodePath, action, args);
			}
			object result = null;
			ICXSNodeCursorV20 iCXSNodeCursorV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSNodeCursorV = ResolveCursor(nodePath);
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					result = iCXSNodeCursorV.InvokeAction(action, args);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCursorV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual object LegacySolverInvokeAction(string path, string action, object[] args)
		{
			if (string.Compare(path, "Top.Solver", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object result = null;
			ICXSSolverV20 iCXSSolverV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSSolverV = ComHelpers.QueryServiceMakeUnique<ICXSSolverV20>(_CxsDocument, typeof(ICXSSolverV20).GUID);
				if (iCXSSolverV != null)
				{
					switch (action)
					{
					case "Run":
						result = iCXSSolverV.AsynchronousSolve();
						break;
					case "Initialize":
						iCXSSolverV.Initialize();
						break;
					case "Stop":
						iCXSSolverV.Stop();
						break;
					default:
						throw new CxsException("Unsupported solver action");
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSSolverV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSSolverV);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

			if (_CxsDocument == null)

				return null;

				iCXSNodeCollectionV.AddNodesFromClipboard();
				object[] attribute = iCXSNodeCollectionV.GetAttribute("path");
				int num = ((attribute != null) ? attribute.Length : 0);
				array = new string[num];
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					array[i] = CxsConverter.ValueToString(attribute[i]);

			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCollectionV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCollectionV);
				}
			}
		}

		public override CxsHistoryData GetHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double startTime, double endTime, double interval)
		{
			if (paths == null || paths.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object[,] array = null;
			ICXSNodeCollectionV20 iCXSNodeCollectionV = null;
			try
			{
				iCXSNodeCollectionV = CreateCollection(paths, units);
				array = iCXSNodeCollectionV.GetHistory(timeUnits, startTime, endTime, interval);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (iCXSNodeCollectionV != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCollectionV);
				}
			}
			Collection<Collection<double?>> collection = null;
			if (array != null)
			{
				long num = array.GetLength(0);
				long num2 = array.GetLength(1);
				collection = new Collection<Collection<double?>>();
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					collection.Add(new Collection<double?>());
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < num2; k++)
					{
						collection[j].Add(CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(array.GetValue(j, k)));
					}
				}
			}
			return new CxsHistoryData(collection);
		}

			finally
			{

				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCollectionV);

		public override void ResetMessageFilters(CxsMessageFilterSpec[] filters)
		{
			if (_CxsDocument == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_MessageService == null)
			{
				_MessageService = ComHelpers.QueryServiceMakeUnique<ICXSMessageServiceV20>(_CxsDocument, typeof(ICXSMessageServiceV20).GUID);
			}
			if (_MessageService == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_MessageService.ClearFilter(CXS_MSGSVC_DESTINATION_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_DEST_ALL);
			if (filters != null && filters.Length != 0)
			{
				foreach (CxsMessageFilterSpec cxsMessageFilterSpec in filters)
				{
					CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20 field = cxsMessageFilterSpec.FilterBy switch
					{
						CxsMessageFilterBy.Severity => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_SEVERITY, 
						CxsMessageFilterBy.System => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_SYSTEM, 
						CxsMessageFilterBy.Number => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_NUMBER, 
						CxsMessageFilterBy.Type => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_TYPE, 
						CxsMessageFilterBy.SuppressAll => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_SUPPRESS_ALL, 
						_ => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_FIELD_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_BY_SEVERITY, 
					};
					CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V20 operand = cxsMessageFilterSpec.FilterOperand switch
					{
						CxsMessageFilterOperand.Except => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_EXCEPT, 
						CxsMessageFilterOperand.Only => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_ONLY, 
						CxsMessageFilterOperand.Lower => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_LOWER, 
						_ => CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_OPERAND_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_FILTER_LOWER, 
					};
					_MessageService.FilterMessages(CXS_MSGSVC_DESTINATION_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_DEST_ALL, field, operand, cxsMessageFilterSpec.TestValue);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void ConnectMessageSink(CxsCaseMessageSink messageSink)
		{
			if (_CxsDocument == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_MessageService == null)
			{
				_MessageService = ComHelpers.QueryServiceMakeUnique<ICXSMessageServiceV20>(_CxsDocument, typeof(ICXSMessageServiceV20).GUID);
			}
			if (_MessageService != null)
			{
				if (_EventHandler == null)
				{
					_EventHandler = new CXSDocumentEventsV20_EventHandler();
					_EventHandler.Connect(this, _MessageService);
				}
				SetMessageSink(messageSink);
			}
		}

		public override void DisconnectMessageSink()
		{
			if (_EventHandler != null)
			{
				_EventHandler.Disconnect();
				_EventHandler = null;
			}
			SetMessageSink(null);
		}

		public override object[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, object[] inputs)

				{

				}
			}

		}

		private ICXSNodeCollectionV20 CreateCollection(string[] paths, string[] units)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			XmlElement xmlElement = xmlDocument.CreateElement("CollectionBackup");
			xmlDocument.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string path in paths)
			{
				int num2 = num;
				num++;
				XmlNode xmlNode = xmlElement;
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = null;
				string[] array = CxsPath.Split(path);
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					xmlNode2 = null;
					for (XmlNode xmlNode3 = xmlNode.FirstChild; xmlNode3 != null; xmlNode3 = xmlNode3.NextSibling)
					{
						if (xmlNode3.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && xmlNode3.Name == "Pathnode")
						{
							string value = xmlNode3.Attributes["id"].Value;
							if (CxsPath.IdsAreEqual(text, value))
							{
								xmlNode = xmlNode3;
								flag = true;
								xmlNode2 = xmlNode;
								xmlNode3 = null;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						XmlNode xmlNode3 = xmlDocument.CreateElement("Pathnode");
						XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = xmlDocument.CreateAttribute("id");
						xmlAttribute.Value = text;
						xmlNode3.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
						xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
						xmlNode = xmlNode3;
						xmlNode2 = xmlNode;
						xmlNode3 = null;
					}
				}
				if (xmlNode2 != null)
				{
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute2 = xmlDocument.CreateAttribute("loc");
					xmlAttribute2.Value = num2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					xmlNode2.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute2);
				}
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, new XmlWriterSettings
			{
				IndentChars = " ",
				Indent = true,
				Encoding = Encoding.UTF8
			});
			xmlDocument.WriteTo(xmlWriter);
			xmlWriter.Close();
			string xml = stringBuilder.ToString();
			ICXSNodeCollectionV20 iCXSNodeCollectionV = ComHelpers.QueryServiceMakeUnique<ICXSNodeCollectionV20>(_CxsDocument, typeof(ICXSNodeCollectionV20).GUID);
			iCXSNodeCollectionV.Restore(xml);
			if (units != null)
			{
				int num3 = 0;
				for (int k = 0; k < units.Length && k < paths.Length; k++)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(units[k]))
					{
						num3++;
					}
				}
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					object[,] array2 = new object[3, num3];
					int num4 = 0;
					for (int l = 0; l < units.Length && l < paths.Length; l++)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(units[l]))
						{
							array2[0, num4] = l;
							array2[1, num4] = "uom";
							array2[2, num4] = units[l];
							num4++;
						}
					}
					try
					{
						if (_EventHandler != null)
						{
							_EventHandler.IgnoreEvents = true;
						}
						iCXSNodeCollectionV.SetSpecificAttributes(array2);
					}
					finally
					{
						if (_EventHandler != null)
						{
							_EventHandler.IgnoreEvents = false;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return iCXSNodeCollectionV;
		}

		private ICXSNodeCursorV20 ResolveCursor(string path)
		{
			ICXSNodeCursorV20 iCXSNodeCursorV = ComHelpers.QueryServiceMakeUnique<ICXSNodeCursorV20>(_CxsDocument, typeof(ICXSNodeCursorV20).GUID);
			path = CxsPath.StripTopSegment(path);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) && !iCXSNodeCursorV.MoveToChild(path))
			{
				Marshal.ReleaseComObject(iCXSNodeCursorV);
				iCXSNodeCursorV = null;
			}
			return iCXSNodeCursorV;
		}

		private static CxsMessageSeverity ComSeverityToCxsSeverity(CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20 comSeverity)
		{
			CxsMessageSeverity result = CxsMessageSeverity.Information;
			switch (comSeverity)
			{
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_SYSTEM:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.SystemError;
				break;
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_TERMINAL:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.TerminalError;
				break;
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERE:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.SevereError;
				break;
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_ERROR:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.Error;
				break;
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_WARNING:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.Warning;
				break;
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_INFORMATION:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.Information;
				break;
			case CXS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_V20.CXS_MESSAGE_DIAGNOSTIC:
				result = CxsMessageSeverity.Diagnostic;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static CxsMessageType ComEventTypeToCxsMessageType(CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20 eventType)
		{
			return eventType switch
			{
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_INFORMATION => CxsMessageType.GeneralInformation, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_WARNING => CxsMessageType.GeneralWarning, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_ERROR => CxsMessageType.GeneralError, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_STARTED => CxsMessageType.RunStarted, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_FAILED => CxsMessageType.RunFailed, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.RunCompleted, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_STEP_COMPLETED => CxsMessageType.RunStepCompleted, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_RUN_PAUSED => CxsMessageType.RunPaused, 
				CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_TYPE_V20.CXS_MSGSVC_EVENT_DOCUMENT_CLOSED => CxsMessageType.DocumentClosed, 
				_ => CxsMessageType.Custom, 
			};
		}

		private bool FilterSolverNodePath(string path)
		{
			if (_solverNodeExists.HasValue && _solverNodeExists.Value)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (CxsPath.RelativePathFromParent("Top.Solver", path) == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return !SolverNodeExists;
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs.BuiltinAdapters
{
	internal sealed class AspenPlusAdapterTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		private static List<string> _CustomReadOnlyAttributes;

		private static List<string> _CustomConstantAttributes;

		public override string ApplicationName => "Aspen Plus";

		internal static string BaseProgId => "Apwn.Document";

		static AspenPlusAdapterTraits()
		{
			_CustomReadOnlyAttributes = new List<string>();
			_CustomConstantAttributes = new List<string>();
			string[] array = new string[2] { "upper_limit", "lower_limit" };
			foreach (string item in array)
			{
				int num = _CustomReadOnlyAttributes.BinarySearch(item);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_CustomReadOnlyAttributes.Insert(~num, item);
				}
			}
			array = new string[2] { "upper_limit", "lower_limit" };
			foreach (string item2 in array)
			{
				int num2 = _CustomConstantAttributes.BinarySearch(item2);
				if (num2 < 0)
				{
					_CustomConstantAttributes.Insert(~num2, item2);
				}
			}
		}

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			if (string.Compare(extension, ".bkp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".apw", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return string.Compare(extension, ".apwz", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(casePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("casePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
			}
			CxsComCaseAdapter cxsComCaseAdapter = null;
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				bool tryAgainOnFailure = !CxsCase.IsAPEDInitialized(VersionFromAspenPlusProgID(text));
				if (adapterSettings != null && adapterSettings.ContainsKey("TryAgainOnFailure"))
				{
					tryAgainOnFailure = adapterSettings["TryAgainOnFailure"] as bool? == true;
				}
				obj = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text, tryAgainOnFailure);
				string extension = Path.GetExtension(casePath);
				bool flag = false;
				string text2 = "Local PC";
				string text3 = null;
				string text4 = null;
				string text5 = null;
				string text6 = null;
				int num = 1;
				BindingFlags invokeAttr = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding | BindingFlags.IgnoreReturn;
				try
				{
					if (string.Compare(extension, ".apw", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0 || string.Compare(extension, ".apwz", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						object[] args = new object[8] { casePath, flag, text2, text3, text4, text5, text6, num };
						obj.GetType().InvokeMember("InitFromFile2", invokeAttr, null, obj, args, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					}
					else
					{
						object[] args2 = new object[7] { casePath, text2, text3, text4, text5, text6, num };
						obj.GetType().InvokeMember("InitFromArchive2", invokeAttr, null, obj, args2, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					if (ex.InnerException is COMException ex2)
					{
						if (ex2.ErrorCode == 2040 && obj != null && obj.GetType() != null)
						{
							BindingFlags invokeAttr2 = BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.GetProperty;
							object obj2 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("FailedToOpenKey", invokeAttr2, null, obj, new object[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
							object obj3 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("FailedToOpenDescription", invokeAttr2, null, obj, new object[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
							throw new CxsLicenseException(ex2.Message, ex2, obj2.ToString(), obj3.ToString());
						}
						throw;
					}
					throw;
				}
				cxsComCaseAdapter = SelectAdapterForCase(obj, casePath);
				if (cxsComCaseAdapter == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Could not construct adapter for case");
				}
				return cxsComCaseAdapter;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
				}
			}
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (versionRequirements != null && versionRequirements.VersionOption != CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion)
			{
				string text = InferProgId(runningCase);
				string text2 = ExactVersionProgId(versionRequirements.VersionString, adapterSettings);
				if (versionRequirements.VersionOption == CxsVersionOption.ExactVersion)
				{
					if (text != text2)
					{
						throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match specific version requirement"));
					}
				}
				else if (ProgIdOrdinal(text) < ProgIdOrdinal(text2))
				{
					throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match minimum version requirement"));
				}
			}
			return SelectAdapterForCase(runningCase, casePath);
		}

		private CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object appDispatch, string casePath)
		{
			AspenPlusAdapterV22 aspenPlusAdapterV = new AspenPlusAdapterV22(this);
			if (aspenPlusAdapterV.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return aspenPlusAdapterV;
			}
			aspenPlusAdapterV.Dispose();
			AspenPlusAdapterV20 aspenPlusAdapterV2 = new AspenPlusAdapterV20(this);
			if (aspenPlusAdapterV2.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return aspenPlusAdapterV2;
			}
			aspenPlusAdapterV2.Dispose();
			return null;
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptor CreateAttributePropertyDescriptor(CxsNode node, string attribute)
		{
			if (attribute == "default")
			{
				return new CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits("Default", attribute)
				{
					Category = "General Properties",
					IsReadOnly = true,
					Description = "The default value"
				}.CreatePropertyDescriptor();
			}
			if (attribute == "basis")
			{
				return new CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits("Basis", attribute)
				{
					Category = "General Properties",
					IsReadOnly = false,
					Description = "The basis of the node"
				}.CreatePropertyDescriptor();
			}
			return base.CreateAttributePropertyDescriptor(node, attribute);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeReadOnly(string attribute)
		{
			if (_CustomReadOnlyAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.IsAttributeReadOnly(attribute);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeConstant(string attribute)
		{
			if (_CustomConstantAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.IsAttributeReadOnly(attribute);
		}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string[] array = new string[3] { ".bkp", ".apw", ".apwz" };
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = SupportedVersions();
			string text = ApplicationName + " Files";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options != null && options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

		protected override int ProgIdOrdinal(string progId)

				result = 23;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".40.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)

		internal static string InferProgId(object runningCase)
		{
			if (runningCase == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = BaseProgId + ".41.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".40.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".39.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".38.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".37.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".36.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".35.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".34.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".32.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".31.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".30.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".29.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".28.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".27.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".26.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".25.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".24.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".23.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".22.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".21.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".20.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".13.2";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".13.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			foreach (string installedAspenPlusVersion in GetInstalledAspenPlusVersions())
			{
				if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, installedAspenPlusVersion))
				{
					return installedAspenPlusVersion;
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (string.Compare(version, "V15.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".41.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V14.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".40.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V12.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".39.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V12.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".38.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V11.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".37.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V10.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".36.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V9.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".35.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.8", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".34.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.6", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".32.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.4", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".30.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".29.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".28.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".27.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".26.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".25.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".24.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".23.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".22.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2006.5", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".21.0";
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (string installedAspenPlusVersion in GetInstalledAspenPlusVersions())
				{
					if (string.Compare(version, VersionFromAspenPlusProgID(installedAspenPlusVersion), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						text = installedAspenPlusVersion;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					text = BaseProgId + "." + version;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected override string GetLatestInstalledProgID(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			List<string> list = (List<string>)GetInstalledAspenPlusVersions();
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				result = list[0];
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetInstalledAspenPlusVersions()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				using (RegistryKey registryKey = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry64))
				{
					using RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aprsystem");
					string[] subKeyNames = registryKey2.GetSubKeyNames();
					foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
					{
						if (!Regex.IsMatch(text, "^\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
						{
							continue;
						}
						using RegistryKey registryKey3 = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey("Apwn.Document." + text);
						if (registryKey3 != null)
						{
							list.Add("Apwn.Document." + text);
						}
					}
				}
				using RegistryKey registryKey4 = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry32);
				using RegistryKey registryKey5 = registryKey4.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aprsystem");
				string[] subKeyNames = registryKey5.GetSubKeyNames();
				foreach (string text2 in subKeyNames)
				{
					if (!Regex.IsMatch(text2, "^\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					using RegistryKey registryKey6 = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey("Apwn.Document." + text2);
					if (registryKey6 != null)
					{
						list.Add("Apwn.Document." + text2);
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			list.Sort();
			list.Reverse();
			return list;
		}

		private static string VersionFromAspenPlusProgID(string ProgID)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^Apwn\\.Document\\.\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
				{
					string[] subKeyNames = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(ProgID + "\\shell").GetSubKeyNames();
					foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
					{
						if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "^Open with Aspen Plus V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
						{
							result = text.Replace("Open with Aspen Plus ", "");
							break;
						}
						if (text == "Open with Aspen Plus 2006.5")
						{
							result = "2006.5";
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static FileVersionInfo GetFileVersionFromProgID(string ProgID)
		{
			FileVersionInfo result = null;
			try
			{
				if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^Apwn\\.Document\\.\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
				{
					string[] subKeyNames = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(ProgID + "\\shell").GetSubKeyNames();
					foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
					{
						if (!Regex.IsMatch(text, "^Open with Aspen Plus V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$") && !Regex.IsMatch(text, "Open with Aspen Plus 2006.5"))
						{
							continue;
						}
						string[] array = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(ProgID + "\\shell\\" + text + "\\command").GetValue("").ToString()
							.Split(' ');
						string text2 = string.Empty;
						string[] array2 = array;
						foreach (string text3 in array2)
						{
							text2 = text2.Insert(text2.Length, " " + text3);
							if (text3.ToUpper().Contains(".EXE"))
							{
								break;
							}
						}
						text2 = text2.TrimStart(' ');
						result = FileVersionInfo.GetVersionInfo(text2);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static List<string> GetInstalledAspenPlusVersionStrings(SimulatorVersionType verType)
		{
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = null;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				enumerable = GetInstalledAspenPlusVersions();
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (enumerable != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					switch (verType)
					{
					case SimulatorVersionType.ProgId:
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item))
						{
							list.Add(item);
						}
						break;
					case SimulatorVersionType.Internal:
					{
						FileVersionInfo fileVersionFromProgID2 = GetFileVersionFromProgID(item);
						if (fileVersionFromProgID2 != null)
						{
							string text2 = fileVersionFromProgID2.FileMajorPart.ToString();
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2))
							{
								list.Add(text2);
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					case SimulatorVersionType.External:
					{
						string text3 = VersionFromAspenPlusProgID(item);
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text3))
						{
							list.Add(text3);
						}
						break;
					}
					case SimulatorVersionType.FileVersion:
					{
						FileVersionInfo fileVersionFromProgID = GetFileVersionFromProgID(item);
						if (fileVersionFromProgID != null)
						{
							string text = fileVersionFromProgID.FileVersion.ToString();
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
							{
								list.Add(text);
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID)
		{
			if (Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{1,2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				LatestProgID = VersionFromAspenPlusProgID(LatestProgID);
				LatestProgID = ((!Regex.IsMatch(LatestProgID, "^V\\d{2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				minimumVersion = ((!Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				int.TryParse(minimumVersion, out var result);
				int.TryParse(LatestProgID, out var result2);
				if (result > result2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	public class AspenPlusAdapterV22 : CxsComCaseAdapterV22
	{
		public AspenPlusAdapterV22(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
			: base(adapterTraits)
		{
		}

		protected override void BatchGetPostProcess(CxsBatchRequestData requests, object[] values)
		{
			string strB = CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", "Autosolve");
			foreach (string canonicalPath in requests.CanonicalPaths)
			{
				if (string.Compare(canonicalPath, strB, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num = 0;
				{
					foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
					{
						if ((object)request.Path == canonicalPath && (string.IsNullOrEmpty(request.Attribute) || request.Attribute == "value") && values != null && values.Length > num)
						{
							values[num] = null;
						}
						num++;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal sealed class AspenPropertiesAdapterTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		private static List<string> _CustomReadOnlyAttributes;

		private static List<string> _CustomConstantAttributes;

		public override string ApplicationName => "Aspen Properties";

		internal static string BaseProgId => "AspenProperties.Document";

		static AspenPropertiesAdapterTraits()
		{
			_CustomReadOnlyAttributes = new List<string>();
			_CustomConstantAttributes = new List<string>();
			string[] array = new string[2] { "upper_limit", "lower_limit" };
			foreach (string item in array)
			{
				int num = _CustomReadOnlyAttributes.BinarySearch(item);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_CustomReadOnlyAttributes.Insert(~num, item);
				}
			}
			array = new string[2] { "upper_limit", "lower_limit" };
			foreach (string item2 in array)
			{
				int num2 = _CustomConstantAttributes.BinarySearch(item2);
				if (num2 < 0)
				{
					_CustomConstantAttributes.Insert(~num2, item2);
				}
			}
		}

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			if (string.Compare(extension, ".aprop", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".aprbkp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return string.Compare(extension, ".apropz", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(casePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("casePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
			}
			CxsComCaseAdapter cxsComCaseAdapter = null;
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				obj = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text);
				string extension = Path.GetExtension(casePath);
				bool flag = false;
				string text2 = "Local PC";
				string text3 = null;
				string text4 = null;
				string text5 = null;
				string text6 = null;
				int num = 1;
				BindingFlags invokeAttr = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding | BindingFlags.IgnoreReturn;
				if (string.Compare(extension, ".aprop", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0 || string.Compare(extension, ".aprbkp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0 || string.Compare(extension, ".apropz", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					object[] args = new object[8] { casePath, flag, text2, text3, text4, text5, text6, num };
					obj.GetType().InvokeMember("InitFromFile2", invokeAttr, null, obj, args, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				else
				{
					object[] args2 = new object[7] { casePath, text2, text3, text4, text5, text6, num };
					obj.GetType().InvokeMember("InitFromArchive2", invokeAttr, null, obj, args2, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				cxsComCaseAdapter = SelectAdapterForCase(obj, casePath);
				if (cxsComCaseAdapter == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Could not construct adapter for case");
				}
				return cxsComCaseAdapter;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
				}
			}
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (versionRequirements != null && versionRequirements.VersionOption != CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion)
			{
				string text = InferProgId(runningCase);
				string text2 = ExactVersionProgId(versionRequirements.VersionString, adapterSettings);
				if (versionRequirements.VersionOption == CxsVersionOption.ExactVersion)
				{
					if (text != text2)
					{
						throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match specific version requirement"));
					}
				}
				else if (ProgIdOrdinal(text) < ProgIdOrdinal(text2))
				{
					throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match minimum version requirement"));
				}
			}
			return SelectAdapterForCase(runningCase, casePath);
		}

		private CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object appDispatch, string casePath)
		{
			AspenPropertiesAdapterV22 aspenPropertiesAdapterV = new AspenPropertiesAdapterV22(this);
			if (aspenPropertiesAdapterV.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return aspenPropertiesAdapterV;
			}
			aspenPropertiesAdapterV.Dispose();
			AspenPropertiesAdapterV20 aspenPropertiesAdapterV2 = new AspenPropertiesAdapterV20(this);
			if (aspenPropertiesAdapterV2.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return aspenPropertiesAdapterV2;
			}
			aspenPropertiesAdapterV2.Dispose();
			return null;
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptor CreateAttributePropertyDescriptor(CxsNode node, string attribute)
		{
			if (attribute == "default")
			{
				return new CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits("Default", attribute)
				{
					Category = "General Properties",
					IsReadOnly = true,
					Description = "The default value"
				}.CreatePropertyDescriptor();
			}
			if (attribute == "basis")
			{
				return new CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits("Basis", attribute)
				{
					Category = "General Properties",
					IsReadOnly = false,
					Description = "The basis of the node"
				}.CreatePropertyDescriptor();
			}
			return base.CreateAttributePropertyDescriptor(node, attribute);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeReadOnly(string attribute)
		{
			if (_CustomReadOnlyAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.IsAttributeReadOnly(attribute);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeConstant(string attribute)
		{
			if (_CustomConstantAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0)

		}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string[] array = new string[3] { ".aprbkp", ".aprop", ".apropz" };
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = SupportedVersions();
			string text = ApplicationName + " Files";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options != null && options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

		protected override int ProgIdOrdinal(string progId)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".41.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 23;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".40.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 22;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".39.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 21;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".38.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 20;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".37.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 19;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".36.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 18;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".35.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 17;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".34.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 16;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".32.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 15;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".31.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 14;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".30.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 13;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".29.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 12;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".28.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 11;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".27.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 10;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".26.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 9;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".25.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 8;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".24.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 7;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".23.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 6;
			}

			{

		internal static string InferProgId(object runningCase)
		{
			if (runningCase == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = BaseProgId + ".41.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".40.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".39.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".38.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".37.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".36.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".35.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".34.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".32.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".31.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".30.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".29.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".28.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".27.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".26.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".25.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".24.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".23.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".22.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".21.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".20.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".13.2";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".13.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			foreach (string installedAspenPropertiesVersion in GetInstalledAspenPropertiesVersions())
			{
				if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, installedAspenPropertiesVersion))
				{
					return installedAspenPropertiesVersion;
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (string.Compare(version, "V15.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".41.0";
			}

			{
				text = BaseProgId + ".21.0";
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (string installedAspenPropertiesVersion in GetInstalledAspenPropertiesVersions())
				{
					if (string.Compare(version, VersionFromAspenPropertiesProgID(installedAspenPropertiesVersion), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						text = installedAspenPropertiesVersion;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					text = BaseProgId + "." + version;
				}
			}
			return text;

		protected override string GetLatestInstalledProgID(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			List<string> list = (List<string>)GetInstalledAspenPropertiesVersions();
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				result = list[0];
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetInstalledAspenPropertiesVersions()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				using RegistryKey registryKey = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry32);
				using RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aprsystem");
				string[] subKeyNames = registryKey2.GetSubKeyNames();
				foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
				{
					if (!Regex.IsMatch(text, "^\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					using RegistryKey registryKey3 = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey("AspenProperties.Document." + text);
					if (registryKey3 != null)
					{
						list.Add("AspenProperties.Document." + text);
					}
				}
				list.Sort();
				list.Reverse();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static string VersionFromAspenPropertiesProgID(string ProgID)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^AspenProperties\\.Document\\.\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
				{
					string[] subKeyNames = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(ProgID + "\\shell").GetSubKeyNames();
					foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
					{
						if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "^Open with Aspen Properties V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
						{
							result = text.Replace("Open with Aspen Properties ", "");
							break;
						}
						if (ProgID == "AspenProperties.Document.21.0")
						{
							result = "2006.5";
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID)
		{
			if (Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{1,2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				LatestProgID = VersionFromAspenPropertiesProgID(LatestProgID);
				LatestProgID = ((!Regex.IsMatch(LatestProgID, "^V\\d{2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				minimumVersion = ((!Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				int.TryParse(minimumVersion, out var result);
				int.TryParse(LatestProgID, out var result2);
				if (result > result2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

	public class AspenPropertiesAdapterV22 : CxsComCaseAdapterV22
	{
		public AspenPropertiesAdapterV22(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
			: base(adapterTraits)
		{
		}

		protected override void BatchGetPostProcess(CxsBatchRequestData requests, object[] values)
		{
			string strB = CxsPath.CombinePaths("Top.Solver", "Autosolve");
			foreach (string canonicalPath in requests.CanonicalPaths)
			{
				if (string.Compare(canonicalPath, strB, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num = 0;
				{
					foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
					{
						if ((object)request.Path == canonicalPath && (string.IsNullOrEmpty(request.Attribute) || request.Attribute == "value"))
						{
							values[num] = null;
						}
						num++;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class HysysAdapterV22 : CxsComCaseAdapterV22
	{
		public HysysAdapterV22(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
			: base(adapterTraits)
		{
		}
	}
	internal sealed class HysysAdapterTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		public override string ApplicationName => "Hysys";

		private static string BaseProgId => "Hysys.Application";

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			if (string.Compare(extension, ".hsc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".fcc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".cat", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".hcr", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".hsp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(extension, ".hscz", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return string.Compare(extension, ".rhp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object appDispatch, string casePath)
		{
			HysysAdapterV22 hysysAdapterV = new HysysAdapterV22(this);
			if (hysysAdapterV.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return hysysAdapterV;
			}
			hysysAdapterV.Dispose();
			HysysAdapterV20 hysysAdapterV2 = new HysysAdapterV20(this);
			if (hysysAdapterV2.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return hysysAdapterV2;
			}
			hysysAdapterV2.Dispose();
			return null;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(casePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("casePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
			}
			CxsComCaseAdapter cxsComCaseAdapter = null;
			object obj = null;
			object obj2 = null;
			object obj3 = null;
			try
			{
				bool tryAgainOnFailure = !CxsCase.IsAPEDInitialized(VersionFromHYSYSProgID(text));
				if (adapterSettings.ContainsKey("TryAgainOnFailure"))
				{
					tryAgainOnFailure = adapterSettings["TryAgainOnFailure"] as bool? == true;
				}
				obj = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text, tryAgainOnFailure);
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.GetProperty;
					string text2 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("AppFailedKeyInfo", invokeAttr, null, obj, null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) as string;
					string slmParsedErrorMessage = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("AppFailedKeyError", invokeAttr, null, obj, null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) as string;
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2))
					{
						throw new CxsLicenseException("Failed to check out a license", null, text2, slmParsedErrorMessage);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					if (ex is CxsLicenseException)
					{
						throw;
					}
				}
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr2 = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding | BindingFlags.IgnoreReturn;
					object[] args = new object[1] { true };
					obj.GetType().InvokeMember("ChangePreferencesToMinimizePopupWindows", invokeAttr2, null, obj, args, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr3 = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.GetProperty;
					obj2 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("SimulationCases", invokeAttr3, null, obj, null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Unable to access simulation cases property", inner);
				}
				if (obj2 == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Unable to access simulation cases property");
				}
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr4 = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding;
					object[] args2 = new object[1] { casePath };
					obj3 = obj2.GetType().InvokeMember("Open", invokeAttr4, null, obj2, args2, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				catch (Exception inner2)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Error opening case", inner2);
				}
				if (obj3 == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Failed to open case");
				}
				cxsComCaseAdapter = SelectAdapterForCase(obj3, casePath);
				if (cxsComCaseAdapter == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Could not construct adapter for case");
				}
				return cxsComCaseAdapter;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj2 != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj2);
					obj2 = null;
				}
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
					obj = null;
				}
				if (obj3 != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj3);
					obj3 = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (versionRequirements != null && versionRequirements.VersionOption != CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion)
			{
				string text = InferProgId(runningCase);
				string text2 = ExactVersionProgId(versionRequirements.VersionString, adapterSettings);
				if (versionRequirements.VersionOption == CxsVersionOption.ExactVersion)
				{
					if (text != text2)
					{
						throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match specific version requirement"));
					}
				}
				else if (ProgIdOrdinal(text) < ProgIdOrdinal(text2))
				{
					throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match minimum version requirement"));
				}
			}
			return SelectAdapterForCase(runningCase, casePath);
		}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string[] array = new string[5] { ".hsc", ".fcc", ".hcr", ".cat", ".hsp" };
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = SupportedVersions();
			string text = ApplicationName + " Files";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.Runtime.V8.2", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 8;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".V8.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 7;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.V8.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 7;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.Runtime.V8.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 7;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".V7.3", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 6;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.V7.3", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 6;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.Runtime.V7.3", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 6;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".V7.2", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 5;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.V7.2", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 5;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.Runtime.V7.2", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 5;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".V7.1", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 4;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.V7.1", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 4;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.Runtime.V7.1", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 4;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".V7.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 4;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.V7.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 4;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + "2006.5", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 3;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.2006.5", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 3;
			}
			return result;
		}

			{

				{
					return text;
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
