		private static bool UseRuntimeLicense(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (adapterSettings == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			object value = null;
			if (adapterSettings.TryGetValue("UseRuntimeLicense", out value))
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(value);
				if (flag.HasValue)
				{
					result = flag.Value;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool UseRTOLicense(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (adapterSettings == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			object value = null;
			if (adapterSettings.TryGetValue("UseRTOLicense", out value))
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(value);
				if (flag.HasValue)
				{
					result = flag.Value;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			string text2 = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance";
			if (UseRuntimeLicense(adapterSettings))
			{
				text2 += ".Runtime";
			}
			if (UseRTOLicense(adapterSettings))
			{
				text2 += ".RTO";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V15.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V15.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V14.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V14.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V12.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V12.1";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V12.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V12.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V11.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V11.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V10.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V10.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V9.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V9.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.8", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V8.8";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.6", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V8.6";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.4", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V8.4";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V8.3";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V8.2";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V8.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V7.3";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V7.2";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V7.1";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".V7.0";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2006.5", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = text2 + ".2006.5";
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (string installedHYSYSVersion in GetInstalledHYSYSVersions())
				{
					if (string.Compare(version, VersionFromHYSYSProgID(installedHYSYSVersion), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						text = installedHYSYSVersion;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					text = BaseProgId + "." + version;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected override string GetLatestInstalledProgID(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			List<string> list = (List<string>)GetInstalledHYSYSVersions();
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				text = list[0];
			}
			if (UseRuntimeLicense(adapterSettings))
			{
				string text2 = text.Replace("HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.", "");
				string text3 = "HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.Runtime." + text2;
				if (Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(text3) != null)
				{
					text = text3;
				}
			}
			else if (UseRTOLicense(adapterSettings))
			{
				string text4 = text.Replace("HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.", "");
				string text5 = "HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.RTO." + text4;
				if (Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(text5) != null)
				{
					text = text5;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetInstalledHYSYSVersions()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				using RegistryKey registryKey = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry32);
				using RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\Classes");
				string[] subKeyNames = registryKey2.GetSubKeyNames();
				foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
				{
					if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
					{
						list.Add(text);
					}
					else if (text == "HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.2006.5")
					{
						list.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				list2 = (from s in list
					select new
					{
						key = ExtractHYSYSVersionNumber(s),
						value = s
					} into p
					orderby p.key
					select p.value).ToList();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			list2.Reverse();
			return list2;
		}

		private static double? ExtractHYSYSVersionNumber(string s)
		{
			double? result = null;
			if (Regex.IsMatch(s, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$") && double.TryParse(Regex.Match(s, "\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$").Value, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result2))
			{
				result = result2;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static FileVersionInfo GetFileVersionFromProgID(string ProgID)
		{
			FileVersionInfo result = null;
			try
			{
				if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$") || Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.Runtime\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$") || Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.RTO\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$") || ProgID == "HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.2006.5")
				{
					string text = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(ProgID + "\\CLSID").GetValue("").ToString();
					using (RegistryKey registryKey = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.ClassesRoot, RegistryView.Registry32))
					{
						using RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.OpenSubKey("CLSID\\" + text);
						if (registryKey2 != null)
						{
							string[] subKeyNames = registryKey2.GetSubKeyNames();
							foreach (string text2 in subKeyNames)
							{
								if (!(text2 == "LocalServer32"))
								{
									continue;
								}
								string[] array = registryKey2.OpenSubKey(text2).GetValue("").ToString()
									.Split(' ');
								string text3 = string.Empty;
								string[] array2 = array;
								foreach (string text4 in array2)
								{
									text3 = text3.Insert(text3.Length, " " + text4);
									if (text4.ToUpper().Contains(".EXE"))
									{
										break;
									}
								}
								text3 = text3.TrimStart(' ');
								result = FileVersionInfo.GetVersionInfo(text3);
							}
						}
					}
					using RegistryKey registryKey3 = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.ClassesRoot, RegistryView.Registry64);
					using RegistryKey registryKey4 = registryKey3.OpenSubKey("CLSID\\" + text);
					if (registryKey4 != null)
					{
						string[] subKeyNames = registryKey4.GetSubKeyNames();
						foreach (string text5 in subKeyNames)
						{
							if (!(text5 == "LocalServer32"))
							{
								continue;
							}
							string[] array3 = registryKey4.OpenSubKey(text5).GetValue("").ToString()
								.Split(' ');
							string text6 = string.Empty;
							string[] array2 = array3;
							foreach (string text7 in array2)
							{
								text6 = text6.Insert(text6.Length, " " + text7);
								if (text7.ToUpper().Contains(".EXE"))
								{
									break;
								}
							}
							text6 = text6.TrimStart(' ');
							result = FileVersionInfo.GetVersionInfo(text6);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static string VersionFromHYSYSProgID(string ProgID)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				result = ProgID.Replace("HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.", "");
			}
			else if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.Runtime\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				result = ProgID.Replace("HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.Runtime.", "");
			}
			else if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^HYSYS\\.Application\\.NewInstance\\.RTO\\.V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				result = ProgID.Replace("HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.RTO.", "");
			}
			else if (ProgID == "HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.2006.5" || ProgID == "HYSYS.Application.NewInstance.Runtime.2006.5")
			{
				result = "2006.5";
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static List<string> GetInstalledHysysVersionStrings(SimulatorVersionType verType)
		{
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = null;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				enumerable = GetInstalledHYSYSVersions();
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (enumerable != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					switch (verType)
					{
					case SimulatorVersionType.ProgId:
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item))
						{
							list.Add(item);
						}
						break;
					case SimulatorVersionType.Internal:
					{
						FileVersionInfo fileVersionFromProgID2 = GetFileVersionFromProgID(item);
						if (fileVersionFromProgID2 != null)
						{
							string text2 = fileVersionFromProgID2.FileMajorPart.ToString();
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2))
							{
								list.Add(text2);
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					case SimulatorVersionType.External:
					{
						string text3 = VersionFromHYSYSProgID(item);
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text3))
						{
							list.Add(text3);
						}
						break;
					}
					case SimulatorVersionType.FileVersion:
					{
						FileVersionInfo fileVersionFromProgID = GetFileVersionFromProgID(item);
						if (fileVersionFromProgID != null)
						{
							string text = fileVersionFromProgID.FileVersion.ToString();
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
							{
								list.Add(text);
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID)
		{
			if (Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{1,2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				LatestProgID = VersionFromHYSYSProgID(LatestProgID);
				LatestProgID = ((!Regex.IsMatch(LatestProgID, "^V\\d{2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				minimumVersion = ((!Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				int.TryParse(minimumVersion, out var result);
				int.TryParse(LatestProgID, out var result2);
				if (result > result2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

	public class EdrAdapterTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		private object _application;

		public override string ApplicationName => "Aspen HTFS+";

		public object Application
		{
			get
			{
				return _application;
			}
			set
			{
				_application = value;
			}
		}

		private static string BaseProgId => "BJACWin.BJacApp";

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			return string.Compare(extension, ".edr", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
		}

		private CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object runningCase, string casePath)
		{
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				try
				{
					obj = runningCase.GetType().InvokeMember("GetCxsServiceProvider", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, runningCase, null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Failed to retrieve Cxs service provider", inner);
				}
				EdrAdapterV22 edrAdapterV = new EdrAdapterV22(this);
				if (edrAdapterV.AttachToCase(runningCase, obj, casePath))
				{
					return edrAdapterV;
				}
				edrAdapterV.Dispose();
				EdrAdapterV20 edrAdapterV2 = new EdrAdapterV20(this);
				if (edrAdapterV2.AttachToCase(runningCase, obj, casePath))
				{
					return edrAdapterV2;
				}
				edrAdapterV2.Dispose();
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
					obj = null;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string fullCasePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullCasePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
			}
			Application = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text);
			bool flag;
			try
			{
				flag = (bool)Application.GetType().InvokeMember("FileOpen", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, Application, new object[1] { fullCasePath }, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new CxsException("FileOpen failed", inner);
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Failed to open case.");
			}
			return SelectAdapterForCase(Application, fullCasePath);
		}

		public void EDRFileClose()
		{
			bool flag;
			try
			{
				object obj = Application.GetType().InvokeMember("GetATVAppByIndex", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, Application, new object[1] { 0 }, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				object obj2 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("ATVService", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, obj, new object[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				obj2.GetType().InvokeMember("IsDirty", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.SetProperty, null, obj2, new object[1] { false }, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				flag = (bool)Application.GetType().InvokeMember("FileClose", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, Application, new object[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				Application.GetType().InvokeMember("FileExit", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, Application, new object[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new CxsException("EDRFileClose failed", inner);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (Application != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(Application);
					Application = null;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Failed to close EDR case.");
			}
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (versionRequirements != null && versionRequirements.VersionOption != CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion)
			{
				string text = InferProgId(runningCase);
				string text2 = ExactVersionProgId(versionRequirements.VersionString, adapterSettings);
				if (versionRequirements.VersionOption == CxsVersionOption.ExactVersion)
				{
					if (text != text2)
					{
						throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match specific version requirement"));
					}
				}
				else if (ProgIdOrdinal(text) < ProgIdOrdinal(text2))
				{
					throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match minimum version requirement"));
				}
			}
			return SelectAdapterForCase(runningCase, casePath);
		}

		public override string[] GetStandardAttributes()
		{
			return new string[22]
			{
				"id", "value", "dimension", "uom", "can_enter", "option_list", "uom_options", "browser_name", "type", "container",
				"container_type", "container_category", "display_name", "isleaf", "has_children", "path", "upper_limit", "lower_limit", "description", "status",
				"is_container", "no_containers_below"

		{
			return base.GetCommonValuesForAttribute(attribute);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeReadOnly(string attribute)
		{
			if (attribute == "description" || attribute == "status")
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.IsAttributeReadOnly(attribute);
		}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string[] array = FileExtensions();
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = SupportedVersions();
			string text = ApplicationName + " Files";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

			return new string[1] { "ACol" };
		}

			return new string[0];

		private static string InferProgId(object runningCase)
		{
			if (runningCase == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = BaseProgId + "41.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.41.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + "40.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.40.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + "40.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.40.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".39.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.39.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".38.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.38.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".37.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.37.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".36.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.36.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".36.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.36.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".35.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.35.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".34.1";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".34.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId;
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (string.Compare(version, "V15.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.41.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V14.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.40.1";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V14.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.40.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V12.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.39.1";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V12.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.38.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V11.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.37.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V10.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.36.1";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V9.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.36.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V9.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.35.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.8.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".34.1";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.8", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".34.0";
			}
			return BaseProgId;
		}

		{
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID)
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal class EdrAdapterV20 : CxsComCaseAdapterV20
	{
		private bool _emulateVisibleAttribute;

		public EdrAdapterV20(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
			: base(adapterTraits)
		{
		}

		public override bool AttachToCase(object docUnknown, object serviceProvider, string casePath)
		{
			bool result = base.AttachToCase(docUnknown, serviceProvider, casePath);
			if (ApplicationVersion == "21.0")
			{
				_emulateVisibleAttribute = true;
				return result;
			}
			_emulateVisibleAttribute = false;
			return result;
		}

		protected override bool BatchGetPostProcessRequired(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			bool flag = base.BatchGetPostProcessRequired(requests);
			if (!flag && _emulateVisibleAttribute)
			{
				foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
				{
					if (request.Path == "Top" && request.Attribute == "visible")
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected override void BatchGetPostProcess(CxsBatchRequestData requests, object[] values)
		{
			if (_emulateVisibleAttribute)
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
				{
					if (request.Path == "Top" && request.Attribute == "visible")
					{
						bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetAutomationProperty("Visible"));
						values[num] = ((flag.HasValue && flag.Value) ? "true" : "false");
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			base.BatchGetPostProcess(requests, values);
		}

		protected override void BatchSetPreprocess(CxsBatchRequestData requests, List<CxsBatchRequest> expandedRequests)
		{
			base.BatchSetPreprocess(requests, expandedRequests);
			if (!_emulateVisibleAttribute)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest expandedRequest in expandedRequests)
			{
				if (expandedRequest.Path == "Top" && expandedRequest.Attribute == "visible")
				{
					bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(expandedRequest.Value);
					try
					{
						SetAutomationProperty("Visible", flag.HasValue && flag.Value);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					expandedRequests.RemoveAt(num);
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
		}
	}

	{
		public EdrAdapterV22(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
			: base(adapterTraits)
		{
		}
	}
	internal sealed class AspenEnergyAnalyzerTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		public override string ApplicationName => "Aspen Energy Analyzer";

		private static string BaseProgId => "HXNet.Application";

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			return string.Compare(extension, ".hch", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
		}

		private CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object appDispatch, string casePath)
		{
			AspenEnergyAnalyzerAdapterV22 aspenEnergyAnalyzerAdapterV = new AspenEnergyAnalyzerAdapterV22(this);
			if (aspenEnergyAnalyzerAdapterV.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return aspenEnergyAnalyzerAdapterV;
			}
			aspenEnergyAnalyzerAdapterV.Dispose();
			return null;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(casePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("casePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
			}
			CxsComCaseAdapter cxsComCaseAdapter = null;
			object obj = null;
			object obj2 = null;

				{
					throw new CxsException("Unable to access ConceptualDesignCaseStudies property", inner);

					throw new CxsException("Unable to access simulation cases property");

					BindingFlags invokeAttr2 = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding;

				catch (Exception inner2)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Error opening case", inner2);

					throw new CxsException("Failed to open case");

				}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{

			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options != null && options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

		protected override int ProgIdOrdinal(string progId)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".1.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 1;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(progId, BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.1.0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				result = 1;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static string InferProgId(object runningCase)
		{
			if (runningCase == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = BaseProgId + ".1.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = BaseProgId + ".NewInstance.1.0";
			if (ComHelpers.IPersistMatchesProgId(runningCase, text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string baseProgId = BaseProgId;
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V8.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V8.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V8.4", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V8.6", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V8.8", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V9.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V10.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V11.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V12.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V12.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V14.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0 || string.Compare(version, "V15.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return baseProgId + ".1.0";
			}
			return BaseProgId + "." + version;
		}

		protected override string GetLatestInstalledProgID(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID)
		{
			return true;
		}

			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				result = BaseProgIdV22 + ".1";

		}

	public abstract class AspenModelerAdapterTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		protected abstract string BaseProgId { get; }

		protected virtual CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object runningCase, string casePath)
		{
			AspenModelerAdapterV22 aspenModelerAdapterV = new AspenModelerAdapterV22(this);
			if (aspenModelerAdapterV.AttachToCase(runningCase, casePath))
			{
				return aspenModelerAdapterV;
			}
			aspenModelerAdapterV.Dispose();
			AspenModelerAdapterV20 aspenModelerAdapterV2 = new AspenModelerAdapterV20(this);
			if (aspenModelerAdapterV2.AttachToCase(runningCase, casePath))
			{
				return aspenModelerAdapterV2;
			}
			aspenModelerAdapterV2.Dispose();
			return null;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string fullCasePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullCasePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			CxsComCaseAdapter cxsComCaseAdapter = null;
			object obj = null;
			object obj2 = null;
			try
			{
				string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
				}
				obj = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text);
				bool flag = false;
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.GetProperty;
					object obj3 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("Visible", invokeAttr, null, obj, null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					if (obj3 != null && (bool)obj3)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
					obj = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text);
				}
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr2 = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding;
					object[] args = new object[1] { fullCasePath };
					obj2 = obj.GetType().InvokeMember("OpenDocument", invokeAttr2, null, obj, args, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Error opening case", inner);
				}
				return SelectAdapterForCase(obj2, fullCasePath);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
					obj = null;
				}
				if (obj2 != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj2);
					obj2 = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (versionRequirements != null && versionRequirements.VersionOption != CxsVersionOption.AnyVersion)
			{
				string text = InferProgId(runningCase);
				string text2 = ExactVersionProgId(versionRequirements.VersionString, adapterSettings);
				if (versionRequirements.VersionOption == CxsVersionOption.ExactVersion)
				{
					if (text != text2)
					{
						throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match specific version requirement"));
					}
				}
				else if (ProgIdOrdinal(text) < ProgIdOrdinal(text2))
				{
					throw new CxsVersionMismatchException(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Running case does not match minimum version requirement"));
				}
			}
			return SelectAdapterForCase(runningCase, casePath);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeReadOnly(string attribute)

			{
			case "description":
			case "status":

			}
		}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string[] array = FileExtensions();
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = SupportedVersions();
			string text = ApplicationName + " Files";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

		protected override string ExactVersionProgId(string version, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (string.Compare(version, "V15.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 4100";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V14.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 4000";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V12.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3900";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V12.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3800";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V11.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3700";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V10.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3600";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V9.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3500";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.8", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3400";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.6", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3200";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.4", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 3000";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2900";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2800";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V8.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2700";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2600";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2500";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2400";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2300";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "V7.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2200";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2006.5", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2100";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2006", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2000";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2006.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 2000";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2004.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 1320";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2004.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 1320";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(version, "2004.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				text = BaseProgId + " 1310";
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (string installedACMVersion in GetInstalledACMVersions())
				{
					if (string.Compare(version, VersionFromACMProgID(installedACMVersion), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						text = installedACMVersion;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					text = BaseProgId + "." + version;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected override string GetLatestInstalledProgID(Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			List<string> list = (List<string>)GetInstalledACMVersions();
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				result = list[0];
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetInstalledACMVersions()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				using (RegistryKey registryKey = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry32))
				{
					using RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aspen Custom Modeler");
					if (registryKey2 != null)
					{
						string[] subKeyNames = registryKey2.GetSubKeyNames();
						foreach (string text in subKeyNames)
						{
							if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "^\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
							{
								string text2 = "ACM Application " + text.Replace(".0", "00");
								if (Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(text2) != null)
								{
									list.Add(text2);
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				using (RegistryKey registryKey3 = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry64))
				{
					using RegistryKey registryKey4 = registryKey3.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aspen Custom Modeler");
					if (registryKey4 != null)
					{
						string[] subKeyNames = registryKey4.GetSubKeyNames();
						foreach (string text3 in subKeyNames)
						{
							if (Regex.IsMatch(text3, "^\\d{2}\\.\\d$"))
							{
								string text4 = "ACM Application " + text3.Replace(".0", "00");
								if (Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey(text4) != null)
								{
									list.Add(text4);
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				list.Sort();
				list.Reverse();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static string VersionFromACMProgID(string ProgID)
		{
			string text = "SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aspen Custom Modeler";
			try
			{
				if (Regex.IsMatch(ProgID, "^ACM Application \\d{4}$"))
				{
					ProgID = ProgID.Replace("ACM Application ", "");
					ProgID = ProgID.TrimEnd('0').PadRight(3, '0');
					ProgID = ProgID.Insert(2, ".");
					using RegistryKey registryKey = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry32);
					using RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.OpenSubKey(text);
					string[] subKeyNames = registryKey2.GetSubKeyNames();
					foreach (string text2 in subKeyNames)
					{
						if (!(text2 == ProgID))
						{
							continue;
						}
						using RegistryKey registryKey3 = registryKey.OpenSubKey(text + "\\" + text2 + "\\Dir");
						string[] array = registryKey3.GetValue("Root").ToString().Split(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
						foreach (string text3 in array)
						{
							if (Regex.IsMatch(text3, "^Aspen Custom Modeler V\\d{1,2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$"))
							{
								return text3.Replace("Aspen Custom Modeler ", "");
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override bool IsMinReqSatisfied(string minimumVersion, string LatestProgID)
		{
			if (Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{1,2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$"))
			{
				LatestProgID = VersionFromACMProgID(LatestProgID);
				LatestProgID = ((!Regex.IsMatch(LatestProgID, "^V\\d{2}\\.\\d(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(LatestProgID, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				minimumVersion = ((!Regex.IsMatch(minimumVersion, "^[Vv]?\\d{2}(\\.\\d)?(\\.\\d)?$")) ? Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(3, '0') : Regex.Replace(minimumVersion, "[^0-9]", "").PadRight(4, '0'));
				int.TryParse(minimumVersion, out var result);
				int.TryParse(LatestProgID, out var result2);
				if (result > result2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	public class AcmAdapterTraits : AspenModelerAdapterTraits
	{
		public override string ApplicationName => "Aspen Custom Modeler";

		protected override string BaseProgId => "ACM Application";

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			if (string.Compare(extension, ".acmf", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return string.Compare(extension, ".acmd", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override string[] FileExtensions()
		{
			return new string[2] { ".acmf", ".acmd" };
		}
	}

	public class OomfAdapterTraits : CxsComCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		public override string ApplicationName => "OOMF";

		public static string BaseProgId => "ASCSimServer.ASCSimServer";

		public override bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			return string.Compare(extension, ".ebs", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
		}

		private CxsComCaseAdapter SelectAdapterForCase(object appDispatch, string casePath)
		{
			OomfAdapterV22 oomfAdapterV = new OomfAdapterV22(this);
			if (oomfAdapterV.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return oomfAdapterV;
			}
			oomfAdapterV.Dispose();
			OomfAdapterV20 oomfAdapterV2 = new OomfAdapterV20(this);
			if (oomfAdapterV2.AttachToCase(appDispatch, casePath))
			{
				return oomfAdapterV2;
			}
			oomfAdapterV2.Dispose();
			return null;
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string fullCasePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullCasePath");
			}
			if (versionRequirements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("versionRequirements");
			}
			string text = VersionSpecificProgId(versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new CxsVersionMismatchException("No installed version matched the version requirements");
			}
			CxsComCaseAdapter cxsComCaseAdapter = null;
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				obj = ComHelpers.CreateObjectFromProgid(text);
				try
				{
					BindingFlags invokeAttr = BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.InvokeMethod | BindingFlags.OptionalParamBinding;
					object[] args = new object[1] { fullCasePath };
					obj.GetType().InvokeMember("LoadSimulation", invokeAttr, null, obj, args, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Error opening case", inner);
				}
				cxsComCaseAdapter = SelectAdapterForCase(obj, fullCasePath);
				if (cxsComCaseAdapter == null)
				{
					throw new CxsException("Could not construct adapter for case");
				}
				return cxsComCaseAdapter;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (obj != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
					obj = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public override ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			_ = versionRequirements?.VersionOption;
			return SelectAdapterForCase(runningCase, casePath);
		}

		public override string[] GetStandardAttributes()
		{
			return new string[22]
			{
				"id", "value", "dimension", "uom", "can_enter", "option_list", "uom_options", "browser_name", "type", "container",
				"container_type", "container_category", "display_name", "isleaf", "has_children", "path", "upper_limit", "lower_limit", "description", "status",
				"is_container", "no_containers_below"
			};
		}

		public override string[] GetCommonValuesForAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			return base.GetCommonValuesForAttribute(attribute);
		}

		public override bool IsAttributeReadOnly(string attribute)
		{
			if (attribute == "description" || attribute == "status")
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.IsAttributeReadOnly(attribute);
		}

		public override CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			string[] array = FileExtensions();
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = SupportedVersions();
			string text = ApplicationName + " Files";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = ((i != 0) ? (text + ";") : (text + "|"));
				text = text + "*" + array[i];
			}
			string[] installedVersions = null;
			if (options.CheckInstalledVersions)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (IsVersionInstalled(item, adapterSettings))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				installedVersions = list.ToArray();
			}
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, array, text, installedVersions);
		}

		protected override string[] GetCommonValuesForContainerCategory()
		{
			return new string[0];
		}

		protected override string[] GetCommonValuesForContainerType()
		{
			return new string[0];
		}

		private static string[] FileExtensions()
		{
			return new string[1] { ".ebs" };
		}

				result = 7;

			{
				result = 8;
			}

			{
				result = 9;

			{
				result = 10;

			{

		{

			{
				return BaseProgId + ".41.0";

			if (string.Compare(version, "V14.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)

			{
				return BaseProgId + ".39.0";

				return BaseProgId + ".38.0";

			if (string.Compare(version, "V11.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".37.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V10.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".36.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V9.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".35.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.8", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".34.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.6", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".32.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.4", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".30.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".29.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".28.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V8.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".27.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".26.0";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V7.3", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{

			if (string.Compare(version, "V7.2", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)

				return BaseProgId + ".2009";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V7.1", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".2008";
			}
			if (string.Compare(version, "V7.0", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				return BaseProgId + ".2007";
			}
			return BaseProgId + "." + version;
		}

namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs.AdapterSdk
{
	internal enum BuiltInAdapterId
	{
		Hysys,
		AspenPlus,
		Acm,
		AspenDynamics,
		Adsim,
		Chrom,
		BatchSep,
		Amr,
		Edr,
		Icarus,
		Oomf,
		AspenProperties,
		Last
	}
	internal static class CxsAdapterServices
	{
		public static ICxsCaseAdapterTraits SelectAdapterTraits(string applicationRequirement, string fileExtension)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(applicationRequirement) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(fileExtension))
			{
				return new CxsCaseAdapterTraits();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
			{
				ICxsCaseAdapterTraits builtInAdapterTraits = GetBuiltInAdapterTraits((BuiltInAdapterId)i);
				if (builtInAdapterTraits != null)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(applicationRequirement) && string.Compare(applicationRequirement, builtInAdapterTraits.ApplicationName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						return builtInAdapterTraits;
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fileExtension) && builtInAdapterTraits.IsValidExtension(fileExtension))
					{
						return builtInAdapterTraits;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static CxsAppInfo[] GetAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (adapterSettings == null)
			{
				adapterSettings = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			}
			List<CxsAppInfo> list = new List<CxsAppInfo>();
			GetAppInfoFromBuiltInTraits(options, list, adapterSettings);
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		private static void GetAppInfoFromBuiltInTraits(CxsAppInfoOptions options, List<CxsAppInfo> appInfoList, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
			{
				ICxsCaseAdapterTraits builtInAdapterTraits = GetBuiltInAdapterTraits((BuiltInAdapterId)i);
				if (builtInAdapterTraits == null || (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.ApplicationName) && string.Compare(options.ApplicationName, builtInAdapterTraits.ApplicationName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0))
				{
					continue;
				}
				CxsAppInfo cxsAppInfo = builtInAdapterTraits.ConstructAppInfo(options, adapterSettings);
				if (cxsAppInfo != null)
				{
					appInfoList.Add(cxsAppInfo);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.ApplicationName))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static ICxsCaseAdapterTraits GetBuiltInAdapterTraits(BuiltInAdapterId adapterId)
		{
			ICxsCaseAdapterTraits result = null;
			switch (adapterId)
			{
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Hysys:
				result = new HysysAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.AspenPlus:
				result = new AspenPlusAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Acm:
				result = new AcmAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.AspenDynamics:
				result = new AspenDynamicsAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Adsim:
				result = new AdsimAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Chrom:
				result = new ChromAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.BatchSep:
				result = new BatchSepAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Amr:
				result = new AmrAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Edr:
				result = new EdrAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Icarus:
				result = new IcarusAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.Oomf:
				result = new OomfAdapterTraits();
				break;
			case BuiltInAdapterId.AspenProperties:
				result = new AspenPropertiesAdapterTraits();
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class CxsAdapterSetting
	{
		public string Name { get; set; }

		public object Value { get; set; }
	}
	public class CxsCaseAdapter : MarshalByRefObject, ICxsCaseAdapter, IDisposable
	{
		private class KillThreadData
		{
			public CxsCaseAdapter Adapter { get; set; }

			public int Timeout { get; set; }

			public bool Result { get; set; }
		}

		private bool _IsDisposed;

		public bool IsDisposing { get; private set; }

		public CxsCaseAdapterTraits AdapterTraits { get; private set; }

		public virtual bool IsRemote => false;

		public bool DetachOnClose { get; set; }

		public bool IsDisposed => _IsDisposed;

		public string WorkingDirectory { get; set; }

		public string CasePath => FullCasePath;

		public string FullCasePath { get; set; }

		public virtual bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsDisposed)
				{
					return false;
				}
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(BatchGet("Top", "visible"));
				if (!flag.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return flag.Value;
			}
			set
			{
				BatchSet("Top", "visible", value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsClosed { get; private set; }

		protected virtual bool IsBound => false;

		protected virtual bool CanKillCase => false;

		public virtual string ApplicationVersion => string.Empty;

		public virtual bool CanSaveCopyAs => false;

		public CxsCaseAdapter(CxsCaseAdapterTraits adapterTraits)
		{
			AdapterTraits = adapterTraits;
			IsClosed = false;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			IsDisposing = true;
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			IsDisposing = false;
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				CloseCase(0);
			}
			_IsDisposed = true;
		}

		public bool CloseCase(int timeoutBeforeKill)
		{
			if (IsClosed)
			{
				return true;
			}
			Thread thread = null;
			KillThreadData killThreadData = null;
			if (timeoutBeforeKill > 0 && CanKillCase && !DetachOnClose && !IsDisposing)
			{
				thread = new Thread(ThreadWaitOrKillCase);
				killThreadData = new KillThreadData
				{
					Adapter = this,
					Timeout = timeoutBeforeKill
				};
				thread.Start(killThreadData);
			}
			if (IsBound && !DetachOnClose)
			{
				try
				{
					Visible = false;
				}
				catch (Exception arg)
				{
					Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Ignoring exception during dispose = {0}", arg));
				}
			}
			try
			{
				Shutdown();
			}
			finally
			{
				IsClosed = true;
			}
			thread?.Join();
			return killThreadData?.Result ?? true;
		}

		private static void ThreadWaitOrKillCase(object data)
		{
			KillThreadData killThreadData = data as KillThreadData;
			try
			{
				killThreadData.Result = killThreadData.Adapter.WaitForExitOrKillCase(killThreadData.Timeout);
			}
			catch (ThreadAbortException)
			{
			}
		}

		protected virtual void Shutdown()
		{
			CleanupWorkingDirectory();
		}

		public virtual object[] BatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual void BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
		}

		public virtual object InvokeAction(string nodePath, string action, params object[] args)
		{
			throw new CxsException("Unsupported action");
		}

		protected virtual bool WaitForExitOrKillCase(int maxWaitTime)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public virtual void KillCase()
		{
		}

		public virtual string[] ConvertMonikersToPaths(string[] monikers)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual object GetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, params object[] args)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual void SetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, object value, params object[] args)
		{
		}

		public virtual object[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, object[] inputs)
		{
			return null;
		}

		}

		public object BatchGet(string path, string attribute)
		{
			return BatchGet(path, attribute, string.Empty);
		}

		public object BatchGet(string path, string attribute, string unit)
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, attribute, unit);
			object[] array = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return array[0];
		}

		{
			BatchSet(path, attribute, string.Empty, value);
		}

		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, attribute, unit, value);
			BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
		}

		}

		public virtual byte[] SaveAndCopyToByteArray()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FullCasePath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			Save();
			byte[] array = null;
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(FullCasePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
			try
			{
				int num = (int)fileStream.Length;
				array = new byte[num];
				fileStream.Read(array, 0, num);
				return array;
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream.Close();
			}
		}

		{
			CxsCaseAdapterTraits.CleanupWorkingDirectory(WorkingDirectory);
			WorkingDirectory = string.Empty;
		}

	public class CxsCaseAdapterTraits : ICxsCaseAdapterTraits, ICxsCaseAdapterNodeTraits
	{
		private static List<string> _ReadOnlyAttributes;

		private static List<string> _ConstantAttributes;

		private static List<string> _AttributesToExcludeFromNodeProperties;

		private static List<string> _AttributesToExcludeFromCache;

		public virtual string ApplicationName => string.Empty;

		static CxsCaseAdapterTraits()
		{
			_ReadOnlyAttributes = new List<string>();
			string[] array = new string[30]
			{
				"id", "dimension", "description", "can_enter", "option_list", "uom_options", "browser_name", "type", "container", "container_type",
				"container_category", "display_name", "isleaf", "has_children", "is_container", "no_containers_below", "source_block", "source_port", "destination_block", "destination_port",
				"valid_attributes", "valid_actions", "child_ids", "action_description", "action_is_valid", "action_status", "source_block", "source_port", "destination_block", "destination_port"
			};
			foreach (string item in array)
			{
				int num = _ReadOnlyAttributes.BinarySearch(item);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					_ReadOnlyAttributes.Insert(~num, item);
				}
			}
			_ConstantAttributes = new List<string>();
			array = new string[18]
			{
				"id", "dimension", "description", "option_list", "uom_options", "browser_name", "type", "container", "container_type", "container_category",
				"display_name", "isleaf", "is_container", "no_containers_below", "source_block", "source_port", "destination_block", "destination_port"
			};
			foreach (string item2 in array)
			{
				int num2 = _ConstantAttributes.BinarySearch(item2);
				if (num2 < 0)
				{
					_ConstantAttributes.Insert(~num2, item2);
				}
			}
			_AttributesToExcludeFromNodeProperties = new List<string>();
			array = new string[6] { "child_ids", "action_description", "action_is_valid", "action_status", "valid_actions", "valid_attributes" };
			foreach (string item3 in array)
			{
				int num3 = _AttributesToExcludeFromNodeProperties.BinarySearch(item3);
				if (num3 < 0)
				{
					_AttributesToExcludeFromNodeProperties.Insert(~num3, item3);
				}
			}
			_AttributesToExcludeFromCache = new List<string>();
			array = new string[6] { "child_ids", "action_description", "action_is_valid", "action_status", "valid_actions", "valid_attributes" };
			foreach (string item4 in array)
			{
				int num4 = _AttributesToExcludeFromCache.BinarySearch(item4);
				if (num4 < 0)
				{
					_AttributesToExcludeFromCache.Insert(~num4, item4);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterFromByteArray(string caseFilePath, byte[] caseData, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(caseFilePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("caseFilePath");
			}
			if (caseData == null || caseData.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("caseData");
			}
			string fileName = Path.GetFileName(caseFilePath);
			string text = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData);
			if (IntPtr.Size == 8)
			{
				string text2 = "system32";
				string value = "syswow64";
				int num = text.ToLower().IndexOf(text2);
				if (num > -1)
				{
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
					stringBuilder.Append(text.Substring(0, num));
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
					if (Directory.Exists(stringBuilder.ToString()))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(text.Substring(num + text2.Length));
						text = stringBuilder.ToString();
					}
				}
			}
			text = Path.Combine(text, "AspenTech");
			text = Path.Combine(text, "AspenCxs");
			text = Path.Combine(text, Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(fileName) + " Files");
			string text3 = text;
			if (Directory.Exists(text3))
			{
				NumberFormatInfo invariantInfo = NumberFormatInfo.InvariantInfo;
				int num2 = 1;
				while (true)
				{
					text3 = text + "(" + num2.ToString(invariantInfo) + ")";
					if (!Directory.Exists(text3))
					{
						break;
					}
					num2++;
				}
			}
			Directory.CreateDirectory(text3);
			string text4 = text3;
			string text5 = Path.Combine(text4, fileName);
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(text5, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
			try
			{
				fileStream.Write(caseData, 0, caseData.Length);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream.Close();
			}
			ICxsCaseAdapter cxsCaseAdapter = null;
			try
			{
				cxsCaseAdapter = CreateAdapterForCase(text5, versionRequirements, adapterSettings);
				if (cxsCaseAdapter != null)
				{
					cxsCaseAdapter.WorkingDirectory = text4;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (cxsCaseAdapter != null)
				{
					cxsCaseAdapter.Dispose();
					cxsCaseAdapter = null;
				}
				CleanupWorkingDirectory(text4);
				throw;
			}
			return cxsCaseAdapter;
		}

		public virtual bool IsAttributeReadOnly(string attribute)
		{
			return _ReadOnlyAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
		}

		public virtual bool ShouldExcludeAttributeFromCache(string attribute)
		{
			return _AttributesToExcludeFromCache.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
		}

		public virtual string[] GetCommonValuesForAttribute(string attribute)
		{
			switch (attribute)
			{
			case "type":
			{
				string[] commonValuesForContainerType = GetCommonValuesForContainerType();
				int num = 5;
				string[] array = new string[((commonValuesForContainerType != null) ? commonValuesForContainerType.Length : 0) + num];
				array[0] = "Integer";
				array[1] = "Real";
				array[2] = "Text";
				array[3] = "Array";
				array[4] = "EnumBoolean";
				commonValuesForContainerType?.CopyTo(array, num);
				return array;
			}
			case "container_category":
				return GetCommonValuesForContainerCategory();
			case "container_type":
				return GetCommonValuesForContainerType();
			case "can_enter":
			case "isleaf":
			case "is_container":
			case "no_container_below":
				return new string[2] { "0", "1" };
			default:
				return null;
			}
		}

		protected virtual string[] GetCommonValuesForContainerType()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual string[] GetCommonValuesForContainerCategory()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected static string ConvertAttributeNameToPropertyName(string attribute)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(attribute))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = true;
			char[] array = attribute.ToCharArray();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = array[i];
				if (c == '_')
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c.ToString().ToUpperInvariant());
					flag = false;
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		internal static void CleanupWorkingDirectory(string directory)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(directory))
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				try
				{
					Directory.Delete(directory, recursive: true);
				}
				catch (DirectoryNotFoundException)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				catch (IOException)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					Thread.CurrentThread.Join(500);
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool IsAttributeConstant(string attribute)
		{
			return _ConstantAttributes.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0;
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptor CreateAttributePropertyDescriptor(CxsNode node, string attribute)
		{
			if (_AttributesToExcludeFromNodeProperties.BinarySearch(attribute) >= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits cxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits = new CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits(ConvertAttributeNameToPropertyName(attribute), attribute);
			if (IsAttributeConstant(attribute))
			{
				cxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits.IsReadOnly = true;
			}
			return cxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits.CreatePropertyDescriptor();
		}

		public virtual CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			return new CxsAppInfo(ApplicationName, new string[0], string.Empty, new string[0]);
		}

		public virtual string[] GetStandardAttributes()
		{
			return new string[26]
			{
				"id", "value", "uom", "dimension", "description", "can_enter", "option_list", "uom_options", "browser_name", "type",
				"container", "container_type", "container_category", "display_name", "isleaf", "has_children", "is_container", "no_containers_below", "source_block", "source_port",
				"destination_block", "destination_port", "path", "upper_limit", "lower_limit", "status"
			};
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptorCollection GetCommonNodeProperties()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(typeof(CxsNode));
		}

		public virtual bool IsNodeValueReadOnly(CxsNode node)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = false;
			string status = node.Status;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(status))
			{
				if (status == "Calculated")
				{
					result = true;
				}
				else if (status == "Fixed")
				{
					result = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual bool IsValidExtension(string extension)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public virtual ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string fullCasePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public interface ICxsCaseAdapter : IDisposable
	{
		bool IsRemote { get; }

		string ApplicationVersion { get; }

		string WorkingDirectory { get; set; }

		string CasePath { get; }

		bool IsClosed { get; }

		bool DetachOnClose { get; set; }

		bool CanSaveCopyAs { get; }

		bool CloseCase(int timeoutBeforeKill);

		object InvokeAction(string nodePath, string action, params object[] args);

		object[] BatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests);

		void BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests);

		void KillCase();

		void Save();

		void SaveAs(string filePath);

		void SaveCopyAs(string filePath);

		byte[] SaveAndCopyToByteArray();

		string[] ConvertMonikersToPaths(string[] monikers);

		CxsHistoryData GetHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double startTime, double endTime, double interval);

		CxsHistoryData GetMostRecentHistory(string[] paths, string[] units, string timeUnits, double interval, int numberOfTimePoints);

		object GetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, params object[] args);

		void SetAutomationProperty(string propertyName, object value, params object[] args);

		object[] ExecuteVBScript(string script, object[] inputs);

		void ResetMessageFilters(CxsMessageFilterSpec[] filters);

		void ConnectMessageSink(CxsCaseMessageSink messageSink);

		void DisconnectMessageSink();
	}
	public interface ICxsCaseAdapterTraits
	{
		string ApplicationName { get; }

		bool IsValidExtension(string extension);

		CxsAppInfo ConstructAppInfo(CxsAppInfoOptions options, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings);

		ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForCase(string fullCasePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings);

		ICxsCaseAdapter CreateAdapterForRunningCase(object runningCase, string casePath, CxsVersionRequirements versionRequirements, Dictionary<string, object> adapterSettings);
