	}
	public class CxsCaseMessageSink : MarshalByRefObject
	{
		public CxsCase Case { get; set; }

		public CxsCaseMessageSink(CxsCase cxsCase)
		{
			Case = cxsCase;
		}

		public void OnMessage(CxsMessageEventArgs args)
		{
			if (Case != null)
			{
				Case.RaiseMessage(args);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits
	{
		private string _PropertyName;

		private string _AttributeName;

		private string _Category;

		private string _Description;

		private string _EditorTypeName;

		private string _ConverterTypeName;

		private bool _IsReadOnly;

		private bool _IsBrowsable;

		private Type _PropertyType;

		private object _DefaultValue;

		public string PropertyName
		{
			get
			{
				return _PropertyName;
			}
			set
			{
				_PropertyName = value;
			}
		}

		public string AttributeName
		{
			get
			{
				return _AttributeName;
			}
			set
			{
				_AttributeName = value;
			}
		}

		public string Category
		{
			get
			{
				return _Category;
			}
			set
			{
				_Category = value;
			}
		}

		public string Description
		{
			get
			{
				return _Description;
			}
			set
			{
				_Description = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsReadOnly;
			}
			set
			{
				_IsReadOnly = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsBrowsable
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsBrowsable;
			}
			set
			{
				_IsBrowsable = value;
			}
		}

		public Type PropertyType
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(_PropertyType != null))
				{
					return typeof(object);
				}
				return _PropertyType;
			}
			set
			{
				_PropertyType = value;
			}
		}

		public string EditorTypeName
		{
			get
			{
				return _EditorTypeName;
			}
			set
			{
				_EditorTypeName = value;
			}
		}

		public string ConverterTypeName
		{
			get
			{
				return _ConverterTypeName;
			}
			set
			{
				_ConverterTypeName = value;
			}
		}

		public object DefaultValue
		{
			get
			{
				return _DefaultValue;
			}
			set
			{
				_DefaultValue = value;
			}
		}

		public CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits(string propertyName)
		{
			_PropertyName = propertyName;
			_IsReadOnly = true;
			_IsBrowsable = true;
		}

		public CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits(string propertyName, string attribute)
		{
			_PropertyName = propertyName;
			_IsReadOnly = true;
			_IsBrowsable = true;
			_AttributeName = attribute;
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptor CreatePropertyDescriptor()
		{
			List<Attribute> list = new List<Attribute>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Category))
			{
				list.Add(new CategoryAttribute(Category));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Description))
			{
				list.Add(new DescriptionAttribute(Description));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(EditorTypeName))
			{
				list.Add(new EditorAttribute(EditorTypeName, typeof(UITypeEditor)));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ConverterTypeName))
			{
				list.Add(new TypeConverterAttribute(ConverterTypeName));
			}
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				list.Add(new ReadOnlyAttribute(isReadOnly: true));
			}
			if (!IsBrowsable)
			{
				list.Add(new BrowsableAttribute(browsable: false));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(AttributeName))
			{
				list.Add(new CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute(AttributeName));
			}
			return new CxsNodeCustomPropertyDescriptor(this, list.ToArray());
		}

		public virtual bool CanResetValue(CxsNode node)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_DefaultValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			object value = GetValue(node);
			if (value == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return !value.Equals(_DefaultValue);
		}

		public virtual object GetValue(CxsNode node)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_AttributeName))
			{
				return null;
			}
			object attribute = node.GetAttribute(_AttributeName);
			return ConvertAttributeValueToPropertyValue(attribute);
		}

		public virtual void SetValue(CxsNode node, object value)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object not set");
			}
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Property is read only");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_AttributeName))
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot modify property as it is not mapped to a Cxs attribute");
			}
			object value2 = ConvertPropertyValueToAttributeValue(value);
			node.SetAttribute(_AttributeName, value2);
		}

		public virtual void ResetValue(CxsNode node)
		{
			if (_DefaultValue != null && !IsReadOnly)
			{
				SetValue(node, _DefaultValue);
			}
		}

		public virtual bool ShouldSerializeValue(CxsNode node)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_DefaultValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			object value = GetValue(node);
			if (value == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return !value.Equals(_DefaultValue);
		}

		protected virtual object ConvertAttributeValueToPropertyValue(object value)
		{
			if (_PropertyType == typeof(bool) && value != null)
			{
				switch (Type.GetTypeCode(value.GetType()))
				{
				case TypeCode.Int16:
				case TypeCode.Int32:
					value = (int)value != 0;
					break;
				case TypeCode.String:
					if (string.Compare((string)value, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						value = false;
					}
					else if (string.Compare((string)value, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						value = true;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected virtual object ConvertPropertyValueToAttributeValue(object value)
		{
			if (_PropertyType == typeof(bool) && value != null)
			{
				if (value.GetType() == typeof(bool))
				{
					value = (((bool)value) ? 1 : 0);
				}
				else if (value.GetType() == typeof(string))
				{
					if (string.Compare((string)value, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						value = 0;
					}
					else if (string.Compare((string)value, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						value = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			return value;
		}
	}
	public class CxsNodeCustomPropertyDescriptor : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		private CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits _Traits;

		public override Type ComponentType => typeof(CxsNode);

		public override bool IsReadOnly => _Traits.IsReadOnly;

		public override Type PropertyType => _Traits.PropertyType;

		public CxsNodeCustomPropertyDescriptor(CxsNodeCustomPropertyTraits traits, Attribute[] attributes)
			: base((traits != null) ? traits.PropertyName : string.Empty, attributes)
		{
			_Traits = traits;
			if (traits == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("traits");
			}
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			if (!(component is CxsNode node))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return _Traits.CanResetValue(node);
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			if (component == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object not set");
			}
			if (!(component is CxsNode cxsNode))
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object is not a CxsNode");
			}
			if (cxsNode == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _Traits.GetValue(cxsNode);
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
			if (component == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object not set");
			}
			if (!(component is CxsNode node))
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object is not a CxsNode");
			}
			_Traits.SetValue(node, value);
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
			if (component == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object not set");
			}
			if (!(component is CxsNode node))
			{
				throw new CxsException("Cannot change property object is not a CxsNode");
			}
			_Traits.ResetValue(node);
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			if (!(component is CxsNode node))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return _Traits.ShouldSerializeValue(node);
		}
	}
	public class CxsProcessMonitor : MarshalByRefObject, IDisposable
	{
		private Process _MonitoredProcess;

		private EventHandler _StoppedRespondingHandlers;

		private EventHandler _ProcessExitedHandlers;

		private System.Timers.Timer _EventTimer;

		private DateTime _NotRespondingEventTimestamp;

		public Process MonitoredProcess => _MonitoredProcess;

		public bool HasExited
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = true;
				if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.Refresh();
					result = _MonitoredProcess.HasExited;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public bool HasStoppedResponding
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = true;
				if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
				{
					if (_StoppedRespondingHandlers != null)
					{
						result = _NotRespondingEventTimestamp != DateTime.MinValue;
					}
					else
					{
						_MonitoredProcess.Refresh();
						result = !_MonitoredProcess.Responding;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public double StoppedRespondingTimerInterval
		{
			get
			{
				return _EventTimer.Interval;
			}
			set
			{
				_EventTimer.Interval = value;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler StoppedResponding
		{
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			add
			{
				if (_StoppedRespondingHandlers == null && _MonitoredProcess != null)
				{
					_EventTimer.Start();
				}
				_StoppedRespondingHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(_StoppedRespondingHandlers, value);
			}
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			remove
			{
				_StoppedRespondingHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(_StoppedRespondingHandlers, value);
				if (_StoppedRespondingHandlers == null)
				{
					_EventTimer.Stop();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler Exited
		{
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			add
			{
				if (_ProcessExitedHandlers == null && _MonitoredProcess != null)
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
					_MonitoredProcess.Exited += WatchedProcess_Exited;
				}
				_ProcessExitedHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(_ProcessExitedHandlers, value);
			}
			[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
			remove
			{
				_ProcessExitedHandlers = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(_ProcessExitedHandlers, value);
				if (_ProcessExitedHandlers == null)
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.Exited -= WatchedProcess_Exited;
					_MonitoredProcess.EnableRaisingEvents = false;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void NotifyStoppedResponding()
		{
			if (_StoppedRespondingHandlers != null)
			{
				_StoppedRespondingHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		internal void NotifyExited()
		{
			if (_ProcessExitedHandlers != null)
			{
				_ProcessExitedHandlers(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void WatchedProcess_Exited(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_MonitoredProcess.Exited -= WatchedProcess_Exited;
			_EventTimer.Stop();
			_MonitoredProcess = null;
			NotifyExited();
		}

		private void EventTimer_Elapsed(object sender, ElapsedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_NotRespondingEventTimestamp != DateTime.MinValue)
			{
				if ((DateTime.Now - _NotRespondingEventTimestamp).Minutes < 5)
				{
					return;
				}
				_NotRespondingEventTimestamp = DateTime.MinValue;
			}
			_MonitoredProcess.Refresh();
			if (_MonitoredProcess.Responding)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					break;
				}
				Thread.CurrentThread.Join(1000);
				if (_MonitoredProcess == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				_MonitoredProcess.Refresh();
				flag = !_MonitoredProcess.Responding;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_NotRespondingEventTimestamp = DateTime.Now;
				NotifyStoppedResponding();
			}
		}

		public CxsProcessMonitor()
		{
			_EventTimer = new System.Timers.Timer();
			_NotRespondingEventTimestamp = DateTime.MinValue;
			_EventTimer.Interval = 10000.0;
			_EventTimer.Elapsed += EventTimer_Elapsed;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (_EventTimer != null)
				{
					_EventTimer.Stop();
					_EventTimer.Elapsed -= EventTimer_Elapsed;
					_EventTimer.Dispose();
				}
				if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.Exited -= WatchedProcess_Exited;
					_MonitoredProcess.Close();
				}
			}
			_MonitoredProcess = null;
			_StoppedRespondingHandlers = null;
			_ProcessExitedHandlers = null;
			_EventTimer = null;
		}

		public void ResetHasStoppedResponding()
		{
			_NotRespondingEventTimestamp = DateTime.MinValue;
		}

		public bool WaitForExit(int maxWaitTime)
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			int num = 100;
			bool hasExited = HasExited;
			int num2 = 0;
			while (!hasExited && num2 < maxWaitTime)
			{
				Thread.CurrentThread.Join(num);
				hasExited = HasExited;
				num2 += num;
			}
			return hasExited;
		}

		public bool WaitForExitOrKill(int maxWaitTime)
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool num = WaitForExit(maxWaitTime);
			if (!num)
			{
				KillProcess();
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void CloseMainWindow()
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
			{
				try
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.Refresh();
					if (!_MonitoredProcess.HasExited)
					{
						_MonitoredProcess.CloseMainWindow();
					}
				}
				catch (Win32Exception)
				{
				}
				catch (NotSupportedException)
				{
				}
				catch (InvalidOperationException)
				{
				}
			}
			if (HasExited)
			{
				StopMonitoring();
			}
		}

		public void KillProcess()
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
			{
				try
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.Refresh();
					if (!_MonitoredProcess.HasExited)
					{
						_MonitoredProcess.Kill();
					}
				}
				catch (Win32Exception)
				{
				}
				catch (NotSupportedException)
				{
				}
				catch (InvalidOperationException)
				{
				}
			}
			if (HasExited)
			{
				StopMonitoring();
			}
		}

		public bool QuitProcess(int maxWaitTime)
		{
			CloseMainWindow();
			return WaitForExitOrKill(maxWaitTime);
		}

		public bool WaitForExitOrQuit(int maxWaitTimeToQuit, int maxWaitTimeToKill)
		{
			bool num = WaitForExit(maxWaitTimeToQuit);
			if (!num)
			{
				QuitProcess(maxWaitTimeToKill);
			}
			return num;
		}

		private static void InternalGetProcessIdsByName(string processName, Collection<int> processIds)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(processName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				Process[] processesByName = Process.GetProcessesByName(processName);
				if (processesByName != null)
				{
					Process[] array = processesByName;
					foreach (Process process in array)
					{
						processIds.Add(process.Id);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (InvalidOperationException)
			{
			}
		}

		public static Collection<int> GetProcessIdsByName(string processName)
		{
			Collection<int> collection = new Collection<int>();
			InternalGetProcessIdsByName(processName, collection);
			return collection;
		}

		public static Collection<int> GetProcessIdsByName(string[] processNames)
		{
			Collection<int> collection = new Collection<int>();
			if (processNames == null)
			{
				return collection;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < processNames.Length; i++)
			{
				InternalGetProcessIdsByName(processNames[i], collection);
			}
			return collection;
		}

		public static int? InferProcessId(Collection<int> initialProcessIds, Collection<int> finalProcessIds)
		{
			if (finalProcessIds == null || finalProcessIds.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (initialProcessIds == null || initialProcessIds.Count == 0)
			{
				if (finalProcessIds.Count == 1)
				{
					return finalProcessIds[0];
				}
				return null;
			}
			int? result = null;
			foreach (int finalProcessId in finalProcessIds)
			{
				if (!initialProcessIds.Contains(finalProcessId))
				{
					if (result.HasValue)
					{
						result = null;
						break;
					}
					result = finalProcessId;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool StartMonitoring(int processId)
		{
			Process process = null;
			try
			{
				process = Process.GetProcessById(processId);
			}
			catch (InvalidOperationException)
			{
			}
			return StartMonitoring(process);
		}

		public bool StartMonitoring(Process process)
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
			{
				StopMonitoring();
				_MonitoredProcess = null;
			}
			_MonitoredProcess = process;
			if (process == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_ProcessExitedHandlers != null)
			{
				_MonitoredProcess.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
				_MonitoredProcess.Exited += WatchedProcess_Exited;
			}
			if (_StoppedRespondingHandlers != null)
			{
				_EventTimer.Start();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void StopMonitoring()
		{
			if (_MonitoredProcess != null)
			{
				if (_StoppedRespondingHandlers != null)
				{
					_EventTimer.Stop();
				}
				if (_ProcessExitedHandlers != null)
				{
					_MonitoredProcess.Exited -= WatchedProcess_Exited;
					_MonitoredProcess.EnableRaisingEvents = false;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxsUnitsUIEditor : UITypeEditor, IDisposable
	{
		private ListBox _ListBox;

		private IWindowsFormsEditorService _EditorService;

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && _ListBox != null)
			{
				_ListBox.Dispose();
			}
			_ListBox = null;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "FullTrust")]
		public override UITypeEditorEditStyle GetEditStyle(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return UITypeEditorEditStyle.DropDown;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "FullTrust")]
		public override object EditValue(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value)
		{
			_EditorService = provider.GetService(typeof(IWindowsFormsEditorService)) as IWindowsFormsEditorService;
			if (_EditorService == null)
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (!(context.Instance is CxsNode { UnitsList: var unitsList }))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (unitsList == null || unitsList.Count <= 1)
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (_ListBox == null)
			{
				_ListBox = new ListBox();
				_ListBox.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
				_ListBox.Click += _ListBox_Click;
			}
			_ListBox.Items.Clear();
			foreach (string item in unitsList)
			{
				_ListBox.Items.Add(item);
			}
			_ListBox.SelectedItem = value;
			_ListBox.Height = _ListBox.PreferredHeight;
			_EditorService.DropDownControl(_ListBox);
			return _ListBox.SelectedItem;
		}

		private void _ListBox_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_EditorService.CloseDropDown();
		}
	}
	public class CxsValueUIEditor : UITypeEditor, IDisposable
	{
		private ListBox _ListBox;

		private IWindowsFormsEditorService _EditorService;

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && _ListBox != null)
			{
				_ListBox.Dispose();
			}
			_ListBox = null;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "FullTrust")]
		public override UITypeEditorEditStyle GetEditStyle(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			CxsNode cxsNode = ((context != null) ? (context.Instance as CxsNode) : null);
			if (cxsNode == null)
			{
				return UITypeEditorEditStyle.None;
			}
			ReadOnlyCollection<string> optionList = cxsNode.OptionList;
			if (optionList != null && optionList.Count > 0)
			{
				return UITypeEditorEditStyle.DropDown;
			}
			return UITypeEditorEditStyle.None;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "FullTrust")]
		public override object EditValue(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value)
		{
			_EditorService = provider.GetService(typeof(IWindowsFormsEditorService)) as IWindowsFormsEditorService;
			if (_EditorService == null)
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (!(context.Instance is CxsNode cxsNode))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (cxsNode.ReadOnly)
			{
				return value;
			}
			ReadOnlyCollection<string> optionList = cxsNode.OptionList;
			if (optionList != null && optionList.Count > 0)
			{
				if (_ListBox == null)
				{
					_ListBox = new ListBox();
					_ListBox.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
					_ListBox.Click += _ListBox_Click;
				}
				_ListBox.Items.Clear();
				foreach (string item in optionList)
				{
					_ListBox.Items.Add(item);
				}
				_ListBox.SelectedItem = value;
				_ListBox.Height = _ListBox.PreferredHeight;
				_EditorService.DropDownControl(_ListBox);
				return _ListBox.SelectedItem;
			}
			return value;
		}

		private void _ListBox_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_EditorService.CloseDropDown();
		}
	}

	{
		public string AttributeName { get; private set; }

		public CxsAttributeDependencyAttribute(string attributeName)
		{
			AttributeName = attributeName;
		}
	}
